# Play next — release log

Every release and no-version fix, newest last. Moved out of CLAUDE.md (which every chat loads) to save tokens. Before working on a feature, grep this file for its name or function (e.g. `grep -n "liteUp" docs/LOG.md`); add a line here for every release.

- v161: renamed Index.html → index.html (site root was broken); removed hand-loaded build override; auto-update on return.
- v162: cleanup. Removed dead code (pnSpeak, 34 unused CSS rules), unused files (key art, duplicate icons). Image sweep now runs only when new elements appear (was every 1.2s forever). Countdown and Grumble timers pause when hidden/off. Idle main-thread work per 10s: 31ms → 15ms; style recalcs 26 → 0.
- v163: "Why you'll love it" button on each game page. Sends your rated games (3+ needed) to the helper's `/why`, which asks free Workers AI (Llama 3.1 8B). Answer cached per game in `S.aiWhy` until ratings change.
- v164: "Why" sheet used class `.aw`, which clashed with an existing layout class (broke on wide screens) → renamed `.awc`. Sheet shows the helper's error text. Helper: tries several AI models, returns errors with CORS, and `GET /why` is a health check (open it in a browser).
- v164 fix: restored the image files removed in v162 (Fortune's Weave cover was one of them).
- v165: lag fix for owner's older phone. With Speed boost (html.fast, on by default) only the first 4 pieces of a screen cascade in; the rest don't animate (~24% less style/layout/paint per tab switch). Nexi's power-up sparks now stop by themselves after 6s instead of running until a tap. Perf harness: Playwright, 4x CPU throttle, trace RunTask/Paint/Layerize per tab switch.
- v166: "↺ Reset scores" on each game page (clears rating, hype, category/sub scores, why-tags, its duels, AI answer; game stays played). Settings → Your data → "Reset all scores" (all of the above for every game + all duels + badges; games/art/hours/diary kept; double confirm). Game-page buttons now wrap and stay clear of the side rail on wide screens.
- v167: Nexi's glow (`#pnbub .aura`, a spinning conic disc) sometimes showed as a square. Removed the stacked blur/clip-path/mask fixes; now plain `border-radius:50%` + painted box-shadow. Don't put filter/clip-path/mask on spinning layers.
- v168: queue card close lag. Row glide (FLIP after renderQueue) had `zIndex` in its keyframes, which kept it off the GPU and repainted every row each frame → z-index now set on the element during the animation; off-screen rows skip the glide. Image-loading shimmer animated background-position (repaint per frame) → opacity pulse. Close: paint ~70ms → ~25ms at 4x throttle.
- v169 (perf round 1): cached Intl formatters globally (Date/Number toLocale* patched at top of first script; ~37x faster, same output). Converted main-thread animations to transform/opacity: "Out now" tag shine, lx progress bar, card-flip shine, boot bar, boss blink/glint; removed looping HP-bar mask/background loops; home ring blob lost its looping blur. Bench harness: scratchpad h/bench.js (60 games, 4x throttle, every tab/scroll/queue card/game/settings/edit).
- v169 (perf round 2): save() is now deferred (1.2s + requestIdleCallback) and coalesced; flushes on visibilitychange/pagehide; use saveNow() when the result matters. Big uploaded images (data: URIs) render via cached blob: URLs (esc/cssUrl) so redraws don't re-parse MBs; form inputs that get saved back use escRaw. Duel-row MutationObserver throttled to once per frame. Measured (4x throttle, 25 uploaded covers): total work -23%, worst frame 233→150ms, queue card close 576→249.
- v170 (perf round 3): queue background (#bgart/#bgart2) had a live CSS blur that the compositor re-ran every frame (queue idle: 57/66 frames dropped). With Speed boost it's now pre-blurred once into a canvas (bgBake; ctx.filter on Chrome, smaller canvas on Safari), redrawn on resize. Dropped near-invisible backdrop-filter blurs on ~90%-opaque panels. Shelf covers no longer transition filter. Queue idle: 0-1 dropped frames.
- v170 (perf round 4): renderQueue uses qPatch(): if the header and row order are unchanged, only rows whose HTML changed are swapped (template → replaceWith); otherwise full innerHTML. Row listener binding guarded with r._qb so kept rows aren't double-bound. Card open click task 275→78ms (4x).
- v170 (perf round 5): #fx sparks canvas is display:none unless sparks are flying (was an always-on full-screen layer). Final bench (4x throttle, 60 games, 25 uploaded covers) vs pre-perf build: worst frame 500→167ms folded, 483→183ms unfolded; dropped frames 290→114 folded, 868→467 unfolded. Bench tip: set S.fxSeen/S.fxCal when seeding or the "Out now" overlay covers the app.
- v171: swipe a queue card (row or open hero) left → red Delete button (confirm; removes game, played entry, its duels, AI answer). Only children slide (`translate` via --sx); `.qdel` sits at z-index -1 inside the row. Horizontal swipe cancels the long-press reorder (synthetic touchcancel) and stops touchend propagation so the sub-tab swipe doesn't fire. Rows have touch-action: pan-y.
- v172: Nexi's what's-new tour (WHATS_NEW + nxTour, card #nxtour, Nexi points via nxPoint without covering the target). First run shows v163–v172 features. Replay button #s-whatsnew.
- v173: tour fixes. Nexi showed as a blank circle during the tour when the rail was open (or on a phone): the bubble is only dressed by pnBubShow, which runs when the rail is hidden → nxDress() calls pnBubFace() before nxPoint/nxFlyTo. Skipped updates stack: tour covers everything since `S.tourFrom` (last finished tour), with a "You missed N updates" intro when >1 version; `S.tourFrom` is only set on Done/Skip, and boot resumes an unfinished tour. Steps with no target park Nexi beside the card. The "Updated" toast is dismissed when the tour starts (it covered the card on phones). The step you're on is saved (`S.tourAt` = {from, n, i}, saveNow on each Next), so reopening resumes at that step ("Picking up where we left off"). One-time `S.tourRedo`: every existing user gets the full tour again from v163 (owner missed the first one); fresh installs skip it.
- v174: Home merged with the owner's Dribbble "Game Dashboard" reference (layout/style only, our black + blue colours). `dxHome()` builds one panel (`.dxw`, all classes `dx*`): tabs + search (`#dxq`, results open the game) + pills; the pick as a player card (`.dxhero`, play button, dice = pick one, ⚔ = duels); Up next list (`.dxr`); queue count with floating covers (`.dxq`); "Playing now" tall cards with progress (`.dxc`); queue-hours donut (`.dxstat`, SVG); next release as a tilted ticket with live countdown (`.dxtick`). Other tiles live in the two swipe stacks under "More for you" (`.dash.dxmore`). Phone (<620, not rail/dock) nav is an icon row; on Home the open tab sits in a notch cut into the panel. 620px+ is two zones (donut + ticket on the bottom row). New Home toggles: "queue", "playing". Library layouts Arc and Wheel removed (saved arc/wheel → Swipe). Perf (4x throttle) vs v173: equal tab switch, fewer dropped frames (412: 59→45, 900: 128→108).
- v175: the old Queue hours glow (`.ring .blob`: wobbly conic disc spinning 9s + breathing radial glow, same recipe as Nexi's aura) is back as `.dxorb` behind the queue count in "Your queue" on Home. Transform/opacity only; 0 dropped frames idle at 4x throttle. Stops under html.still / reduced motion.
- v176: "More for you" cards get the backdrop of a related game (post-pass in renderHome, class `.dxbg`, `--bgi` + game-colour tint `--bgt`): Your read = biggest hype miss, What decides it = top-rated played, Duels = last winner, Settle a duel = runner-up, Needs a verdict = first unscored, Standings = #1, hours = most hours, year = a game in the peak month; tiles with data-home-open use that game. Tonight keeps its own imgcard.
- v177: play sessions + year wrapped (code sits just before `load();`, since load() renders Home and needs these defined). `S.sessions` = [{g, s, e}] (ms). stopTimer is wrapped: it saves the session before the timer screen redraws and, for 10+ min, opens the diary ("How did that N min feel?"). Game page chip ⏱ Sessions → `openSessions(id)` (dy sheet: stats, 16-week grid `sessHeat`, quick +30m/+1h/+2h/+3h logs that also add to `g.logged`, delete subtracts). Timer screen: "Recent sessions" (`sessRecent`). Home cards "Play sessions" (`.k-sess`, 7-day bars via scaleY) and "YYYY so far / wrapped" (`.k-wrap`, `data-wrapped`) → `openWrapped()`: story overlay `#wrap` (bars animate scaleX; animationend advances; tap right/left, hold to pause), Nexi shown via html.nxguide + pnBubPlace, slides from `wrData(Y)` skip when empty, ends with `wrCard()` canvas share card (reuses .ccov). "so far" until December (`wrLate`). Stack order: cards missing from a saved stack order now go first (so new cards are seen). More-for-you backdrops load lazily (IntersectionObserver). Reset-all-settings keeps sessions. Home toggles: "sessions", "wrapped".
- v178: calmer Settings/Edit + monthly recap. Settings: search box (`.ssch`) finds any setting on any screen. Index built once from the same template: openSettings has a `tplOnly` flag that returns the HTML string. Results jump via `setJump` (sets the sub, goTab, openSettings(scope), opens the section, flashes the label). App Settings shows "Most used" shortcuts (`SET_QUICK`). Sections open one at a time as their own page (`#sheet-in.drill`, `.setgroup.cur`, "‹ Back to all" is the summary). Sections with >7 labels show 5 and fold the rest into `.more-opts` behind a "More options (N)" button (`setMoreOpen` unfolds it for search/tour; nxOpenSettings wrapped). Icons per section: `GROUP_ICON`. Edit gets the same icons/one-at-a-time/More options (tabs hide while a section is open). Customise still starts with only that screen's settings; search is the way across. Monthly recap: `wrData(Y, M)` / `openWrapped(Y, M)`, `data-wrapped="YYYY-MM"`; shorter (no surprise/releases/mood slides). Home card `.k-month`: this month so far, or last month's recap in the first 7 days. Home toggle "monthly".
- v179: fixes found with the owner's real backup (36 games, 3 competitive, 1 session, all 15 played entries dated Sep 2026). Hero shows "N duels" (the "#1 · " got cut off at 412). Playing now: competitive games (`g.comp`) show "⚔ Competitive" + diary win rate instead of an empty progress bar (`.dxcomp`). Recaps: `d.thin` (fewer than 3 sessions in the period) skips the hours and most-played slides and uses finished/duels/added/released (`wrCells`) on the last slide, the card and the Home cards. Edit → Played has "When you finished it" (`#e-fin`, sets the played record's `t`, which the recaps use). Queue header "waiting" count no longer includes dropped games (matches Home). `.dash.dxmore` uses content-visibility:auto (below the fold): with real data at 4x throttle, Home tab switch 123ms vs v173 112 (folded), 177 vs 174 (unfolded); worst frame 117 vs 133 / 167 vs 183. Real-data harness: scratchpad realh.js (loads the backup into localStorage and swaps remote images for local stand-ins, since this container can't reach SteamGridDB/RAWG/Steam).
- v180: the finish date used a native `<input type=date>` that the owner could not change on the Fold. Replaced with `finPick()`/`finWire()`: a year row (‹ ›) and 12 month buttons, future months disabled; it keeps the day if the month stays the same, otherwise uses the 15th (or today for this month). Used in Edit → Played (`#e-fin`) and from the Top board: the month under each game (`[data-findate]`, "Mar 2026 ✎") opens a small sheet (`openFinDate`) and saves right away. Avoid native date inputs for new UI.
- v181: Game Shuffler + one-tap themes. Themes: every hard-coded accent in the `<style>` blocks now uses variables (`--volt`, `--volt2`, `rgba(var(--vrgb), a)`, `rgba(var(--v2rgb), a)`; hex colours in JS/SVG such as PALETTE game colours were left alone). `THEMES` (midnight/crimson/emerald/amethyst/sunset/mono) → applyLook sets the vars and `html[data-theme]`; choosing one also sets `S.paperCol` to the theme's wash. Picker `#s-theme` at the top of Settings → Look of the app, and "Theme" in Most used. Use var(--volt)/var(--vrgb) for any new accent colour, never hex. Shuffler: `openShuffle()` (`#shuf`, all classes `sh-*`), opened by `[data-shuffle]` (Home header pill, where "Pick one" used to be; the hero's dice still picks one; Queue chip "🔀 Shuffle"). Layout copied from the owner's Dribbble chess reference: a big serif name (Playfair Display, added to the Google Fonts link) in front of the game's key art or a tilted cover, a thumbnail row of the deck with the current one ringed, "Why play X next" + outlined fact chips, a round-button bar (undo · not now · ★ tempted · reshuffle · picks with badge), and tilted side cards with vertical serif names (tap = step). White/black dots switch between everything waiting and out-now only (`S.shufMode`). Drag follows the finger (transform only) with TEMPTED/NOT NOW stamps; >90px decides. The picks screen offers "▶ Play now" (sets state playing) and open.
- v182: your own lists + time to beat by play style. Lists: `S.lists` = [{id, n, e}] (name, emoji); a game's `g.lists` = [ids]. `openLists(gid)` (dy sheet: tap a list to toggle this game, make a new list with an emoji; no gid = manage: rename ✎, delete 🗑 with confirm, "show in queue"). Entry: game page chip "📋 Lists · N" and "Manage lists / Make a list" at the bottom of the Queue's filter menu, where the lists also appear as filters (`S.qFilter = "L:<id>"`; qNow/qCounts/queueList know them; deleting the list you're on resets to Everything). `listsChanged()` re-renders the queue (its header holds the menu). The Shuffler picks screen has "📋 Save as a list". Play style: `S.playStyle` main/extras/all (Customise Plan → Your evening → "How you play", also in Most used). A game has `hours` (main story), `hoursX`, `hours100` (Edit → Release). `hrsOf(g)` returns the style's number, or an estimate from main (×1.5 / ×2.4, or hoursX×1.6), 0 for competitive. Every forward-looking hours read (queue "to beat", evenings, Home donut/sums/Playing now, plan, timer %, hub/launcher length, sort longest/shortest, shuffler, Nexi/boss lines) uses hrsOf; played-game history (Top board, taste stats) keeps the main-story hours. Home donut subtitle names the style when it isn't main.
- v183 (upgrade plan phase 1, cleanup): Light-then-full images: `esc()`/`cssUrl()` ask `liteFor(url)`, which returns a light copy once one is known to load (Steam `library_600x900` / `library_hero_blur`, RAWG `/media/resize/640/-/`, SteamGridDB `thumb`/`hero_thumb`; checked light URLs remembered in localStorage `gq:lite`), or a 520px canvas thumb of an upload or same-origin file (blob URL, per session). `liteUp()` (idle, 650ms after DOM changes/scroll) decodes the full picture and swaps it in only where an element shows bigger than the light copy; once swapped the URL is `LITE_WARM` and renders full for the rest of the session. bgBake always uses the light copy. `sw.js` caches cross-origin images from Steam/SGDB/RAWG cache-first in `play-next-img-1` (350 max, kept on SHELL wipes). Diary and Timer are off by default (`S.diaryOn`, `S.timerOn`; Settings → Extra features): `xFeatures()` (runs inside applyLook) sets `html.nodiary/.notimer` (CSS hides `[data-diary]`, `[data-sess]`, timer starts) and removes Timer from `SUBS.plan`; the Home "sessions" card is forced off without the timer. Private usage counter `S.usage` = {since, v: screen visits ("games/queue"), o: opens (app, game, settings/<scope>, edit, duel, score, shuffle, add), days, byV[APP_V]}; `uCount(kind, key)`, sheet `uSheet()` from Settings → Your data → 📊 Your usage. Quick actions: `qDuel(focusId)` sheet (played, non-competitive games only; `qDuelPair` = closest Elo pair not yet compared; shows ↑N rank change; next pair loads straight away), `qScore(id)` (one tap on a number, `SCORE_LO`..10 in halves), `qScorePick()`; `[data-qduel]` / `[data-qscore]` anywhere open them (Home hero ⚔, game page ⭐ Score / ⚔ Duel it chips, Nexi wheel ⭐ when the diary is off). Add: a ＋ on each RAWG hit adds it straight away. Backups: `backupText(withKey)` strips `BK_SECRET` (RAWG key, sync id/token/sig); "Put my RAWG key in the file" is now "Always leave it out / Ask me each time" (confirm when making the file); restoring never overwrites this phone's sync id.
- v184 (upgrade plan phase 2, ranking): Scores are 0–10 in halves or "Don't remember". `scored(p)` is the one test for "has a score" (a number > 0, or 0 with `p.z = 1`; `p.dk = 1` = Don't remember → shown as Unscored, left out of every stat); write scores with `scoreWrite(rec, v | "dk")`. One-time `scoreMigrate()` (flag `S.scoreMig`) turned old 0 scores into `dk`. `tierOf(score)`: S 9+, A 7.5+, B 6+, C. `rankTable()` = the ranking of played, non-competitive, scored games: Bradley–Terry fit (T .6) with each game pulled toward its score (λ .4), memoised on a signature; `{order, pos, r, n (duels), sc}`; `rankOf`, `rankedGames`. Duels: `duelResult(d)` win 1 / slight edge (`d.e = 1`) .75 / even .5, also used by eloTable. `qDuelPair(focus)` picks the closest-rated pair not yet duelled (or skipped recently, `S.duelSkip`) and skips pairs >3 apart; `qDuelRecord` tracks `S.climbMax`, `S.duelDays`, `S.edgeN` for the duel badges (PN_BADGES duel10/100/250, climb5, duelday7, edge10, settled3). Confidence dots `confDots(n)`: 1–2 / 3–5 / 6+ duels. Taste → Duels (`tasteDuels`) is played-only now: pair + ‹ Slight edge / Even / Slight edge › / Skip (`[data-tdw]`), ranking list `.rkl`. Top board (`pnBoardRows`) follows the ranking: big `#rank`, tier, small score, dots. Taste psych/patterns get `insightsBlock()` (`tasteInsights()`: duel win rate of genres/why-tags vs the rest, S-tier make-up, and signals). Signals `S.sig` [{t,k: add/remove/drop/trailer,id,n,tg}] come from `sigWatch()` diffing game ids on save (reset after backups) and `sigAdd()`. On deck: `onDeckScores()` (similarity of genres to your ranked games weighted by rank and score, queue Elo, platform `S.curPlat` (default Switch, "any"), hype, playing; out-now games always first), `onDeck(n)`, `deckWhy(g)`, strip `.deck` under the queue's sort row (`S.deckOff`), sort "On deck (my pick)". Home: the hero pick is On deck #1; `nxHomeLine()` puts Nexi at the top of Home (`.nxhome`: what to play next + top 3; face opens a duel). Customise (Queue) → On deck: platform + row toggle. Global `[data-opg]` opens a game page. Perf (4x throttle, render timings): same as v183 within noise; rankTable ~3ms cold, memoised.
- v185 (upgrade plan phase 3, Nexi + discovery): Personalities `S.nxPersona` (`NX_PERSONAS`: hype (default, lines unchanged), butler, coach, rival, bard, nerd). `nxVoice(text)` re-voices plain lines (openers/closers per persona, butler drops emoji and "!"); `pnBubSay` is wrapped so every bubble line goes through it; Home's line has per-persona wording (`nxHomeLine` wrapped). Old "beta" lines replaced. Chat: `nxChat()` (dy sheet: persona chips, log `S.nxChat` last 40, quick asks, ⚔ Duel, ⚙ Find a setting → old `nxAsk`), `nxSend` → helper `POST /chat` {persona, memory, context: `nxContext()` (ranking, queue/On deck, playing, competitive, insights, platform), messages}; after a reply `nxRemember()` → `POST /chat/memory` rewrites `S.nxMem` (≤700 chars; Settings → Nexi can forget it). Opened by tapping Nexi's face on Home, the Nexi wheel 💬, `[data-nxchat]`. Competitive games: `NX_COMP` comment lines (FC, Mobile Legends, Chess, generic) mixed into `pnBubLines` and tips; no win/loss tracking. Nudges (Settings → Nexi → Nudges, `S.nudgeOn`): `nudgeOn()` asks permission, subscribes with the helper's VAPID key, uploads `nxPlan(5)` (2 per day at random times inside `nxWindow()` from `S.nxHours`, default 18–22; rotating kinds next/duel/tip/badge/release, voiced) to `POST /nudge` keyed by `S.nudgeId`; re-planned on each open after 4h. The helper cron sends due ones (web push, RFC 8291 aes128gcm verified against the RFC test vector, VAPID made on first use and kept in KV). `sw.js` handles `push`, `notificationclick` (opens `./?nudge=kind`, which the app routes to a duel / the pick / Played) and `periodicsync` (fallback only when there's no push subscription, reads cache `play-next-nudge`). Similar games: `simLocal(g)` (genre + why-tag Jaccard), `simRawg(g)` (RAWG genres+tags query, library excluded, cached `S.simCache` 14 days), sheet `openSimilar(id)` (library rows, RAWG rows with ▶ trailer and ＋ add via `simAdd` = fill + #f-save), queue filter "Like <game>" (`S.qFilter = "S:<id>"`, `simFilter`, restored by `simRestore`), `simBack(g)` on the back of the queue card. Trailer: `[data-trailer]` → YouTube search "<title> trailer" and a `trailer` taste signal. Game page chips ▶ Trailer, 🔍 Similar. Perf (4x, render timings): same as v184.
- v186: Similar games fixed. RAWG list-filter commas are OR, and the old query used generic tags (Singleplayer, Steam Achievements) + `-rating`, so it returned obscure indie games. `simRawg` now takes the game's rarest meaningful tags (`SIM_GENERIC` blocklist, games_count ≥150, top 5), runs one `ordering=-added` query per top-3 tag plus `games/{id}/game-series`, scores each candidate by shared tags (rarer = heavier) + genres + series bonus + popularity, needs 2+ shared tags or same series and 300+ adds (or a metacritic), drops DLC/soundtracks; cache moved to `S.simCache2` (old `S.simCache` deleted). `simLocal` weights tags by rarity in the library (IDF). The similar sheet slid sideways: `.dy-gl` grid column grew to the longest title → `grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr)` and `.dy-in { overflow-x: hidden }`.
- v187: Similar games are picked by the AI, not RAWG tag searches (RAWG's tags for Japanese games are too poor; v186 still gave Control/Dishonored for Persona 3 Reload). Helper `POST /similar` {title, year, genres, favourites (top 8 ranked), exclude (library titles)} → the big model returns [{name, why}] JSON (parser falls back to regex). `simFind(g)` then checks every name on RAWG (`search_precise`, best title match by `simSame` ≥ .6), drops unknown/owned ones, shows the AI's "why" line; cached `S.simCache3` 30 days (old caches deleted). Old helper (404 "not found" → `nxPost` throws "helper-old") → same-series games from RAWG + a note; chat says the helper needs updating. Helper: `HELPER_V = 2`, `GET /version`, `MODELS_BIG` (llama-3.3-70b fp8-fast, llama-4-scout) tried first for chat and similar, then the 8B models. Settings → Nexi shows the helper status (`helperCheck()`). `worker/wrangler.jsonc` lists the bindings (AI, NUDGE KV, cron).
- v188: "Clean" style, the rest of the app in Home's design language. One `<style>` block near the end of `index.html`, every rule scoped to `html.ui2` (tokens `--u-pan` #0a0e1b, `--u-card` #131a32, `--u-line`/`--u-line2` hairlines). Settings → Look of the app → Style: Clean (default) / Arcade (`S.uiStyle = "arcade"` drops `html.ui2`, back to the slanted all-caps look; applyLook toggles it). Clean: headings upright sentence case Poppins 700 (screen titles, sheet titles, game page title, calendar month, Top board/Badges heads, settings sections); no italics outside em/i and the celebration screens (wrapped, level-up); pill chips with a hairline (pressed = accent fill); flat rounded buttons (danger = soft red outline); rounded fields with a focus ring (the pill search boxes `.dxs`/`.ssch` keep bare inputs); queue rows/hits/empty cards = one dark surface + hairline, no thick left accent edge (the open card keeps a small accent bar); sheets lose the 6px accent top edge and get a grab handle. Fixes: with the Nexi rail on a 620–1099px screen, `.wrap` gets padding on the rail side on every tab (it covered Customise, status tags) and the game page Back button moves left of the rail; Library titles lost their first letter to the card's rounded corner (`.libcard` overflow). Texts that were literally in caps (TOP BOARD, BADGES, BACK) are now in sentence case with `text-transform: uppercase` in the Arcade CSS. For new UI: use the `--u-*` tokens and don't add italic/uppercase headings. Perf: style/layout per tab switch equal to v187 (CDP metrics, 18 tab switches + 3 game pages + 3 settings). Queue rows lost their `filter: drop-shadow` (now a plain box-shadow + `isolation: isolate` so the swipe Delete button still sits behind): queue scroll at 4x throttle dropped frames 27→19 (412), 60–83→24–42 (900). Keep the italic reset a simple `*:not(em):not(i)`: a `:not(#wrap *)` version walks ancestors on every recalc.
- v189: tour + pictures. Tour: `nxNotch(done)` opens every tour. A black pill (`#nxisle`: two circle caps `.nxi-c` + a middle `.nxi-m`, so it stays a true pill while it grows; transform only) grows out of the top centre where the camera is (`#nxsat` probe reads env(safe-area-inset-top): centred on the camera when there's an inset, else from just above the screen). Nexi appears inside it, drops out with a squash, and the pill tucks back. Nexi then rides on the tour card (`nxDock(card, target)`): he peeks over its top edge (card at the bottom) or under it (card at the top), behind it (z 99 vs 100), and his eyes look at the target. The target gets `#nxring` (fixed outline that follows scroll, opacity pulse) instead of `.nxhl`. `html.nxtouring` while the tour runs: `pnBubSay` and `pnBubShow` are wrapped to stay quiet / re-dock, the bubble's resize handler skips, taps/drags on Nexi only bounce him (capture pointerdown). This fixes hiding/showing the rail mid-tour sending him back to his corner talking. `nxTour.refit` re-docks on resize/fold. The "Updated" toast waits for DOMContentLoaded and is skipped when a tour will run; the first card says "Updated · vX → vY". `nxHome`/`nxQueue` no longer redraw the screen you're already on (the tour's main hitch: dropped frames after the entrance 33→10 folded, 58→14 unfolded at 4x). Pictures: Steam `library_hero` no longer maps to `library_hero_blur` (same size, blurred on purpose, never upgraded → wide art stayed blurry); liteUp judges both sides (`object-fit: cover`) and backgrounds by their longer side, and re-runs when a light copy finishes loading; an `<img>` whose light copy fails falls back to the full picture (and remembers it), and a failed remote picture gets one retry (`?r=1`). `sw.js`: images are fetched with CORS first and cached only when `ok` (a no-cors 404 used to be cached forever); non-CORS hosts still load but aren't kept; cache renamed `play-next-img-2` so the old one (with possible failures) is dropped.
- v190: the owner saw Nexi half-hidden behind the tour card. `nxDock` now keeps him fully in view and in front (`html.nxtouring #pnbub` z 101 > card 100): beside the card when there's room (left, else right, 20px clear of it, level with its top) — the unfolded Fold — otherwise just above the card (card at the bottom) or just below it (card at the top), 14px clear. Don't dock him behind or overlapping the card again.
- v191: notifications. Every release now reaches the phones: the helper's cron (`checkUpdate`) fetches the live index.html, and when `APP_V` is higher than KV `appv` it pushes "✨ Play next vN is here" with the first `WHATS_NEW` "say" line for that version (`whatsNew()`, tags stripped, ~170 chars) to every subscription with `upd !== false` (first run only records the version). Keep each release's first WHATS_NEW `say` a good one-line pitch: it becomes the notification. Settings → Nexi → Notifications (`#s-nudge`): Off / "Updates + releases" (`S.nudgeMode = "key"`) / "+ 2 nudges a day" (`"all"`, the default for anyone who had nudges on). `nxPlan` always adds release pings (evening before, inside the usual-hours window, and 10:00 on the day; `./?nudge=release&g=<id>` opens the game) and the monthly recap on the 1st at 11:00 (`./?nudge=recap&m=YYYY-MM` opens `openWrapped(Y, M-1)`); the 2-a-day kinds (next/duel/tip/badge) only in "all". Plan sorted, capped at 30. `sendDue` sends up to the 2 newest due (<3h old) per phone; subscriptions last 60 days and are refreshed on each open after 4h. The nudge link handler reads the query before `replaceState` clears it (it used to lose `g`/`m`). `HELPER_V = 3`. Tour docking on phones: Nexi sits above/below the card's right end (buttons are on the left). The owner has to add the Cloudflare token once; until then the helper stays on whatever was last deployed by hand.
- helper fix (no app version): KV `expirationTtl` is in seconds and must fit a 32-bit int. The sign-up used `30 * 864e5` (ms) since v185, so KV threw "Value out of range" and the phone showed "Couldn't turn nudges on: value out of range" — nudges never worked. Now `SUB_TTL = 60 * 86400`.
- v192: every kind of notification, each switchable. `NX_KINDS` (update, release, countdown, recap, weekly, next, duel, playing, verdict, badge, tip, memory, comeback); `nxKinds()` = the enabled set: `S.nudgeMode` "all" (default) = everything, "pick" = `S.nudgeKinds` (default `NX_PICK_DEF`: update/release/countdown/recap/weekly); v191's "key" migrates to pick with update/release/recap. Settings → Nexi → Notifications: Off / All / Pick, and under Pick a chip per kind (`#s-nkinds`, `[data-k]`; changes re-upload the plan after 1.2s). Everyday kinds (`NX_DAILY`: next, duel, playing, verdict, badge, tip) fill the 2-a-day slots; fixed ones: release (eve + 10:00), countdown (7 days before, evening), recap (1st, 11:00), weekly (Sunday evening: finished/duels/added in the last 7 days), memory (a played game's finish anniversary, 12:00), comeback (now + 3 days; each open re-plans it forward). Max 4 per day, fixed kinds first. New links: `./?nudge=game&g=<id>` opens a game, `./?nudge=verdict` opens qScorePick. The helper's `upd` flag follows the "New versions" switch.
- Instant update pings (no app version): helper `POST /announce` runs `checkUpdate` now (returns {ok, v, sent}; only ever announces a version once, so it's safe unauthenticated). `.github/workflows/announce.yml` runs on every push to main that changes index.html: waits until GitHub Pages serves the new `APP_V` (10 s polls, up to 10 min), then calls /announce (retries a minute in case the helper is redeploying). The 15-minute cron stays as the backup.
- Notification badge (no app version): the status-bar icon showed as a white square because `badge` was the full opaque logo; Android draws the badge as a white silhouette of its alpha. `badge-96.png` = the logo's » chevrons in white on transparent (drawn with canvas, precached in sw.js). Keep `icon` the full-colour logo.
- v193: every update notification gets through. The 4-a-day cap was only ever on the planned nudges (`nxPlan`); update pings come from the helper's `checkUpdate`. But they all used tag `update`, so each new one silently replaced the last in the tray. Now: tag `update-<v>`; if several versions went live between checks, each one with a `WHATS_NEW` entry gets its own ping (up to 10; the live version always does); update pushes wait `UPD_TTL` (28 days) for an offline phone instead of 12 h (`webPush(env, sub, msg, ttl)`). Settings note says new versions don't count toward the 4 a day.
- Push urgency (no app version): every push went out with `Urgency: normal`, which Android/FCM holds while the phone dozes, so the owner's Fold only showed the v193 ping once the phone woke (the app was opened) (the iPhone got it at once). `webPush` now sends `Urgency: high` (allowed: every push we send shows a notification). Pop-up (heads-up) on Android is the notification channel's setting, which Chrome creates as "no pop-up"; the app can't change it, the owner turns on "Pop-up" for Play next in Android's notification settings.
- v194: Home's look on every screen (Clean style only). Each screen's `.tabbar` becomes a header card (`.tabbar.shx`, code + CSS in the last `<style>`/`<script>` at the end of `index.html`): big title, 2–3 live numbers (`.shx-st`, first one in the accent), a fanned hand of that screen's covers (`.shx-fan`, first/most relevant in front on the right, tap = open the game via `[data-opg]`; 3 covers under 620px, 4 above) and a static glow in the first game's colour (`--shc`). `shxData(kind)` gives the numbers/covers per screen (queue: waiting/hours to beat/out now + On deck; lib; played: finished/S-tier/average + ranking; replay; plan: days to next release/in 30 days; taste: ranked/duels/S-tier; add). `shxDress(el, kind)` runs after each screen's render (renderQueue/Lib/Played/Replay/Cal/Taste are wrapped; Add refreshes with renderHome) and skips when nothing changed; the covers deal in (transform/opacity, once per change of covers). applyLook strips the cards in Arcade. qPatch compares header strings, so the dressing doesn't break its fast path. Calendar: a release day shows its cover inside the ring (`.ccov`); an empty month shows the next release (`calNextCard()`, `.cnext`). Game page (ui2): `.hub-data` numbers are tiles (auto-fit, min 74px so "13 Jan" fits at 412), `.chip.hot` actions (Score, Duel) are filled. Perf (4x throttle, 12 tab switches, 8 runs each at 412): total work 5046 vs 4999ms (+1%, noise ±300), 900 equal; dress cost ≤1.2ms per screen. Test harness: scratchpad h.cjs (seeds 18 games with canvas-made covers/art served from /img, sets storage from /manifest.webmanifest so the app's pagehide save can't overwrite it), shots.cjs, bench.cjs.
- v195: Add screen + game page in Home's language, each switchable. Game page: `S.fullStyle` "dash" = New (default; `fullStyleOf()`), "hub" = Showcase, "simple" = Plain (Customise → A game's own page → Style). One-time `S.gdMig` moved saved "hub" to New. `hubMarkup` returns `gdMarkup(g)` for New: `.hub.gpage` (NOT `.dash`: that class is Home's tile grid) scrolls inside the page (`#full .hub` is absolute, one screen tall), `.gd-hero` = backdrop (`bgSource`) + key art `g.key` (`.gd-fig`) + logo `g.art` (`.gd-logo`), then `.gd-body` (margin-top -30px): cover + kicker pill + h2 (`.gd-head`), tags (state/genres/platforms), desc, `.hub-data` tiles, `.gd-main` (Score/Duel filled + Edit), `.gd-more` icon tiles (4 per row, 6 at 620+). Numbers and buttons come from `hubStats(g)` / `hubActs(g)` (shared with Showcase, whose markup is byte-identical to before); the handlers key off the same data-* attributes, and the swipe between games works unchanged (hubMarkup builds the neighbours). Add: `#p-add.anew` (Clean + `S.addStyle !== "classic"`, toggled in the applyLook wrapper; Settings → Look of the app → Add screen): steps as a 3-segment bar (`data-l` labels), search as a pill (`.aq` wraps #q + #q-go; `display: contents` in Classic), RAWG hits as picture cards (2 cols, 3 at 900+) with a round ＋, preview as a player card. Header cards (v194) can be turned off: `S.shxOff` (Settings → Look of the app → Header cards). Tested: every page button, swipe g8→g9, scroll, toggles, tour at 412/900. Perf (4x): open a game 56 vs 53ms (412), 68 vs 63 (900), noise ±10; scrolling the page 1–2 of ~200 frames over 20ms. Harness: RAWG search is faked in h.cjs (route api.rawg.io → local images); stand-in key art/logos k*.png/l*.png.
- v196: problem reports (the app files its own bugs) + character art off on the New game page. Reports: last `<script>` in `index.html`. `bugReport(kind, msg, {stack, at, screen, note})` → queue → helper `POST /bug` (batch ≤10, keepalive; retried when back online). Kinds: `error` (window error, not resource loads), `promise` (unhandledrejection), `layout` (`bugLayout()`, idle 1.6s after every screen change: page scrolls sideways, a button cut by the screen edge (`bugClipped` skips anything clipped/scrolled/mid-slide on purpose), a button whose visible part (`bugVisible`) is under the Nexi rail), `slow` (longtask > 400ms after boot, Chrome only), `helper` (nxPost failing while online), `tour` (a WHATS_NEW step whose `at` finds nothing or whose `go` throws), `user` (🐞 Report a problem: `bugAsk()`, from Settings → This build, `[data-bugreport]`, or Nexi's wheel 🐞). Noise filtered by `BUG_NOISE` (network errors, ResizeObserver, "Script error."). Privacy (the repo and its issues are public): `bugScrub` turns game titles, list names and Nexi's name into ‹game›, blanks keys/tokens/blob/data URLs; breadcrumbs (`BUG.crumbs`) record taps by data-attribute/id, never text. One signature per problem (`bugHash`, digits ignored; layout/slow per screen), sent once per day per phone (`S.bugSent`). Off switch `S.bugOff` (Settings → This build → Problem reports). Helper (`HELPER_V = 4`): `/bug` (Origin must be the app; ≤300 records, `bug:<sig>` in NUDGE KV for 45 days, merges count/versions/phones/screens), `GET /bugs` + `POST /bugs/ack` need `Authorization: Bearer BUG_KEY`. BUG_KEY = sha256(CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN + ":play-next-bugs"), set by deploy-helper.yml (`wrangler secret put`), so no extra secret. `.github/workflows/bugs.yml` (hourly at :17 + by hand) runs `.github/scripts/bugsync.mjs`: new problem → issue "🐞 Kind: msg [bug:<sig>]" labelled `auto-bug` (≤15 per run); again → comment (closed issues reopen); then acks. To fix bugs: read the open `auto-bug` issues; close them when fixed (they reopen if it happens again). First sweep (every screen at 360/375/412/700/900) found: the Home pick card (`.dxhero`) was 352px wide on phones under ~400px (220px min-height × 16:10 ratio set a min width) → `width: 100%`. Layout check costs ≤8ms at 4x throttle. Character art: `S.gdFig` (default off; Customise → A game's own page → Character art) gates `.gd-fig`.
- Daily bug-fix routine (no app version): Routine "Play next: daily bug fixes" (trigger `trig_01RcRcYQJqZPDuBGMhvJmZZG`, cron `46 3 * * *` UTC) wakes its own dedicated session "Play next · daily bug fixer" (`session_01UrvHBmr3w5i37FFfMfe9a8`, repo attached, GitHub tools + push checked). It's separate from the owner's chat sessions, which they replace often to save tokens (a fresh-session-per-run routine got no connectors or repo, so it couldn't read issues or open PRs). Each run: runs bugs.yml by hand first (the hourly schedule is unreliable), then open `auto-bug` issues → reproduce, fix, one release (APP_V, WHATS_NEW "Fixed: …", tour, speed check, Log) → close with "Fixed in vN"; nothing open → no changes. Feature/design requests and unsure cases get a comment and stay open. Pause or change it with update_trigger / delete_trigger; if that session is ever archived, make a new one with create_session (source_url = the repo) and point the trigger at it.
- v197: Nexi says when he's spotted (and already reported) a problem, so the owner doesn't report it again. `bugReport` calls `bugNoted(kind, scr, msg)` for every new automatic report: it adds a plain-English line (`bugNice`, screen names via `bugPlace`: "Library", "a game page", "Settings"…) to `S.bugLog` (7 days, last 20) and queues `nxBugSay()`: at most once per 90s (more glitches in that time → "I spotted N glitches…"), waits while the tour runs, always ends "I've already reported it 🐞 No need to tell anyone." If Nexi's bubble is on screen it's his speech bubble (`pnBubSay([txt, "bugs"])`, tap = `bugAsk()`); otherwise a small card `#nxbug` (🐞 + his name + the line, 7s, tap = `bugAsk()`; clear of the rail only at 620px+). The Report a problem sheet lists what he already sent (`bugLogHtml()`, repeats merged "×N") and names the screen in plain words. Switch: `S.bugSayOff` (Settings → This build → "Nexi tells me when he spots one").
- v198: first fixes from problem reports + lag detection. #48 (crash on Home): the More-for-you backdrop IntersectionObserver was saved on `renderHome.bgIO` and its callback read `renderHome.bgIO.unobserve`, but renderHome is wrapped by later scripts, so the property was on an older function → undefined. The callback now uses its own `(entries, observer)` argument. Rule: never read state off a function that later code wraps (`fn.x`); use a closure or the callback's arguments. #49 (owner, unfolded, rail hidden: "circle is big and is cut on the side"): Nexi floated 12px from the edge but his glow (`.orb`, 22px outside the 62px face) went past it → `PN_EDGE = 34` for every edge position (pnBubEdge, first placement, wander, pointing, wrapped). bugLayout reports Nexi's glow cut by the edge (not while `.peek`). bugScrub no longer hides Nexi's name. Lag (kind `slow`, all after 8s): long-animation-frame ≥150ms (names the blocking function in `at`, e.g. "BUTTON#x.onclick"), event timing tap ≥250ms, and a rAF frame timer that only runs while a finger moves (≥4 hitches over 60ms or one ≥150ms) → "Scrolling stutters on X"; Nexi's lines: "stuttered", "a tap… was slow to respond", "scrolling… was choppy"; lag reports count as one hiccup in his message. The hourly bugs.yml schedule hadn't started on its own yet (GitHub delays new schedules), so the daily routine now runs bugs.yml by hand first. Full sweep at 412/859 with the detectors: 0 false reports.
- v199: more automatic eyes (the owner said Nexi found nothing on his own; v196–v198 only saw crashes, off-screen buttons and, since v198, lag). `bugLook(root, scr)` runs after `bugLayout` (wrapped; idle, once a screen settles): cut-off text without "…" (own box clipped, or nowrap text past a clipping card), text drawn on top of other text, and a screen panel that came up blank. `lookText` only takes text that's really drawn: on screen, `checkVisibility` (opacity/visibility/content-visibility), not clipped away (`bugVisible`), inline text compared line box by line box (`getClientRects`), and pairs skipped when one side is on a pinned (sticky/fixed) bar (content scrolls under those on purpose). Skips `LOOK_SKIP` (decor, Nexi, toasts, aria-hidden). Tuned against the full sweep (every screen + game pages + settings at 412/859/360): 0 false reports; planted cut/overlap/blank all caught. Cost 10–18ms at 4x throttle, once per screen in idle. Nexi's lines: "Some text on X is cut off / is overlapping", "X came up blank". What automation still can't see: things that look wrong but are laid out "correctly" (ugly spacing, wrong colours, a picture that's the wrong game) — those need 🐞.
- v200: the owner's two lags. Queue scroll: each row's logo (`.rowart img`) had five stacked filters (brightness, contrast, three drop-shadows, two blurred) on a rotated image; with realistic art (logos on every game, 3840px heroes, 40 games, 2.6x dpr, 4x throttle) unfolded scroll raster was 693–867ms vs 536–563 now (one thin pale edge, readable on dark logos). The blurred halo is opt-in (`html.rowglow` when `S.rowArtGlow` > 0; default 0, the old default 7 migrated once via `S.rowGlowMig`). Game page open: (1) the grow-from-tap animation animated `borderRadius` (not compositable → the whole page repainted every frame) for 480ms starting from opacity 0 → now transform/opacity only, 300ms, from .35/.93; (2) the New page's backdrop was a CSS background (a 3840px decode held the first frame) → `<img class="gd-bg" decoding="async">` that fades in (`.in` on load); (3) `warmGame(g)` starts on pointerdown on [data-hub]/[data-opg]/[data-home-open]/.deck-g. Film at 4x: fully in by ~310ms vs ~470. Problem checks never run within 1.2s of touch/scroll (they wait). `lookText` memoises ancestor styles/clip boxes/pins per pass. Report a problem: "📍 Show me where" (`bugPoint` → `bugSnap`: tap the spot; records element path, size, scroll vs client, styles, clipping chain, picture state; never text) → `at` + `stack` of the user report; bugsync labels it "What was at the spot they tapped". Test harness: `BIG=1` in h.cjs uses 3840×1240 heroes (H*.jpg) and a logo for every game (L*.png); trace.cjs (CDP trace: RasterTask/decode), film.cjs (screencast of an opening), gopen.cjs, lookcost.cjs.
- v201: picture sizes before you pick (the owner likes HQ art). `probeImage(url)` now resolves `{w, h}` (still falsy on failure, so old `if (await probeImage(u))` checks work). `picTier(w, h)` by the long side: 4K ≥3000, HD ≥1500, Low <700, else nothing; `picQ(w, h, on)` = the `.pq` label (`on` = over a thumbnail); `picArea(x)` for SGDB items. SteamGridDB browse (`sgdbFind`) shows every pick's size from the API's `width`/`height`, sorted sharpest first (stable, so equal sizes keep the vote order), up to 12 per row; grids ask for `600x900,660x930` (SGDB's fixed sizes; bigger covers come from Steam's `library_600x900_2x`). Auto-pick/fill (`sgdbTop`) takes the sharpest of the top 5. Steam art tiles (`bindSteam`) and pasted links (`bindLink` message) show the probed size. Test: scratchpad hq.cjs (fakes the helper's /sgdb and Steam's CDN; needs `serviceWorkers: "block"`, since sw.js fetches Steam images itself and page.route can't see those).
- v202: 4K art never showed in 4K (owner picked a 3840×1240 hero, the game page looked soft). The light-then-full swap (`liteUp`) was kicked by a 650ms debounce on every DOM change, and the live countdown (`.cdb`) changes the DOM twice a second (new digit, then the ghost digit leaves at 520ms), so while a countdown existed anywhere (the Home ticket is always in the DOM) liteUp never ran: every SGDB/Steam/RAWG picture stayed on its light copy (measured: 0 of 6 game pages showed the 3840 art before, 6 of 6 after, same frame cost). Now the MutationObserver only kicks for added nodes that are/contain an `<img>` or `style="…url("`, a pending kick can't slip more than 2s (scroll still waits until you stop), and pointerup kicks too. pnSweep ignores the countdown's `<i>` digits (it ran twice a second). Rule: don't debounce idle work on "any DOM change"; filter the mutations or cap the wait. Nexi learned it: `bugBlurry()` (in a bugLayout wrapper, quiet 1.2s, not touring) finds an img/background still on its light copy (`LITE_FULL.has`, never tried `_lu`) drawn >1.4× bigger than it can carry, re-checks 3s later, and reports "A picture stayed blurry" (Nexi: "A picture on X stayed blurry."). Planted (liteUp disabled) → reported; sweep at 412/859/360, dpr 2.6, BIG art: 0 blurry reports (the Played "slow" report in the harness happens on v201 too). Browse and choose also shows RAWG screenshots (`rawgShots(title)`: editing.rawgId when the title matches, else `search_precise`; `/games/{id}/screenshots` gives width/height; thumbs via `/media/resize/420/-/`) as a wide "hero" row. Harness: scratchpad sharp.cjs (fake SGDB hero + hero_thumb at 693×770 dpr 2.6), blurplant.cjs, swapcost.cjs, bugt2.cjs (WAIT/DPR env).
- v203: game pages go sharp in ~0.6s instead of ~1.2s after a tap. warmGame (pointerdown + swipe neighbours) already downloaded and decoded the full pictures but the page still waited for the 650ms + idle liteUp kick (which then decoded them again). Now warmGame's decode calls `warmDone(u)`: the URL goes in `LITE_DEC`, liteUp swaps a LITE_DEC picture without another decode, and one liteUp pass runs 330ms after openFull (after the grow). Don't mark those URLs `LITE_WARM` early (a page drawn with the full 4K straight away decodes it on its first frame: long-frame total doubled), and don't swap every `#full` image (the off-screen neighbour pages cost a 170ms frame); liteUp's on-screen check does it. Measured (4x, dpr 2.6, BIG): long frames equal to v202. Harness: sharpt.cjs (time to sharp after a tap), swapdbg.cjs (LoAF per open).
- v204: Nexi's personalities are characters. `NX_PLINES[persona]` = ~21 context lines each ([when(c), text(c), act(c)] over `nxPCtx()`: pick/top/fut/days/played/un/ranked/sT/avg/duels/best/playing/hours/longest/genre/streak/peak/time of day/weekday/comp); `nxPersonaLine()` (no repeat of the last 6) is used for 60% of unprompted lines, prefixed with `NX_RAW` (⁣) so `nxVoice` leaves it as written; `nxMoreLines()` adds everyday lines for all. Faces/moves: `#pnbub[data-p]` (set by `nxPDress()`, which also adds `.eyes .acc` for accessories): hype sparkly eyes + grin, butler half-lidded + gold monocle, coach relaxed + sweatband, rival brows + smirk, bard ✦ eyes + singing mouth + drifting ♪, nerd glasses + scanning eyes. Persona rules use `#pnbub:where([data-p=x])` so moods (#pnbub.happy …) still win; idle move per persona replaces bubBob (`:where(html:not(.still))`), `.sig` = a signature move for 950ms each time he speaks. Double tap opens the wheel (was a poke → angry/faint, removed); wheel has ⚙ (setJump to Settings → Nexi); both wheel rings are half circles on the open side (r 122 / 178) so nothing is squeezed at the edge. Radar: chip moved from the Queue header to Plan's header (`.tbacts` wraps Radar + Customise; in the shx header card they share grid area c; "Radar" word hidden under 400px). #57 (owner: "Nexi aura ring is square"): idle peek added class `pl`/`pr` and never removed it; `.pl` is another component's class with `overflow: hidden`, so #pnbub clipped his glow. Now `pkl`/`pkr` (and removed on un-peek paths). Learned: a bugLayout wrapper reports when #pnbub's computed overflow/clip-path/contain would clip the glow (Nexi: "My glow turned into a square."). Planted `pl` → reported; sweeps at 412/859/360 with Nexi floating: 0. Rule: give runtime state classes on shared elements unique names (check `grep '\.name\b'` first); class clashes have bitten v164 (.aw), v195 (.dash) and now .pl.
- v205: Taste → Insights. Psychology, Patterns and Analytics merged into one sub (`SUBS.taste` = insights/duels/friend, `SUBDEF.taste = "insights"`; `setSub` maps psych/patterns/analytics → insights, so old saved views and goTab("psych") land there; Customise groups use data-sub="insights"). `tasteInsightsPage()` (last scripts; renderTaste is wrapped and swaps it in for tastePsych, so the old frame, pill and handlers stay), all classes `ix-*`. Order = how attention works: 1 identity (`ixType()`: Oracle ≥75% acc and ≥60% hits / Optimist / Skeptic (not with Oracle) / Genre Devotee / Tough Critic / Big Heart / Collector / Finisher / Explorer / Duelist; strongest names you, next 3 are trait chips; not enough scores → progress bar), 2 judgement (hype-accuracy ring + hype-vs-verdict track, mini stats, 2 reads + "N more about you" fold), 3 What I've learned (tasteInsights + top-5 strip), 4 taste shape (radar; when empty it moves to the bottom), 5 "How your next games will land" (hype hollow → expected filled), 6 folds: What gets you / What decides your score / Where your hype misleads you / All the numbers (open state in `S.ixOpen`; bodies built only when open, `IX_BODY`). Reveals: `.ix-rv` + IntersectionObserver add `.in` (transform/opacity); pieces already seen don't replay on a redraw until you leave the tab (`ixShown`, cleared in a goTab wrapper). Dots on tracks are positioned without measuring: a full-width `<s>` layer slid by `translateX(var(--x) * 100%)` (measuring after render forced a full layout: renderTaste 53ms → 14ms at 4x). Bars grow with scaleX/scaleY (`.pre` on a freshly filled fold body lets them animate). Wide screens: everything full width, small folds pair up (`align-items: start`). Also fixed: the old Analytics page printed a stray "})()}". Perf (4x, dpr 2.6): renderTaste 13–25ms vs 9–16 (old Psychology), tab switch equal, scroll drop share equal. Harness: ix.cjs, ixgate.cjs, ixperf.cjs, ixprof.cjs.
- v206 (lag reports #55–#63): (1) #55/#56 "4.3s freeze" on Download backup was the native `confirm()` ("Put your RAWG key in this backup?") waiting for the owner. `alert/confirm/prompt` are wrapped to record their time (`BUG_MODAL`, `bugModal(t, d)`) and the longtask, long-animation-frame and slow-tap checks skip anything overlapping one (planted 1.5s confirm: old build reported a 1500ms freeze + stutter, now nothing; a real 450ms busy tap is still reported three ways). (2) #59 blamed "tick ← FrameRequestCallback" (the scroll-stutter sampler's own 7ms) for a 170ms frame: a script under 30% of the frame is no longer named as the cause ("rendering …; biggest script Nms: …"). (3) #58 260ms tap on Edit → Save: `render()` rebuilt all seven screens. Now (last scripts) it rebuilds the visible panel (`panelOf(curTab())`, plus Home when on Add) and marks the rest in `RND_DIRTY`; `rndKick` rebuilds one per idle slice; `paintTab` flushes the target first; each renderX wrapper clears its own flag; visibilitychange flushes all. Save tap at 4x (859×847 dpr 2.63, big art): ~570ms → ~310ms. (4) `renderHome` 113ms → 20ms at 4x: `revealSetup` read every card's rect right after the redraw (forced layout, 99ms) → the IntersectionObserver's first report decides which cards start below the fold (`pnRevIO`, `el._rv`); `pnStack` reset `.stk > *` scrollTop straight after building (37ms) and `pnFit()` read computed style/rects (32ms) → both moved into requestAnimationFrame. Rule: never read layout (rects, scrollTop, computed style) right after an innerHTML redraw; do it in rAF or let an observer report it. Game-page open/scroll (#61/#62) and Home first-scroll (#60) are from v200 (before v202/v203's picture changes) and didn't reproduce: worst frames 60–100ms at 4x. Harness: scratchpad lagloaf.cjs (MODE=home|game|save|chat; LoAF + event timing), rhprof.cjs (CPU profile of renderHome), ctx.cjs, stale.cjs (deferred screens are fresh on arrival), modal.cjs.
- v207: #69 (owner: "Nexi double tap doesn't work, still tap and hold"). With real touch the wheel did open on the second pointerup, but the tap's compatibility click (~6ms later) hit-tested onto the new full-screen `#bubwheel` backdrop and its onclick closed it at once (a mouse click goes to the pressed element, so desktop tests passed). The wheel now ignores clicks in its first 350ms; the double-tap window is 400ms (a single tap to show the rail waits that long) and touch allows 14px of drift (was 6px Manhattan). Test with touch, not mouse: scratchpad dtap.cjs (CDP Input.dispatchTouchEvent, hasTouch context; old build fails, new opens at 250–400ms up-to-up). Learned: a body-level MutationObserver reports any overlay in `OVL` (bubwheel, shuf, wrap, fxrad, fxboss, bugtip, nxbug) removed within 200ms of being added ("A menu opened and closed at once"; Nexi: "A menu on X closed as soon as it opened."). Planted 20ms close → reported; sweeps 412/859/360: 0. Lag reports #72–#76 (v201/v202, before v206) re-measured at 859×847 dpr 2.63 4x: row tap 50–130ms except a first-open ~430ms (script ~140ms, the rest the FLIP glide's layout), edit tab 104–152, view switch 144–296, queue scroll ≤64ms frames, same as the old build; the after-add ones (#73/#74) came from render() rebuilding every screen, fixed in v206. Harness: lag2.cjs, rowprof.cjs (CPU profile + call paths of forced layouts), ovplant.cjs.
- v208: Nexi's new senses (the owner: "he still misses the problems I run into, even design ones"). All in the last scripts. (1) Flicker: `flickWatch(el, name, opening)` samples an overlay's effective opacity every frame for 750ms after closeFull/closeEdit/closePeek/dyClose (and openFull/openEdit) and reports a dip ≥.4 that comes back (or, opening, a peak that falls away). (2) Stuck taps (kind `stuck`): after a tap (≤12px, ≤600ms) on anything in `LOOK_TAP` or with `cursor: pointer`, a MutationObserver watches 900ms for any visible change (ignores `#pnbub, .cdb, #grum, #loadbar, #nxbug…` and changes on invisible elements; scroll and window.open count as a response); two dead taps on the same element within 8s → "Tapped something twice and nothing happened". (3–7) `lookDesign(root, scr)` after bugLook in the idle pass (quiet 1.2s): text cut short (line clamp or overflow) with no read-more control of its own and not inside something tappable; text under 3:1 contrast (2.2 for large) against its real background (`lookBehind` composites ancestor background colours; skips gradients/pictures/backdrop blur and anything else drawn under it inside the same card, e.g. a progress fill; skips when the point isn't hit-testable); tap targets under 24×24 not inside a bigger tappable; `<img>` with object-fit fill squashed >15%; pictures that failed after their retry. Kind `look` ("Design" issues). (8) Design review (kind `review`, helper `POST /review`, `HELPER_V = 5`): once per screen per APP_V (max 4/day, `S.revDone`, `S.reviewOff` switch in Settings → This build), 3s after a screen settles, `lookMap()` sends ≤80 lines "id | kind | x,y,w,h | font | colour on bg (contrast) | radius | scrubbed text" (titles → ‹game›, never sent); Llama 3.3 70B answers ≤3 problems citing ids; the app files those with confidence ≥ .75 whose ids exist. Old helper (404) doesn't raise a helper report. Perf: shared `lookText` (memo 400ms) + per-pass style cache (`LOOK_CS`): design pass 13–22ms at 4x, review map ~25ms once per screen/version. Proven: planted clamp/faint/tiny/squashed/dead/flicker all reported; #70's real long description reported before the fix; a working button tapped twice, a clean fade and a dropdown over text are not; deep sweep (every screen at top/middle/bottom + game pages scrolled + edit) at 412/859/360: 0; tapping 53 buttons twice across screens: 0 stuck. Overlap check: a layer with an opaque background between the two texts means covering, not overlapping (#71, the Home search dropdown over Up next), and text inside a rotated/skewed card is skipped (`lookTilt`, the Home ticket). Fixes it found: queue `.rank` numbers were var(--edge) (1.9:1) → var(--mute); `.icon` buttons min 32×32 (were ~23); year chart month bars were the buttons (3px tall when quiet) → full-height buttons with the bar in `::before` (`--h`). #68: v145's `smooth("closeFull")` faded the page out, removed its fade (page snapped back), then the v200 close faded it again → removed for closeFull (screencast: 1→0→1→0 before, one fade after). #70: `.gd-desc` gets "Read more / Read less" (`[data-descmore]`, over 170 chars). For the daily fixer: `look`/`stuck` reports are measured facts (reproduce, fix, or explain); `review` reports are an AI's opinion: screenshot the spot in Playwright, fix it if a designer would agree, otherwise comment why it's fine and close. Harness: plant208.cjs, bugt5.cjs (deep sweep), tapall.cjs, review.cjs (mocked /review), flick.cjs, r68.cjs (screencast frames), ldcost.cjs/ldprof.cjs.
- helper fix (no app version): v208's deploy failed ("Unterminated string literal", helper.js:122) because an editing script turned `items.join("\n")` into a real line break inside the string. Before pushing any `worker/helper.js` change, check it as a module: copy it to a `.mjs` file and run `node --check` on that (a plain `node --check helper.js` treats it as CommonJS and isn't a real check), and exercise a changed route in Node with a fake `env` (scratchpad revtest.mjs does /review and /version with a stub `env.AI`).
- v209: #81 (owner: "4K picture keeps showing low-res", game page backdrop showed the 850×274 SGDB `hero_thumb`). liteUp's swap gave up for good after one failed download or one `decode()` refusal (it swallowed the error and marked the element `_lu`), and bugBlurry skipped `_lu` elements, so Nexi never saw it. Now `go()` uses a loaded picture even when `decode()` rejects, retries a failed download twice (`?r=1`, `?r=2`, 2.5s/5s apart), and only after that gives up (`LITE_NOFULL`, element `_lu = "fail"`, which bugBlurry reports). warmGame also counts a loaded picture whose decode() was refused. Harness h81.cjs (fake SGDB 3840×1240 hero + 850×274 thumb; FAILN=n fails the first n downloads, DECFAIL makes decode() reject): old build stuck soft in every case, new sharp after 1–2 failures and with decode rejecting; 9 failures → reported as blurry. Game open at 4x equal (≤1 long frame of 55–63ms either build). bugsync.mjs: a closed issue seen again on a version older than its "Fixed in vN" comment stays closed (comment only) — #55/#56 reopened from a v205 sighting of a v206 fix.
- v210: fuller header cards (the owner liked v194's card but found it empty). Extras, each switchable: spotlight line (`.shx-ln`, a button that opens its game: Queue "Up next", Library "Newest", Played/Taste "Your #1", Replay "Replay next", Plan "Next out", Add "Last added"), top genres (`.shx-tg`, top 3 with 2+ games in that screen's set), 6-month chart (`.shx-ch`: added / finished / duels per month, Plan = releases in the next 6; bars scaleY, grow once per change of covers), mix bar (`.shx-mx`: Library played/playing/waiting/coming, Queue playing/out now/coming, Played + Taste tiers S/A/B/C/unscored, Replay, Plan this week/month/later; flex segments + legend) and backdrop art (`.shx-art`: the front game's `bgSource` at .4 behind a fade). `shxExtra(kind, front)` → data, `shxExtraHtml(x, on)` → markup (its `sig` joins shxDress's key). `S.shxX` = list of on extras (`SHX_X`; default = all, `SHX_FULL`); "Simple" = `["fan"]` = the v194 card; Off = `S.shxOff`. Settings: own group "Header cards" (`shxSetHtml()`/`shxSetWire()`: #s-shx Full/Simple/Off, #s-shxx one chip per extra) in App settings and injected into every screen's Customise (not into the search index, which keeps the App one). State classes on the card are `shxw-<k>` — `shx-<k>` clashed with the element classes (.shx-art made the whole card 72% wide). Phones: the chart sits under Customise (title and Customise span both columns so the chart column stays narrow); ≥620 it sits in the top row beside Customise.
- v211: cloud backup (the owner's data lived only in the phone's browser storage; file backups were manual). Last `<script>` in `index.html`. Restore code `PN-` + 20 chars of `CB_ALPHA` (100 random bits) made on first run (`cbEnsure`); from it (`cbKeys`, plain SHA-256 with prefixes) come the vault id (24 hex), the write token and the AES-GCM key — the code never leaves the phone. Sealed copy = [1][gzip flag][12-byte iv][ciphertext] (`cbSeal`/`cbOpen`, gzip via CompressionStream when present); the plaintext is `{"rk": RAWG key}` + "\n" + the saved state string straight from localStorage (`cbText`), so no extra stringify. Hash, gzip and encryption run in a Blob worker built from the same functions' source (`cbSealOff`; main-thread fallback): on the main thread a 1.4 MB state cost long tasks up to 320ms at 4x. Cloud bookkeeping lives in its own key `gq:cloud` (`CB`: code, on, at, hash, n, kb, err, saved), NOT in S — otherwise every backup would change what's being backed up. Schedule: every save marks dirty and `cbKick`s (first run ≥15s after a kick, then ≥10 min apart, 5 min after an error); leaving the app uploads at once if dirty; the same hash is never re-sent. Far fewer games than the last copy (<½ of ≥6) sends `keep=1`. Helper (`HELPER_V = 6`, `vault()` in worker/helper.js): `POST /vault/put?id&n&v[&keep=1]` (body = sealed bytes, ≤20 MB, one per 20s → 429, client retries after 22s), `GET /vault/list`, `GET /vault/get?k=latest|d0..d6|k`, `POST /vault/del`; every call `Authorization: Bearer <tok>`, the first write keeps sha256(tok) in the KV metadata and later calls must match; app origin only. KV `vault:<id>` = latest, `:d<UTC weekday>` = the previous copy when the first write of a new day lands (7 daily copies), `:k` = the copy before a keep=1 write; TTL 400 days. Settings → Cloud backup (☁️, App scope, own group; "Backup" in Most used points here): On/Off (`#s-cb`), status (`#s-cbst`, `cbStatus()`), the code (`#s-cbcode`), 🔑 Save it (`cbSaveCode`: Chrome → PasswordCredential into Google Password Manager; else the share sheet; else copy), Copy, Back up now, Restore from a code… (`cbRestoreSheet`: dy sheet with a hidden username + password-type field so password managers fill it, lists the copies, confirm, then `readBackup` + `applyBackup` and the phone adopts that code), Delete the cloud copy (when off). The Queue's backup nag (`backupDue`) is silent while the cloud is healthy and the code saved; with the cloud on but the code unsaved, `nudgeBar` asks to save the code (`#nudge-cbsave`). 3 failed tries in a row while online → `helper` problem report (not for an old helper). Tested with the real worker code over an in-memory KV in the harness (scratchpad h.cjs `helper: true`, page on https://pn.test so crypto.subtle exists; cbt.cjs round trip A→fresh B, cbfail.cjs, cbwipe.cjs, cbperf.cjs/cbprof2.cjs): copy unreadable without the code, wrong/unknown code refused, restore + reload keeps the data, unchanged state skips the upload, wipe keeps "Before a big change". Upload long tasks seen in the harness come from Playwright's request interception copying the body, not the app.
- v212: "Where was I?" notes (last scripts). A game's `g.wasI` = [{t, x}] (newest last, 20 kept, 240 chars). `wiCard(g)` on the New game page under the tags: the newest note ("Where you left off · N days ago", or "Welcome back · last time, …" when it's 2+ days old, class `.back`), or for a "playing" game with none a dashed prompt (`.wi-empty`); the 📍 tile in `hubActs` (`data-wasi`) is hidden on the New page while the card shows, and is the way in on Showcase/Plain. `[data-wasi]` anywhere (capture listener) → `wiSheet(id)`: textarea + quick phrases (`WI_QUICK`) + Save + earlier notes with 🗑. `wiAdd` saves and `wiRefresh` swaps `.wi[data-g]` in place + renderHome. Home Playing now cards show `📍 note` (`small.wiln`). Asking: `wiAsk()` when the app comes back (or opens) after ≥3h away — the last-seen time is its own localStorage key `gq:seen` (NOT in S: a change to S on every exit would mean a cloud upload every exit) — for `wiPick()` (playing, non-competitive, most recent note first), at most once per 20h per game (`S.wiAsked`) and not when a note is <20h old; waits for no tour/sheet/game page/#nxbug; card `#wiask` (Update/Write it opens the sheet, Not now, hides after 15s). Off switch `S.wiAskOff` (Settings → Extra features → "Where was I? reminders"). The comeback notification becomes "👋 Back to X? Last time: <note>" (link `./?nudge=game&g=`), and `nxContext().playing` carries "(they left off: …)" so chat tips know where you are (no helper change). Harness: wit.cjs (flow; fake the away time with an init script, since a reload's pagehide marks gq:seen), wisweep.cjs (0 reports at 412/859/360).
- v213: price alerts, Steam + PlayStation Store (last scripts, all `px*`). Watched = waiting games (not played/dropped/competitive) whose platforms include PC/Steam (Steam) or PS (PS Store), or that have none set (both), or that are tied to a store item; switches `S.pxSteam`/`S.pxPS`, all off with `S.pxOff`. `g.pxSt` (Steam app id; `g.steamId` is used when present) / `g.pxPs` (PS product id `XX0000-XXXX00000_00-XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX`) tie a game to a store item: found by title the first time, or pasted in the game's 💸 Prices sheet (`pxSheet`; a PS link must be a /product/ link, not /concept/); -1 / "-" = "not on this store"; a title that found nothing is looked up again after 14 days (`pxStNo`/`pxPsNo`). `pxRun(force, onlyGame)` → helper `POST /prices` {cc, items [{k, t, st, ps}], min, id+watch when notifications are on with the 💸 kind} at most every 12h on open (and one game right away when its page opens without a price, ≤1/h); results in `g.px = {st, ps}` ({id, name, cur, base, now, pct, baseF, nowF, low, lowF, at, t0}); a lower price than last time (≥ `S.pxMin` % off) = a drop → toast + Nexi's line (act "deals" opens `pxDeals()`) + `S.pxNews`. Region `S.pxCC` (2 letters; default from the time zone `PX_TZ`, else the language, else us; Steam `cc=`, PS locale `en-<cc>`; changing it clears g.px). Game page (New): `pxCard(g)` under the Where-was-I card (a row per store: price, struck base, −N%, tap = open the store `pxUrl`; "Price alerts ›" → sheet); the 💸 tile in `hubActs` (`data-px`) is the way in elsewhere. Settings → Price alerts (💸, App scope, `#s-pxbox`): on/off, status, See deals, stores, region chips (`PX_REGIONS`), Tell me when (any / 25% / 50%). Notification kind `deal` ("💸 Price drops", in `NX_PICK_DEF`; existing pick lists get it once via `S.pxKindMig`). Helper (`HELPER_V = 7`): `pxCheck(cc, items)` — Steam `storesearch` (best title match ≥ .7) then `appdetails?appids=…&filters=price_overview` (up to 50 ids in one call; formatted prices come from there); PS Store has no API, so `pxPsParse` reads the embedded page data: each flat price object around `"basePriceValue"` (discountedValue, currencyCode, basePrice/discountedPrice strings; `isTiedToSubscription:true` = a PS Plus price, skipped), the product id = nearest id before it (`pxPsIdBefore`, up to 40k chars back, because media sits between), the name next to that id (`pxPsName`); search results skip bundles/DLC/season passes; ≤8 lookups + 12 PS pages per call (Workers' ~50 outside requests). With id+watch the list (found items only, with today's prices as the baseline) is kept as KV `px:<nudge id>` (60 days); the cron takes the one checked longest ago (>20h), re-prices it and pushes up to 3 drops ("💸 X is 40% off", "Steam: $29.99 (was $59.99)", link `./?nudge=game&g=`, tag `deal-<id>`). nxPost helper-old on /prices doesn't file a problem report. This container can't reach Steam/PS, so the parser is tested on fixtures shaped like their data (scratchpad pxtest.mjs: helper only, real push encryption; h/fakestores.cjs + pxt.cjs/pxsweep.cjs/pxopen.cjs: the app with the real helper over fake stores). If PS prices stop appearing on the phone, the page format changed: look at pxPsParse first. Selling plan says no PlayStation Store: drop the PS half (or keep it off) in a sellable build.
- v214: PlayStation prices rebuilt from REAL store data + PS Plus. v213's PS reader parsed embedded page data, but the PS Store's search page is drawn in the browser (no prices in the HTML), so every PS game came back empty. `.github/workflows/price-probe.yml` (runs on pushes that touch `worker/helper.js` or the probe scripts, or by hand with titles/regions) runs the helper's own price code from GitHub's servers against the real stores and prints what each game would show — read it with get_job_logs (this container can't reach Steam/PS, and artifact downloads are blocked here). Now (helper `HELPER_V = 8`): search = the store's old "chihiro tumbler" (`store.playstation.com/store/api/chihiro/00_09_000/tumbler/<CC>/en/999/<q>?mode=game`, product ids + names; a second query with punctuation removed when the first finds nothing); price = the store's GraphQL with persisted-query hashes (`PSQ`: metGetProductById → concept id, metGetPricingDataByConceptId → GameCTAs); `pxPsPrice` picks the product's own CTAs (sku starts with the product id): buy price from ADD_TO_CART/PREORDER/DOWNLOAD (sale `endTime`), `UPSELL_PS_PLUS_GAME_CATALOG` (tierNumber 2 Extra / 3 Premium) = in the catalogue, `UPSELL_PS_PLUS_TRIAL` = Premium trial, other subscription CTAs with a price = member price (`plusNow`). The concept id goes back to the phone (`g.pxPsC`), so a re-check is one call. If the hashes ever stop working ("not whitelisted"), rerun the probe and update `PSQ`. Matching (`pxSame`, Steam and PS): every word of your title must be in the store's name ("Final Fantasy VII Rebirth" no longer matches plain FF VII, "Death Stranding 2" no longer the first game), extra words cost .06 each, threshold .55 both stores; accents stripped, "PS4 & PS5" ignored. Probe result (15 well-known games, US/UAE): Steam all right, PS 14/15 (FF VII Rebirth isn't in the store's search: paste its link). App: `S.psPlus` 0–3 (Settings → Price alerts → Your PS Plus, `#s-psplus`); `pxIncl(p)` = catalogue tier ≤ yours (Extra+) → the row says "✓ In your PS Plus Extra · or buy $X" (`.px-r.incl`); `pxEff(p)` = member price when you have PS Plus and it's lower → shown as "PS Plus price" and used for drops/lowest; sale end ("sale ends 8 Oct"), pre-order, "in PS Plus Premium" for a higher tier, Premium trial notes; a waiting game joining your catalogue → toast + Nexi line, and the helper cron pushes "🎮 X is in PS Plus Extra" (tier sent as `tier`). Deals sheet: "Included in your PS Plus" first, then sales. Also: the app now sends the list in batches of 10 (the helper merges each batch into the kept daily list, `b.all` = every watched id) so every game gets checked, not just the first few; Steam games with no price yet say "No price yet" (they showed "Free"), region-blocked ones "Not sold in your region". Tour steps are sorted by version (nxTour), so a later push of an older version's entries can't come first.
- v215: #102/#103/#99 "app frozen" after the tour: the tour does `closeFull(); nxShut()`; the second close took the instant path (`pnClosing` set) and left the first close's fill-forwards opacity-0 animation on `#full` (onfinish skipped `a.cancel()` because pnClosing was cleared). The next game page faded in and turned invisible 300ms later while still `on`, catching every tap. Fix: the instant path cancels pnClosing, onfinish always cancels, openFull cancels leftover animations on a closed #full. Rule: a `fill: "forwards"` animation must be cancelled on every path that ends it. Nexi learned (last scripts): (1) a tap on something big (≥35% of the screen) whose opacity chain is under .05 and not mid-animation = an invisible layer → its animations are cancelled (or the page is closed), the click is swallowed, kind `stuck` "An invisible layer was blocking taps"; (2) `contentvisibilityautostatechange` keeps the set of skipped elements; one still skipped while ≥40px of it is on screen on 3 checks during a scroll → `layout` "Part of the screen stayed blank while scrolling"; (3) lookDesign wrapper: a visible `[data-descmore]` whose text isn't clamped → `look` "A Read more button has nothing more to show". The AI design review now has `revTrue(x, els)`: a finding is filed only if the measurements agree (equal radius/font/height/gaps/alignment, a picture inside its own card, already-clamped text, mixed kinds of text → dropped; contrast/size claims dropped because lookDesign measures them; confidence ≥ .85). All 7 earlier review issues were false and fail it. #100: Read more showed for any description over 170 chars; on the unfolded Fold 300 chars fit in 4 lines → a ResizeObserver (`gdRO`, wired by a MutationObserver on #full) hides `.gd-rm` unless the text is really clamped. #85: Read more/less slides what's below (FLIP, transform) and fades the text up. #86: `.ix-rk` wraps (names no longer cut at 150px). #89: `#p-queue .row` no longer uses content-visibility:auto (fast flings showed blank rows; queue scroll LoAF at 4x 477→275ms). #84: pictures are full quality from the first frame (`S.picHQ`, default on; `liteFor(t, any)` returns null unless "Fast" is picked; bgBake still asks for the light copy with `any`). Settings → Look of the app → Pictures (`#s-pichq`). Perf (4x, 859, dpr 2.6, BIG art, median of 3): open game LoAF 183 vs 228ms, settings 234 vs 227, queue scroll 275 vs 477. Sweep 412/859/360 + 96 real taps: 0 false reports. Harness (scratchpad h/): h.cjs, t5.cjs (double close repro), t9.cjs (plants), sweep.cjs, tapall.cjs, bench.cjs, tour.cjs, rev.cjs.
- v216 (helper `HELPER_V = 9`): (1) The story so far: helper `POST /recap` {title, year, platform, about (RAWG description), hours, note, earlier notes} → 70B model writes 4–6 sentences of plot up to the note's point (told never to go past it and to keep to the setup when unsure) + `where` + `sure`. App (last scripts): `wiStoryHtml(g)` under the 📍 card on the New game page (`.wis`, 4-line clamp + Read more with the FLIP slide; "roughly" note when sure < .5), cached per note in `g.wiStory = {t (note time), x, w, sure, at}` (failure → {t, err, at}, retried after 6h). Automatic: fetched when a note is saved (wiAdd wrapper) and when a page with a note opens without one; the "Played X since last time?" card (`#wiask`) shows its first sentence. Switch: Settings → Extra features → The story so far (`S.wiStoryOff`). (2) Nintendo eShop prices: helper `pxNsFind` (Americas: Nintendo of America's Algolia index `store_game_en_us` with the public key their site ships; Europe/UK/AU/NZ/ZA: `searching.nintendo-europe.com`, `fq=type:GAME`), drops upgrade packs/DLC/bundles (`NS_JUNK`, Algolia `dlcType`), prefers the Switch 2 Edition only when the game's platforms say Switch 2 (`sw2`); `pxNsPrices` = `api.ec.nintendo.com/v1/price?country=XX&ids=` (≤50, `discount_price` with `end_datetime`). pxCheck takes `nscc` (the eShop region: there's no eShop in the Gulf, AE/SA return not_found → `nsCC()` falls back to US). App: store key `ns` everywhere (`PX_S = ["st","ps","ns"]`, `pxWantNs` = Switch platforms or none, `g.pxNs` nsuid / "-", `g.pxNsU` store URL, `g.pxNsNo`), `S.pxNS` store switch, `S.pxNsCC` (Settings → Price alerts → Your Nintendo eShop, `#s-pxnscc`, `NS_REGIONS`); sale end shown. Cron pushes eShop drops too. Probe (`.github/scripts/nsprobe.mjs`, run by price-probe.yml) runs the helper's real code: 12 Switch games correct in US/UK/AU and AE→US, a live US sale read with its end date. (3) Steam genres: helper `POST /steam` {cc, items [{k, t, st}], more?}: store search (≤8 lookups/call) → `IStoreBrowseService/GetItems` (names + weighted tag ids for up to 40 apps, no key) + `IStoreService/GetTagList` (names, KV `steam:tags` a week); `more` = app ids from `store.steampowered.com/recommended/morelike/app/<id>/` (age-gate cookies). App: `stSync()` by itself 15s after opening and once a day (batches; known ids cheap) → `g.stTags` (top 15), `g.stId` (its own field: being on Steam doesn't turn on Steam prices for a Switch game), `g.stAt`, `g.stNo` (not on Steam, relook 30 days); `stApply(g)` puts `stGenres(tags)` (≤4: specific tags first, `ST_GENERIC` skipped, a few `ST_BROAD` ones) at the front of g.genres and remembers them in `g.stG` so Settings → Games database → Genres from Steam (`S.stGenOff`) removes exactly those. `simTags` includes `stSimTags(g)` (simTags is a const: extend it, don't reassign it — reassigning threw and killed the rest of the script). Similar sheet: "Steam players also like" (`stMore(g)`, cached 14 days in `S.stMore`, rows show the tags shared with your game, ＋ adds via a RAWG lookup + simAdd, games you have link to their page). The v215 blank-while-scrolling check now skips hidden panels, invisible elements and anything covered (it fired on the hidden Played board from Plan). nxPost's `helper` report skips helper-old for /recap and /steam. Perf (4x, 859, 5 runs, median) vs v215: open game LoAF 270 vs 234 (noise ±50; worst frame 167 vs 183), settings 287 vs 263, queue scroll equal. Sweeps 412/859/360: 0. Harness: wis.cjs (recap, mocked /recap), st.cjs (Steam genres + similar + eShop, mocked helper).
- v217 (helper `HELPER_V = 10`), two automatic features (last scripts): (1) Release-date watcher `rdWatch()`: games you're waiting for (`rdWaiting`: upcoming, TBA, no date or a future date) are re-checked on RAWG (`games/<rawgId>`, or a precise title search that must `titleMatch`) every 3 days, ≤12 per open, 400ms apart, 20s after opening; a new/later/earlier date or back-to-TBA updates `g.date`/`g.status`, goes in `S.rdLog` (last 20, listed under Settings → Price alerts → Release dates), and gets a toast + Nexi line; then `nudgeOn(true)` re-plans the release/countdown notifications. `S.rdOff` switch. (2) Deal radar `drRun()`: Steam "More like this" (`stMore`) of your top 3 ranked + playing games that have a Steam id, minus games you have, scored by how many favourites point at each (≤20) → priced through `/prices` with keys `d:<appid>` (st known, no lookups); `S.drList`; new 50%+ → Nexi's line ("Players who love X also love Y…"); Deals shows "🔭 You'd probably like these · on sale" (≥30%). pxRun's `all` includes `drKeys()` so the helper keeps them in the daily watch; helper cron pushes a `d:` drop only at 50%+ and links to `./?nudge=deals`. `S.drOff` switch (needs price alerts + Steam on). Tested with fake RAWG/helper (scratchpad h/v217.cjs): delay + TBA detected and announced, radar found the 70%-off game and spoke. Perf (4x, 859, 3 runs): equal to v216 (open game 212, settings 294, queue scroll 336). Sweeps 412/859/360: 0.
- v218: "Finished it?"
