(last scripts, all `fn*`). `fnWhy(g)` for a playing, non-competitive game: latest 📍 note matches `FN_END` (final boss, credits, epilogue, beat it, finished, post-game, 100%…), or the story-so-far `where` does; logged hours ≥ 90% of `hrsOf(g)`; or with an hours-a-day plan (`dailyOf`) days since `g.plAt` (set when a game becomes playing by a syncPlayed wrapper; games already playing start now) × pace ≥ hrsOf (≥7 days). `fnCheck()` 9s after opening and after 3h+ away asks about one game (`fnPick`) in a calm moment (no tour/sheet/page/#nxbug/#wiask; retries every 3s); card `#fnask` shares the `#wiask` styles (`:is(#wiask, #fnask)`): 🎉 Yes, finished → `fnDone` (played + stamp, finish date today, then `qScore`), Not yet, I dropped it; one ask per game per 7 days (`S.fnAsked`). A note that matches FN_END asks right away (wiAdd wrapper). Notification: with the "playing" kind, nxPlan (wrapped) adds one "🏁 Did you finish X?" the next evening → `./?nudge=finish&g=` (opens the card, or the score if already played). Switch `S.fnOff` (Settings → Extra features → Finished it?). Lesson: when copying CSS rules, don't filter lines — multi-line rules break; share the selector with `:is()` instead. Tested (scratchpad h/fn.cjs) at 412/900: note and hours signals, Yes → played + score sheet, snooze, plan ping; sweeps 0; perf within noise.

- v219 (helper `HELPER_V = 11`): (1) #104 Settings stutter. The page has NO doctype, so it runs in quirks mode, where `querySelector("#id")` can't use the id map and walks the whole DOM (~.07ms per call, ~.3ms at 4x; Settings made ~220 calls per open). `$` now sends a plain `#id` to `getElementById` (checked: no `$("#…")` relies on quirks-mode case-insensitive ids). Don't add a doctype without a full layout sweep: standards mode changes layout. `setOnly(tpl, scope)` cuts other screens' setgroups out of the Settings HTML before it's parsed (same result, compared for every scope/sub). `pxCC` caches the time zone (it built an Intl.DateTimeFormat per call). Measured (4x, 859×847 dpr 2.63, median of 3, two rounds): open Settings js 123–135 → 27–41ms, worst frame 149–178 → 63–86; Customise 91–93 → 42–46 / 127 → 81–90; Edit 95–103 → 35–44 / 140–162 → 69–85; game page, tabs equal within noise. (2) Reviews are in (last scripts, `rv*`): games out within 3 days or ≤60 days old (not played/dropped/competitive/TBA) are checked by themselves (30s after opening, on return, and when their page opens; every 8h for 2 weeks, then 2 days): RAWG `games/<id>` → Metacritic (`metacritic_platforms` for your platform first; "PS5" = "PlayStation 5") + players' rating, and the helper's `POST /reviews` → Steam `appreviews` summary (games on PC/Steam or with a Steam id; `g.rvSt = -1` = not on Steam). `g.rv = {mc, mcP, us, un, st: {d, p, n}, at, ann}`; the first real score (Metacritic or 30+ Steam reviews) is announced once (toast + Nexi line, tap = the game; `S.rvLog`) and fills an empty `g.critic`. Notification (Release days kind): the evening after a release, "⭐ Reviews for X" → `./?nudge=reviews&g=`. (3) Your score, predicted: `rvFor(g)` = critic score (Metacritic, blended 70/30 with Steam % mapped by `rvStTen`) + how you land against critics (`rvBias()`: your score − critic/10 over scored games with a critic score, per genre when 2+, shrunk by n/(n+3), needs 3+), blended 65/35 with `expected(g)` (hype corrected by your record). `rvCard(g)` on the New game page before the description (any unplayed game with a critic or Steam score; "Not in yet" for fresh games without one). (4) Hours to beat (`hb*`): games with no main hours → helper `POST /hours` (big model, HowLongToBeat-style main/extras/100% + `sure`; only ≥ .6 used), ≤24 per day, 40s after opening; filled values kept in `g.hbV` (Edit shows "Typical time, filled in by Nexi" while the value is unchanged); `g.hbNo` retries after 30 days. (5) Still playing? (`sg*`): a playing game untouched for 14 days (`sgLast`: plAt, notes, sessions, logged-hours change `g.logAt`, page visits in localStorage `gq:seenG`, "still on it" `g.sgOk`) gets one question in the `#fnask` card: Still on it / Back to the queue / I dropped it; ≤ every 14 days per game (`S.sgAsked`). Switches: Settings → Extra features (`S.rvOff`, `S.hbOff`, `S.sgOff`). Detector fix: flickWatch's opening watch now stops when the sheet is closed (`el._flkShut`): opening and closing a sheet within 0.75s was reported as "The edit sheet flickers when it opens" (v218 too); a planted real flicker is still caught. Old helper (404) → no problem reports for /reviews or /hours, hours retry after 8h. Sweeps 412/859/360 (every screen, game pages incl. the review card, Settings, Edit, the Still-playing card): 0 reports. Harness note: Playwright screenshots force a big paint that the lag detector reports as a "stutter"; run detector sweeps without screenshots (and one at a time: parallel runs starve the CPU). Harness (scratchpad h/): h.cjs (seed, fake images), fake.cjs (RAWG + /reviews + /hours), rv.cjs, hbsg.cjs, tour.cjs (NOSHOT=1), sweep.cjs, bench.cjs, setperf.cjs, setprof.cjs, flk*.cjs; helper: hlptest.mjs.
- v220: six more automatic things (the owner asked for "unique, rare, out of the box"; research: trackers like Backloggr/GameTrack/WListDB auto-sync playtime and trophies, Spotify's AI DJ talks you through your own listening, time-capsule apps, wallpaper-coloured themes). All in the last scripts. (1) While you were away (`aw*`): back after 6h+ (`AW_GAP`), `awShow(since)` opens a dy sheet "While you were away · N hours · Nexi's report" listing `awItems(since)`, built only from logs that already exist (`S.pxNews`, `S.drList` at/pct, `S.rvLog`, `S.rdLog`, `g.hbV`+`g.hbAt`, `S.bugLog`, releases today/within 7 days, `etaClashes()`, the newest 📍 note); tap a line = that game (or Deals / Library). "🔊 Read it to me" = `speechSynthesis` (`awSpeak`, rate/pitch per persona in `AW_VOICE`, emoji stripped; stopped on close). Last seen comes from `window.PN_SEEN0`, which the v212 gq:seen block now sets before it marks the new visit (it used to overwrite it at once). Waits for a calm moment (no tour/sheet/page/#wiask/#fnask), else skipped. `S.awOff`. (2) Finish forecast (`eta*`): `etaOf(g)` for a playing game with hours: left = hrsOf − logged; pace = logged ÷ days since plAt (3+ days), else the hours-a-day plan, else `S.evening` × 4/7; `etaCard(g)` (🏁, New page before the description); `etaClashes()` = hype 7+ games out within 60 days but before the ETA; `etaFix(c)` says "About Nh a day finishes it in time", or above 5h a day "you'll probably switch to X and come back". One Nexi line per clash (`S.etaSaid`, 45s after opening). `S.etaOff`. (3) Now playing theme: `THEMES.chameleon` has getters for a/b from `chamGame()` (the playing game touched most recently by `sgLast`, else On deck #1) toned by `chamTone(hex, lightness)` (saturation ≥ .55, accent L .64, second L .42); a render/syncPlayed wrapper re-runs applyLook when the colour changes. `chamGame` (500ms) and `sgLast`'s gq:seenG parse (1s, cleared on write) are memoised: the theme picker read the getters per Settings open and the sort parsed localStorage per comparison (Settings js went 13–22 → 26–49ms at 4x before the memo; equal to v219 after, 20-open medians). (4) Seasons (`ssn*`): `ssnNow()` by the Umm al-Qura calendar (`Intl` `islamic-umalqura`, fallback `islamic`; checked: 1 Oct 2026 = 20 Rabiʿ II, Ramadan 2027 ≈ Feb 8–Mar 9, Eid al-Fitr Mar 10, al-Adha May 16–19): Eid al-Fitr 1–3 Shawwal, Eid al-Adha 10–13 Dhu al-Hijjah (🎊), Ramadan (🌙; `nxWindow` wrapped → [≥20, 23]), New Year Dec 31–Jan 2 (🥳, last year's finished count), Halloween Oct 24–31 (🎃, picks a horror/survival game). The hat goes in Nexi's existing `.hat` slot (`pnBubReact` wrapped; his birthday 🎂 still wins); lines are added twice to `pnBubLines`; one greeting per season per year (`S.ssnSaid`). `S.ssnOff`. (5) Battery saver: `navigator.getBattery` (Chrome; Safari has none) ≤15% and not charging → `html.still.batsave` (applyLook wrapped so it doesn't undo it), Nexi says so; charging → back to `S.motion`. `S.batOff`. (6) Then & now (`cap*`): a syncPlayed wrapper snapshots `g.cap = {h hype, pr Nexi's guess (rvFor or expected), cr critics, st plAt, ad added, hrs, n1 first note, at}` when a game becomes played; `capCard(g)` on a played game's New page: hype → score, Nexi's guess and how far off, days from start to finish (or in the library), critics vs you, first and last 📍 notes (needs 2 facts or a note). Settings → Extra features: switches for all of these (`#s-xaw`, `#s-xeta`, `#s-xcap`, `#s-xssn`, `#s-xbat`); the Theme note explains Now playing. Detector fix (Nexi's flicker check): closing a sheet and opening it again within 0.75s (Settings → Edit, Edit → Settings) was reported as "The sheet flickers when it closes" (v219 too). The closing watch now stops when an open call happens (`el._flkOpen`, set by the openFull/openEdit wrappers and by new openSettings/dyShow wrappers). A sheet that comes back without an open call is still reported (planted class toggle → reported; v219 also falsely reported both reopens). Sweeps 412/859/360 (all screens + away report + forecast with a clash + time capsule + Now playing theme): 0. Tour 412/900: 5 steps, all targets found (the away step points at Nexi's greeting when the list is empty). Perf (4x, 859): equal to v219 within noise. Harness (scratchpad h/): v220.cjs (all six; fake battery via init script, gq:seen 14h ago), flk4/5/6.cjs (flicker plants: an inline style can't beat the closed sheet's opacity rule, so toggle `.on` instead), micro.cjs (20-open medians), sweep.cjs (LASTV=220 so no tour runs: the problem checks are off while touring).
- v221: reports #118 / #114 / #123 + three more automatic things (last scripts). (1) #118 "Says checking in ps store and doesn't change it": a store answer of `none` (not found) or `err` used to leave `g.px[s]` empty, and the row said "checking…" forever (and the game-page trigger only ran for games with no `g.px` at all, never for the eShop). Now pxTake keeps `{none: 1, at}` / `{err, at}` (an older real price is kept over an error) and a missing price as `nop`; the row says "Not found on this store · paste its link…" / "Couldn't check just now · I'll try again" / "not checked yet", and "checking…" (`.pxck`) only while `pxBusy` (pxRun paints at start and end); opening a game checks every watched store without an answer (≤1/h, waits for a running check); the Prices sheet explains none/err. (2) #114 "Adjust the logo in the queue tab automatically to fit the tile": `lfMeasure(url)` measures each row logo once (localStorage `gq:logofit`: aspect + the box of its non-transparent pixels via a 200px canvas when the host allows CORS, else the plain aspect); `lfRule` sizes the content to ~36% of the tile's area, ≤80% of its height and ≤ its width, centred vertically, starting at the tile's left edge. The fit lives in one stylesheet (`#lfcss`, a rule per game, rebuilt only when its key changes), so a queue redraw needs no DOM writes; Chrome crops to the content with `object-view-box` (`@supports`), Safari draws the padded picture with margins (same placement). Applied at start (load() draws the queue before the last scripts exist), after renderQueue, and on goTab/setSub until the tile has been measured once (in rAF; it can't be measured while hidden). Switch: Customise → Queue cards → "Fit each logo automatically" (`S.lfOff`). Tried and dropped: absolute positioning (every rotated logo became its own layer: layerize ×5). (3) #123 (the friend: "the rail on the left and Nexi at the same time, with a good transition"): `html.nxseat` (wide screens, rail open, not touring): Nexi sits at the rail's foot (`seatPos` from offsetLeft/Top, NOT the rect: the rail slides in with a transform and mid-slide it's off screen; the rail keeps 92px of bottom padding; children scaled .84 so his glow fits), glides into the seat (MutationObserver on html's class → `seatSync(true)`) and out of it (pnBubShow wrapped: animate from the seat to his free spot). Seated taps go through a window capture listener: tap = a line, double tap / long press = his wheel (no drag). `S.nxSeatOff` (Extra features). #129 (owner: "the rail could open on the opposite side of Nexi"): a tap on floating Nexi now sets `S.railSide` from his position before showing the rail, so it opens on his side and he glides straight into it. Harness: seed `nxAsked: 1` (the first-run "what's your name?" card `#bubedit` covers Nexi otherwise). Nexi learned both reports: lookDesign wrapper → `look` "A price stayed on “checking…”" (a visible `.pxck` while no check runs) and "A logo is too small for its card" (a queue logo without a fit drawn under 30% of its tile's height; not when fitting is off). Planted both → reported; fitted → not. (4) Dusty backlog (`dust*`): released, waiting games untouched for 60/120/240 days (`dustDays`: added, release date, `sgLast`, `g.dustOff`) get dust specks over their queue row at 3 strengths (cobweb 🕸️ at level 3), from one stylesheet `#dustcss`; opening one blows it off (`g.dustOff`, a grey puff of transform/opacity dots `.dustpuff`, Nexi: "*cough* X had N months of dust on it"); Nexi's lines point at the dustiest one. `S.dustOff`. (5) App icon badge: sw.js `iconBadge()` sets the home-screen badge to the number of Play next notifications still showing (after each push, click and close); the app clears it when it's on screen and, when you leave, sets it to the count of games out today that you haven't opened. `S.bdgOff`. (6) Nexi's crystal ball (`orc*`): scoreWrite wrapped: scoring a game whose `g.cap.pr` (his guess, v220) exists gets "Called it! 🔮" (≤ .5), "Close" (≤ 1.5) or "You surprised me" once (`g.cap.got`); `orcCard()` at the end of Taste → Insights: calls within a point / N and the average miss, last 5. The first finish forecast is kept (`g.eta0`, etaOf wrapped) and Then & now shows "N days early/late vs my finish forecast" (`g.cap.e0`). Sweeps 412/859/360 (logos, dust, rail seat): 0. Tour 412/900 (6 steps): clean. Perf (4x, 859, 40 games with logos): queue scroll equal (0–1 long frames both); a queue redraw frame 18–20 → ~30ms: ~5ms is Nexi now visible in the rail seat (the same idle animation cost as Nexi floating), the rest drawing dust and fitted logos; script per redraw equal (11–14ms). Harness (scratchpad h/): h.cjs serves RGBA logos with see-through padding and CORS from img.test/L<n> (n%3: wide 600×100, tall 240×300, square), v221.cjs, rows.cjs, safpath.cjs (Safari path: strip the @supports rules), qmicro.cjs (SETTLE=ms, NOLF/NODUST/NOSEAT), qtrace.cjs (trace phases per redraw).
- v222 (daily fixer): #153 "Tour step v215 can't find what it points at" (friend's Mac, no ranked games): the v215 step pointed at Insights' top 5 (`.ix-rk`), which only exists once games are ranked → falls back to the "What I've learned" card; the v184 step still pointed at `.tins` (gone since v205's Insights merge) → same fallback + `goSub("taste", "insights")`. nxTour's show() only reports a missing target when the phone has ≥3 scored games (`scored`): steps for the ranking, Played board, finish dates and diary legitimately have nothing to point at without them (the step parks Nexi). Audit harness tourat.cjs (runs every WHATS_NEW go/at; SEEDK=norank|full; PLANT=1 plants a broken step): with scored games 0 of 115 steps miss; without, 5 miss and none is reported; a planted broken step is reported with scored games only. Triage: #150–#152 ("blank while scrolling" naming rows in hidden Played/Queue/Calendar panels while on Insights) are v215 false alarms already fixed by v216's hidden-panel check; #154 (510ms tap on the tour's Next into Settings, v215, Mac Safari): the step (setJump → Settings → Pictures) at 4x, 1080×810: v215 median 395–409ms vs now 174–216 (v219's Settings speed-up); the first open is ~420 in both, of which ~100ms is script and the rest the browser drawing the sheet the first time.
- v223: the owner preferred the queue logos as they were before v221's auto-fit (#114) → fitting is off unless turned on: `LF_OFF()` = `S.lfOff !== false` (undefined = off, so a settings reset keeps the classic look; Customise → Queue cards → "Fit each logo automatically" sets `S.lfOff = false`). The fitting code and the "logo too small" check stay, both only while it's on. No `#lfcss` rules by default, so a queue redraw does less than v222.
- v224: prices in one line + a tap on seated Nexi hides the rail. Prices (`pxCard`, rewritten): one row `.px-l` of store chips `.px-c` in the order PS Store, Steam, eShop (only the stores watched for that game), each with its logo (`PX_LOGO`, simple-icons paths: PlayStation, Steam, Nintendo Switch, drawn in `currentColor`) and a short price (`pxShort`: drops `.00` and Steam's " USD"; a currency code like "AED" is drawn small via `<u>`). The discount is a green tag on the chip's top edge (absolute, costs no width). The best deal gets the accent ring (`.best`): in your PS Plus (green, `.incl`), else the cheapest when the priced stores share a currency (the eShop can be another region's), else the biggest sale. States: `checking` (`.pxck`, opacity pulse, still what the v221 "stuck on checking" check looks for), "—" not checked, "n/a" not found / not sold, "retry" error, "soon" no price, "Free", "✓ Plus". Details live in each chip's title/aria-label and the Prices sheet. One store = full-width chip with the store name and the struck-out old price. The line under it (`.px-more`, opens Price alerts) says why the ringed one wins, the sale's end and the lowest seen (ellipsis). Narrow phones: `.px-l` is a size container; with 3 stores under 400px the chips go tight (also `.tight` from JS for long prices); `.px` grid uses `minmax(0, 1fr)` (a nowrap caption had widened it). Chips use `flex: 1 1 auto` (equal widths cut "AED 249" at 360). Rail: `railHideNow()` (shared with the » button); with Nexi seated (`html.nxseat`) one tap hides the rail (he glides out; tapping floating Nexi brings it back on his side), double tap / long press = wheel; the » button is hidden while he's seated (kept when the seat is switched off).
- v225: the cover you tap flies into the game page (last `<script>`/`<style>`). An outermost openFull wrapper: `cvSource()` takes the element from the last pointerdown (≤1.5s ago, `cvDown.el`; not inside #full / the tour / Nexi), its closest tappable container (`[data-home-open]`, `[data-opg]`, `[data-hub]`, `.deck-g`, buttons, rows…) and picks a cover-shaped `<img>` in it (28px+, width/height ≤ 1.25 so wide backdrops like the Home hero's `img.bg` are skipped, under 40% of the screen; the one under the finger first, else the biggest; `pointer-events: none` pictures count, which is why it doesn't use elementsFromPoint). `cvFly` hides the page's `.gd-cov` at once with `.cvwait` (`visibility: hidden`: an entrance animation on the cover animates opacity and beat `opacity: 0` for its first 170ms) and in the next frame `cvLaunch` measures its resting box (offsetWidth/Height + its centre mapped back through #full's grow transform, scale about `transformOrigin`), adds one fixed `<img class="cvfly">` (z 71, over #full's 70) and animates transform only from the source box (uniform scale, no tilt) to the cover (overshoot 1.06 / −6° at 72%, then −4° like `.gd-cov`), 380ms; on finish/cancel (or 900ms) the copy goes and the cover shows. Measuring in the click task forced an early layout (an extra long frame at 4x); in rAF it's equal to v224 (tap → page at 859×847 dpr 2.6, 4x, median of 5, 3 rounds: worst frame 42–51 vs 43–50ms). Only the New page style has `.gd-cov`; skipped with calm(), `S.hubAnim === false` (Customise → A game's own page → Motion on this page), the tour, a page already open (swipes), or no cover found. closeFull removes a copy still flying. Queue rows don't open the page (they expand the card), so they don't fly. Harness (scratchpad h/): fly2.cjs (per-frame position of the copy vs the cover), film.cjs (CDP screencast + filmstrip), dbg2.cjs (cover hidden during the flight), tapperf.cjs (COVERS=1 SMALL=1). Sweep 412/859/360: 0 (the 3 MB test cover gives "slow" reports on v224 too). Tour 412/900 fine. Also: v224 was merged via PR #160.
- v226: platforms as logos on the New game page (the owner: "replace PC / Switch / PS5 with logos"). Next to `platList`: `platOf(name)` matches a name from any source (RAWG "PlayStation 5", typed "PS5"/"ps5", "Nintendo Switch 2", "Xbox Series S/X", "PC"/"Windows", "macOS", "iOS", "Android", "Linux", "Steam Deck", "PS Vita", "PSP", "Xbox 360/One") to a family + version; `platChips(str)` groups a family's versions into one chip (PlayStation "4 · 5", Xbox "X|S · One", Switch "2") in `PLAT_ORDER`, and keeps up to 2 unrecognised names (Wii U, 3DS, retro…) as text; `platHtml(str)` = `<span class="plat">` with the logo (`PLAT_LOGO`, simple-icons CC0 paths; iOS is the word "iOS") + `<small>` version, full names in title/aria-label. gdMarkup's tag row = state + 3 genres + platHtml (was 3 platform names). `.plat::before` is a zero-width-space strut so a row of only logo chips is as tall as the name chips (27 vs 29px otherwise); `text-transform: none` on the version text (an inherited rule capitalised "One"/"iOS"). Switch back: Customise → A game's own page → Platforms → Names (`S.platText`, `#s-plattext`). Sweep 412/859/360: 0; tour 412/900 fine; open game at 4x equal to v225.
- v227 (helper `HELPER_V = 12`): the owner's GTA 6 had no PlayStation price though the store sells it. Two causes: (1) the helper's PS search (the old chihiro "tumbler") doesn't list some new games at all — "Grand Theft Auto VI" itself came back not found in US and UAE (same as FF VII Rebirth in v214); (2) "GTA 6" vs the store's "Grand Theft Auto VI" scored 0 in `pxSame`. Fixes: helper `pxPsFind` now asks the store website's own search first (`pxPsSearchWeb`: GraphQL `getSearchResults`, persisted hash in `PSQ`, vars {countryCode, languageCode "en", nextCursor "", pageOffset 0, pageSize 24, searchTerm}, results in `data.universalSearch.results` (Product with PS id, or Concept with a numeric id); skips add-ons/currency/themes/demos by `localizedStoreDisplayClassification`; GTA VI's standard product is classified "Game Bundle", so that class isn't skipped) and falls back to the tumbler (`pxPsFindOld`). The hash was found by loading store.playstation.com/<locale>/search/<term> in a headless browser on GitHub and recording its requests (`.github/scripts/pssearchprobe.mjs`, run by price-probe.yml with input browser=yes): the site computes persisted-query hashes at run time, so they are NOT in its scripts. If PS searches start failing, rerun that probe and update `PSQ.getSearchResults`. Names: `pxNorm` spells out a leading short name (`PX_ABBR`: GTA, FF, COD, RDR, TLOU, MGS, KH, NFS, GOW, DMC, SMT, BG, HZD, AC, MHW, RE; RE/AC/BG only before a number), turns Roman numerals into digits (`pxNum`) and drops apostrophes, on both sides; searches (PS + Steam) also try the spelled-out title (`pxExpand`). A concept id alone (`it.pc`, no product) is priced straight from the concept (the product id comes back from the buy CTA's sku). App: `pxItem` sends the concept even when the title search failed before (`g.pxPsC`), a pasted /concept/ link is accepted, and when a store says "not found" the app reads the game's RAWG store links (`pxRawgLinks`: `games/<rawgId>/stores`, store_id 3 = PS (/concept/ or /product/), 1 = Steam /app/) once per 14 days (`g.pxRw`) and re-checks (`pxRawgFix`, from a pxTake wrapper and 25s after opening for games already "not found"). Helper status in Settings → Nexi names v11 as one behind. Tested: matching table in Node (GTA 6/GTA VI/gta6/FF7 Rebirth/RE4/BG3 match, GTA V vs VI and FF VII vs Rebirth don't), the RAWG fallback in the harness (scratchpad h/rw.cjs: n/a → RAWG concept → helper → AED price), the real helper code against the real stores via price-probe.yml.
- v228 (helper `HELPER_V = 13`): the owner's STEINS;GATE ELITE showed PS "—" and Steam "soon", and the v226 platform chips "looked bad". (1) Platforms: v226 used class `.plat`/`.plats`, which already existed (old hub chips: 10px uppercase, hard border, `.plats` absolutely positioned) — that's what capitalised "IOS" and made the chips cramped. Now unique `.pfms` (one pill for all known platforms) with `.pfm` items: logo 17px, version right after it in the same colour/weight ("4·5"), thin dividers between logos; unknown platforms stay separate text chips. (2) "—" = a store never answered: a call can only look up a few games per store (the helper's per-call request budget), and the app sorted by the most recently checked store, so a game whose Steam/eShop prices were fresh never got its PS turn. Now `pxStale` sorts by the store checked longest ago (a watched store with no answer = 0), calls carry 5 games (was 10), and a second pass re-asks up to 10 games that still have a watched store with no answer (`pxGap`). (3) Helper: `pxPsSearchWeb` takes only games by `localizedStoreDisplayClassification` (Full Game / … Bundle / … Edition / concepts with none; NieR:Automata had matched the "Stellar Blade x NieR:Automata" Episode); `pxNorm` turns ®™© into spaces ("Persona®5 Strikers" was missed, so the Deluxe Edition won); a title with a colon is also searched by the part before it; Steam apps that are out but have no price (`coming_soon` false, not free; one `filters=basic,release_date` call each, ≤3) are "not sold here" instead of "no price yet". Debug view: price-probe.yml input debug=yes prints the raw PS web search results (typename, id, name, classification, score) and Steam storesearch/appdetails per title.
- v229 (helper `HELPER_V = 14`): prices in your currency, Egypt's PlayStation prices, Nexi's brain, iPad-aware problem reports. (1) Currency: the UAE/Gulf PS Store, the US eShop (the Gulf's fallback) and Steam in Egypt sell in USD. Helper `fxRates(env)` (route `/fx`, any method, no origin check; also returned as `fx` from `/prices` when the body has `fx: 1`): rates per 1 USD from open.er-api.com, fawazahmed0 currency-api as backup, KV `fx` 12 h. App: `S.fx` (`fxTake`, `fxGet`, `fxOld`), `pxMyCur()` (`S.pxCur`: "" = the region's currency via `PX_CUR`, "store" = no conversion, or a code; Settings → Price alerts → Show prices in, `#s-pxcur`), `pxTo(s, p, v)` / `pxM(s, p, v, storeText)` / `pxMx` convert a store value; units: Steam and PS send cents, the eShop the amount itself (`pxMaj`, checked on the real stores with the probe), `FX_PEG` (Gulf pegs) until rates arrive. Converted everywhere a price is drawn: chips (best deal compared in your currency, caption "≈ in AED"), tooltips/Prices sheet keep the store's own price ("Store price $44.99 · converted at today's rate"), Deals, drop toasts/Nexi lines, `S.pxNews`, deal radar (`x.cur` stored), and the helper's cron push ("≈ AED 165 ($44.99)", the phone sends `mc`). (2) Countries with no PlayStation Store (probe: eg, ma, dz, jo, iq, pk, ng, ph return nothing): `PS_NONE` → the UAE store for Arab countries, else US; `S.pxPsCC` picks another (Settings → Your PlayStation Store, shown only there or when set); helper `psCC(cc, pscc)`, `pscc` sent with every /prices call and kept for the cron; PS links use `pxPsCC()`. (3) Matching: `pxSame` takes .5 off when the store's name has a 1–2-digit number yours doesn't ("The Caligula Effect" had matched "The Caligula Effect 2" on Steam; now Overdose everywhere); a store that doesn't find your title is searched with the name another store knows it by (`n2` from the app = a store name differing from your title, plus this call's Steam name). One-time `S.pxMig229`: ids whose saved store name has a number the title lacks are dropped, every "not found" is looked up again. Yu-Gi-Oh! TAG FORCE GX really isn't on the PS Store. (4) Nexi's brain (last script): `nxSC()` (context memoised 20 s: deals in your currency, PS Plus, releases, finish forecast, clash, notes, dust, predictions, reviews, tiers, duels, habits, season), `NX_SMART` (~150 [when, text, act] lines, genre tips via `nxGen`), `nxSmartLine()` (no repeat of the last 20, data lines 80%), `nxNamed()` puts `S.userName` into ~45% of lines; pnBubSay wrapper uses a smart line for 45% of idle chatter; bubble acts deals/plan/settings; `nxFacts()` → `nxContext().facts`, and the helper's chat prompt uses them, uses the name in most replies and gives concrete reasons + a next step. (5) Problem reports: `bugDev()` says "iPad iOS 17.5" (an iPad's Safari sends a Mac user agent; `maxTouchPoints > 1` gives it away) / "iPhone iOS x"; bugsync titles carry the device family ("🐞 Layout (iPad): …") and Safari reports say to reproduce in WebKit. `.github/workflows/webkit-check.yml` (+ `.github/scripts/webkitcheck.mjs`): on pushes to main that change index.html (or by hand) it walks every screen, a game page, Settings, Edit and chat in Playwright WebKit at iPhone 11 / iPad / iPad landscape, catching the app's own /bug calls; fails on script errors; timing reports listed separately (no GPU on CI); screenshots in the "webkit" artifact. For the daily fixer: reports from the friend come from an iPhone 11 / iPad in Safari — reproduce them with that workflow (or WebKit), not Chromium; before v229 an iPad report said "Mac … Safari". Dry-run locally with `PW_PATH=/opt/node-tools/node_modules/playwright/index.mjs CHROMIUM=/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome node .github/scripts/webkitcheck.mjs`.
- WebKit check fixes (no app version): (1) the first run on main crashed at once ("Mouse wheel is not supported in mobile WebKit") yet passed, because `| tee` hid the exit code → the walk scrolls with `scrollBy` on the visible scroller (`scrollBy(page, dy)` in webkitcheck.mjs) and the step runs with `set -o pipefail`. Now it walks all 3 devices (~5 min): no script errors. (2) It reported Home's Nexi face (`.nxh-face`) "cut off by the left edge" on iPhone 11 — the same intermittent report the Chromium sweep gave on v228/v229. Probed (scratchpad h/face2.cjs): the layout pass ran 31ms into the panel's `slashin` entrance (#p-home translateX −24px), face at −8px; settled it's at 16px. `bugMoving(el)`: the cut-off-button check skips an element while it or an ancestor has a running, non-looping animation. Planted real cut (left −60px) still reported; face probe 0/8 (was 1/6); sweep 412/859/360: 0. Shipped without an APP_V bump (detector only, nothing to see; a bump would push an update notification for nothing).
- v230 (helper `HELPER_V = 15`): more places for game pictures (the owner: "more and better options, Steam or other powerful ones"). Helper `POST /art` {t, st?, cc, pscc, sw2, want?} → {found: {st|xb|ps|ns: {id, name}}, pics: [{s, r: cover/hero/art/shot, u, th (small copy), w, h, k (label)}]}. Shapes recorded from the real stores with `.github/scripts/artprobe.mjs` (run by price-probe.yml; `RAW=1` dumps the raw store answers): Steam = `IStoreBrowseService/GetItems` with include_assets + include_screenshots (`asset_url_format` + file names; newer games keep pictures under a hash folder, which is why guessing failed; base `shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/`; logo isn't listed, so `logo_2x.png`/`logo.png` are tried and dropped if absent; screenshots `ss_*.jpg` are the original size, often 4K, `.600x338.jpg` the small copy); Xbox = displaycatalog `productFamilies/autosuggest` (needs `productFamilyNames=Games`) → `products?bigIds=` (every picture with its size: SuperHeroArt 3840×2160, Poster 1440×2160, BoxArt 2160², Logo, Screenshot; small copy `?w=&h=`); PlayStation = the website's `getSearchResults` `media` (EDITION_KEY_ART / GAMEHUB_COVER_ART 3840×2160, PORTRAIT_BANNER 1440×2160, MASTER 1024², LOGO, SCREENSHOT; small copy needs `?w=480&h=480`, `?w=` alone is ignored); Nintendo = the US Algolia hit's `productImage` (Cloudinary id → `assets.nintendo.com/image/upload/<id>`, 1920×1080) + `productImageSquare`. Pictures without a size are measured from their first 64 KB (`artSize`/`artDim`: PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF headers, a Range request; ≤24 per call, screenshots ≤4 per store) — a missing picture is dropped. Matching is stricter than prices: `artSame` takes .3 off when the store's name has a word your title lacks (other than edition words), so "Hollow Knight" ≠ "Silksong", except a numbered title + the store's subtitle after a colon ("The Witcher 3" = "…: Wild Hunt"); Steam has its own search ranked by it (`artSteamFind`: the price search picked "The Witcher 3 REDkit"). ~1 s per call, ≤~35 outside requests. App (last script, `art*`): Browse and choose (`sgdbFind` replaced) shows SteamGridDB + Steam + Xbox + PlayStation + Nintendo + RAWG in rows Cover (`.sgrow.tall`, box-shaped first) / Backdrop / Logo (`.sgrow.logo`) / Screenshots, sharpest first, each tile tagged with its source (`.sgs`) and size; chips `[data-sgsrc]` (`.sgsrcs`) show one source at a time (`.sgoff`); a picture that fails to load hides itself; the picked one gets `.sgsel`. `artStores(title, {st, want})` caches per session (`ART_C`); the Steam id comes from editing.stId/pxSt/steamId when the title matches. Auto-pick (and `sgdbFillOne`/bulk, via a `sgdbTop` wrapper): a store fills a role SteamGridDB has none of (cover = sharpest portrait, logo = sharpest), and a clean store backdrop (`ART_HERO_OK`: library hero, 4K backdrop, key art…) replaces SteamGridDB's when it has ≥1.5× the pixels (`sgdbTop` now returns `heroA`). The Steam link panel (`bindSteam`, Add screen) asks /art with `want: ["st"]` first (exact addresses + sizes, small copies in the tiles) and only guesses CDN paths without the helper. liteGuess knows the new hosts (Steam store_item_assets 600x900, Xbox `?w=960`, PS `?w=960&h=960`, Nintendo `c_scale,w_960`); sw.js caches Xbox/PS/Nintendo pictures too (`IMG_HOST`). Settings → Nexi helper status names v14 as one behind. Selling plan: PlayStation art comes from the PS Store (drop `ps` from `want` in a sellable build). Tested: helper route in Node with store-shaped fakes (scratchpad art/hn-test.mjs), real stores via the probe (12 games: right game on every store found, Switch-only/PS-only games only where they exist), UI harness art/t.mjs (fake SGDB + /art, W=412/859/360: rows, chips, pick, auto-pick, Steam panel, old helper; 0 problem reports), tour 412/900.
- Helper v16 (no app version): "Apothecary Diaries: The False Imperial Brothers" got only RAWG screenshots. The stores call it "…Brother" (singular) and the matcher needs every word of your title, and Steam's search finds nothing for the full plural title at all; SteamGridDB knows the game but has 0 pictures yet (new game). Now `pxNorm` treats singular = plural (`pxStem`, a hoisted function because `ART_ED`, earlier in the file, uses it at load — a `const` arrow there threw at module load, which `node --check` does NOT catch: import the module in Node before pushing), every art finder also searches the part before the colon (`artQs`, results still scored against the full title), `ART_ED` has legendary/enhanced, and the Steam logo is also looked for in the hash folders of the box art and library hero (this game has none yet); the PS art search retries once (the live helper got a one-off 403 from the PS Store). Probe: price-probe.yml input `art` (titles, `|`) prints each store's raw search for the title and shorter forms, SteamGridDB's hit and picture counts (through the live helper), and the live helper's /art. Result: that game now gets Steam (3840 hero, box art, 4K screenshots) + PlayStation (4K art, logo, screenshots); the 12-game art probe and the price probe (US/AE, eShop) are unchanged.
- v231 (daily fixer): #180/#181 (and #154 again on v224): slow taps on the tour's Next (Mac/iPad Safari, 260–620ms) when the next step opened a game page or Settings. The Next handler ran `mark()` (`saveNow()`: the whole state stringified + written) and `show()` (the step's `go()`) inside the tap, so nothing painted until the next screen was built. Now the tap only fades the card out (`.on` off), and mark + show run after that frame (`requestAnimationFrame` → `setTimeout`); Next is ignored while the card isn't `.on`, so a quick double tap no longer skips a step (it did before too). Measured (tnext.cjs: 14 real Next clicks through the full tour, ~770 KB state, 1080×810, 4x, Event Timing): median 208 → 80ms, worst 312 → 104. #179/#143 (AI design review "inconsistent button radius"): it compared a Settings section row (`summary`) with buttons, and a 26px header link with 240px Played rows → `revTrue` drops radius claims when the elements are different tags or differ >2× in height (rev179.cjs: both real cases true → false; a planted pair of same-size buttons with 4px vs 14px corners still true). #178 (450ms freeze on Home after returning from a store link and closing a game page, v226, no `at`) didn't reproduce on v231 (tret.cjs fakes hidden→visible since headless tabs never hide: 0 frames over 50ms in 6 rounds) → left open. #177 (the friend: no sounds on iPhone 11 / iPad, Safari): not reproducible here (no WebKit locally, no audio); likely the iOS silent switch / Silent mode, which mutes Web Audio in web apps → asked on the issue, left open. Note for the fixer: write "Fixed in vN" (or "fixed in vN") in a closing comment, or bugsync reopens the issue on any later sighting (#154 reopened because its comment said "v219 already fixed it").
- v232: the owner: "each queue card is a different size when open" and "the New game page lacks organisation: each game looks different because of its status". (1) Open queue card (`big` in renderQueue) has fixed slots: tags on one line (`.toprow` nowrap + ellipsis; under 420px card width the release-status tag hides via a container query on `.hero .meta`), title in `.htitle` (2 lines, bottom-aligned, clamp), description always 3 lines (`.desc`, clamp; no description → genres, class `none`), always 4 stats (the 4th falls back to my score / critics / "—"), stats a 2×2 grid (4 across when `.meta` ≥470px). Measured: every open card 613px at 412 (was 514–736), 376 at 900 (346–532), Arcade 647/373. (2) `gdMarkup` is replaced in the last script (the v219/v220 string-insert wrappers that slipped cards in before the description are no longer in the chain): one skeleton for every game: head, tags, `.gd-glance` (4 tiles always: release (another year → year big, day under it), length, hype, score = yours / likely score (`gdGuess`: rvFor, else expected) / critics / "—"), `.gd-main` = Score · Duel · Edit always (not available → `button.off[disabled]`, dashed, with the reason in title), then `.gd-sec` sections in a fixed order: Your progress (`gdProgress`: a 3-step track Out → Playing → Finished (`.gd-trk`, done/now/drop) + per state: date line / "ready when you are" / wiCard + etaCard / capCard or a finished line / dropped line), About (`gdAbout`, desc + Read more; empty → a line), then `.gd-pair` (side by side at 760+) Reviews (`gdReviews`: rvCard, else critics + you cells, else "No reviews yet") and Prices (`gdPrices`: pxCard, else why it isn't watched + "Price alerts ›"), then the same `.gd-more` tiles for every game (Prices and Where was I? always there). Inner cards (.wi/.wis/.rv/.eta/.cap/.px) lose their own frame and title inside a section (CSS `.gd-sec …`); their refreshers (wiRefresh, rvPaint, pxPaint) still swap them in place. Showcase/Plain unchanged. Nexi learned: a ResizeObserver on the open queue card reports `look` "Open queue cards are different sizes" when two different games' cards at the same width/style/layout differ by >12px (planted (fixed slots removed) → reported 714/545/687px; fixed → 0). Sweeps 412/859/360 (every card opened + every game page top/middle/bottom): 0 reports besides the harness's fake helper. Tour 412/900 fine. Perf (4x, dpr 2.6): open game 15–18ms js vs 15, card switch 42–44 vs 38–40, worst frames equal. Harness (scratchpad h/): h.cjs (9 games of every state), q.cjs (card heights, ARC=1 Arcade), gp.cjs (page shots), sweep.cjs, plant.cjs, tour.cjs, perf.cjs.
- v233: the owner (screenshot of v232 unfolded): "looks good but too many buttons and stuff, make it better and smarter". New game page: `.gd-main` = one smart button from `gdNext(g)` by state (upcoming ▶ Watch the trailer, backlog ▶ Start playing / dropped ▶ Pick it up again (`[data-gdplay]`: syncPlayed + state playing), playing 🏁 I finished it (`[data-gdfin]` → fnDone), played ⭐ score + ⚔️ Duel (`.soft`), competitive 📍 Leave a note) + round ✏️ Edit and ⋯ (`.ic`); the 9 tiles moved into a drawer `.gd-x` (pills, `hidden` until ⋯ `[data-gdx]`, which slides what's below with transform and fades the pills in); no more greyed Score/Duel. Sections only show with something to say (order unchanged): About only with a description, Reviews only with rvCard or a critic score (the "You, probably" cell `.rv-c.me` is hidden in sections: it repeats the likely-score tile; played games get "You · liked it more/less"), Prices only when a store has a price or is checking (all n/a/— = hidden; Prices stays in the drawer). Progress lines shortened. Glance: another year's date = year big + "25 Aug" under it (3-letter month: en-GB "Sept" was cut at 412). Wide screens: rows full width, main buttons ≤300px, Reviews/Prices `auto-fit minmax(300px)`. Old WHATS_NEW steps that point at drawer buttons (aiwhy, resetgame, lists, sim, sess): every step's `at` is wrapped (last script, before the v232/v233 pushes) to un-hide the drawer for that step only; `toString` keeps the original for tour reports. Tested: each smart button (backlog → playing → finished + score sheet), drawer, tour steps into the drawer, sweeps 412/859/360 with the drawer open + scrolled: 0 reports; tour 412/900; open game at 4x 14–21ms js vs 15 (noise), same worst frames; 360 row fits (played score label is "⭐ 9.5", aria-label says what it does). Harness: act.cjs, drw.cjs, tat2.cjs, sweep2.cjs, row360.cjs.
- v234: the owner: "it only shows 2–3 genres, what about the rest? tap to show all?". New game page tag row (`gdTags`): state + first 3 genres + `+N` (`[data-gdtags]`) that unfolds the rest in place (`.gd-tmore`, `display: contents`, `hidden` until tapped; what's below slides with transform, chips fade in; "Less" folds back), plus "Steam players say" = `g.stTags` not already in the genres and not in `ST_GENERIC` (≤8, dashed chips `.st`). Genre chips are buttons (`.gtag[data-gtag]`): a small count = how many of your games share it (genres + Steam tags, `gdGenreSet`), tap = Queue filtered to that genre (`gtagFilter`: one `G:<genre>` entry in QFILTERS with icon 🏷️, like the v185 "Like X" filter; qCounts/queueList wrapped; restored after a reload by the last script, which re-renders the queue). About lost its genre meta (the tags show them). Fixes found on the way: the glance tile rules lost to the older `.hub.gpage .hub-data b` (18px, so "13 Sep" was cut at 412) → `.hub.gpage .gd-glance …` (14–22px, 5px side padding); in 4-across mode the open queue card's "10.0/10" was cut at 859 → stat numbers `min(clamp(28px, 3.4vw, 40px), 5.4cqi)` + tighter padding. Nexi learned: a lookDesign wrapper reports `look` "A number in a tile is cut off" for `.gd-glance b, .hub-data b, .hero .stat > b` drawn with an ellipsis (the ellipsis kept them out of the cut-text check); planted (30px tile numbers) → reported, clean → 0, and it found the queue card one. Sweeps 412 (×2)/859/360 with genres unfolded + drawer open + queue cards: 0 (one early run reported the kicker pill as faint once; the pills measure 4:1+ at rest and it didn't recur in 4 runs). Card heights still uniform (613 at 412, 372 at 900). Tour 412/900 fine. Perf equal to v233. Harness: tags.cjs (unfold, filter, reload with `keep: true`), glw.cjs (cut glance text at 360/412), plant2.cjs, inv.cjs, sweep3.cjs.
- v235: the owner (unfolded screenshots): Persona 6's open queue card was shorter than Metaphor's ("fix it to the Metaphor size"). v232 fixed the front's slots, but `.hflip` stacks the front and the flip side (`.pback`) in one grid cell, so the card took the taller side's height, and a played game's back (scores, rating rings, learn/tips, Like this) is taller. Now `.hero .hflip > .pback { height: 0; min-height: 100%; overflow-y: auto }` (the back fills the card and scrolls inside, scrollbar hidden) and at 620px+ `.hero .hflip { min-height: 555px }` (= the owner's Metaphor card, 537px hero). Measured: every open card 613 at 412, 537 at 700/900/1100 (played game with rings was 681 / 642). Nexi's v232 size check catches it (planted: fix removed + played game with rings → reported 372/642 at 900, 613/681 at 412; fixed → 0); the harness missed it because no test game had scores on its back (h.cjs g7 now has `mine`, learn, tips). Detector fix: an outermost bugLayout wrapper waits (450ms, ≤8 tries) while #full/#sheet/#diary/#peek has a running finite animation; an idle pass queued for the previous screen ran in the first frame of a game page's grow (#full at opacity .35) and reported the kicker pill as faint (the v234 one-off). Sweeps 360 (×2)/412/859: 0. Tour 412/900 fine. Perf equal. Harness: qb.cjs (hero + back heights, SHOT=id flips it), qf.cjs, plant3.cjs, op.cjs (#full opacity over time).
- v236: the owner: "added Baldur's Gate 3 from Similar but it added the wrong game; the pictures are too small and I need more description; maybe tap and hold; I only add games by hand, give me smarter ways". (1) Wrong game: `simSame` counted "Baldur's Gate" an .85 match for "Baldur's Gate 3" (one name contains the other) and RAWG's "Baldur's Gate III" only .6. Now numbers decide: `simNum` turns roman numerals ii–xvi into digits (a lone "I" stays a word: I Am Setsuna), `simNums` compares the numbers and different numbers cap the score at .3. Nexi learned: `sgAdd` reports `look` "Added a different game than the one picked" when RAWG's name differs (<.9 or other numbers); planted old matcher → reproduces BG → reported, fixed → BG III, 0. (2) Similar sheet rebuilt in the last script (`openSimilar` replaced; the v187 body and v216 Steam wrapper are out of the chain): library as a strip of covers (`.sg-lib`), then big 16:9 cards (`sgCards(key, list)`, `.sgl`/`.sgc`, lists kept in `SGL[key]`: "ai" Nexi's picks via simFind, "st" Steam's More like this via stMore with `capsule_616x353.jpg` falling back to header.jpg) with ▶ and ＋ (`[data-sgadd]`), "In your library ›" when you have it. Tap a card → preview `#gprev` (z 97, `gpOpen(list, i, key)`): screenshot carousel (scroll-snap + dots) from the helper's /art (`gpPickShots`: Steam, else PlayStation, else Nintendo; Nintendo first when every platform is Nintendo; ≤5; `gpBig` asks Steam's `.1920x1080.jpg` / PS `?w=1280&h=1280` / Xbox `?w=1280&h=720`; RAWG screenshots fill in), name, Nexi's/Steam's why, facts (year, platform logos via platHtml in `.gp-facts.gd-tags`, Metacritic, ~hours, genres), the full RAWG `description_raw` (7-line clamp + Read more), ＋ Add to queue / ▶ Trailer / Not for me, ‹ › to the next suggestion (the next one preloads). Gestures (`sgSwipe`, pointer events, transform only): a card or the preview body dragged → 90px+ = add, ← = not for me (`sgNope` → `S.simNo[sgK(name)]`, never suggested again, also dropped from Discover). Adding (`sgAdd`): RAWG record (`sgResolve`, number-aware) → simAdd; the pick's why becomes the game's one-line desc, Steam id/tags carried over, RAWG description into `about`. Small "Added ✓" stamps inside the sheet (`toastIn`; the app's #toast sits under sheets). (3) Discover (`dcRun`, ~50s after open and 20s after coming back, once per 20h): Nexi pools simFind + stMore picks of your top 3 ranked + playing games (minus your library and Not-for-me), scores by how many favourites point at each, keeps 24 in `S.dcPool`; the Add screen gets a ✨ card (`#dcAdd`, fanned pictures) that opens the pool as a deck in the preview (swipe → add, ← skip); 3+ new picks → Nexi's line (act `discover`). Settings → Extra features → Discover (`#s-xdc`, `S.dcOff`). (4) Share to add: manifest `share_target` (GET to ./index.html with title/text/url; a GET share replaces the query, so the app detects a share by those params); `shName` reads a Steam /app/<id>/<slug>, RAWG, Nintendo product slug, or a title with "| PS5 Games"/"- Official Trailer"/"on Steam" stripped → `shOpen` → the game's preview on the Add tab. Android installed app only (Safari has no share target; Chrome refreshes the installed app's manifest by itself, which can take a day). Tested with a fake RAWG + helper (scratchpad h/fake236.cjs; RAWG returns the 1998 game first): t236.cjs (cards, ＋ adds BG III, preview 4 Steam shots + description, Switch-only game → Nintendo shots, Not for me, Discover pool + deck swipe add), sw236.cjs (card swipes), sh236.cjs (YouTube / Steam link / plain name shares), pv.cjs (no cut labels at 360/412/900; under 400px Add takes its own row), swp236.cjs + sweep3 at 412/859/360: 0 reports, pl236.cjs (plant), tour 412/900, perf equal.
- v237: four owner reports. (1) Grumble lost HP when a game was deleted, and when a game added without saying it was played was then marked played. His HP = hours left in the backlog, so every drop counted as a hit (boss HUD "−Nh", Grumble's "OW", Nexi's "Grumble just lost Nh of lunch"). An outermost `save` wrapper (last script) diffs the backlog (`grumL()`, same rule as the HUD's `bossNow`, which lives inside another script's scope — calling it from outside threw and killed the script at load, caught by the test) against the last save: a game that left because it was deleted, or became played "as a correction" (`fixPlayed`: wasn't Playing, no notes/sessions/hours/plAt, and added ≤7 days ago or finished before it was added), runs that save with `pnSilentHP` and lowers `S.fxBossMax` by the same hours; a mixed save (a real hit too) stays normal. `g.hpFix` → the `pnBubReact` wrapper skips the "DONE" reaction and Nexi says "already played, Grumble keeps his HP". Finishing a game you were playing still hits. Old build reproduces both (grum.cjs: delete → "OW. −70h", correction → "lost 45h of lunch"); new: quiet, max follows, real finish still "No! That was MINE!". (2) Screenshots on the New game page: `gdShots(g)` section (📸, `.gd-s-shots[data-g]`, after About; hidden placeholder until there are some) with a 16:9 strip `.gd-shots`; `shotsFetch(g)` (on page open, plus up to 6 games a day by themselves 70s after opening: playing, On deck, the rest) = helper /art screenshots picked by `gpPickShots` (Steam, else PlayStation, else Nintendo; Nintendo first for Nintendo-only games) + RAWG `games/<rawgId>/screenshots` to fill → `g.shots` (≤5 {u, b big copy, t small copy, s}), `g.shotsSrc`, `g.shotsAt` (30 days), `g.shotsNo`; a preview's screenshots come along when you add from it. Tap = full-screen viewer `#shv` (scroll-snap, counter, ✕/Esc/tap outside). (3) "Switching pictures in the preview also switches the game page": the page swipe (`hubX`/`hd` touch handlers) and the tab swipe listen on `document`, so touches inside #gprev / #diary / #shv / the page's `.gd-shots` and `.gd-tags` moved the page behind. `noHub(el)` stops touch events at those layers (gpBox/dyBox wrapped; #full listener for the strips). Nexi learned: `hubStep`/`hubDragStart` wrapped → `layout` "The game page swiped behind an open sheet" when #gprev/#diary/#shv/#sheet is open (planted noHub off → page moved g5→g6 + reported; fixed → stays, 0). (4) "Story Rich" twice: Steam's "Story Rich" + RAWG's "Story-rich". `stApply` compares genres without case/spaces/hyphens; `gdKey` does the same for the tag row, counts and genre filter; one-time `S.genDedup` cleans every game. Tests (scratchpad h/): grum.cjs (old vs new), t237.cjs (shots section, viewer, real touch swipes via CDP Input.dispatchTouchEvent on the strip/viewer/preview/sheet, genre dedupe), oldsw.cjs (old build moves the page), plsw.cjs (plant), sweeps 412/859/360 incl. pages with screenshots: 0; tour 412/900; perf equal.
- v238: the owner: "only a few games got screenshots, and they take time to load; I want them instant and always ready, not when I open the game". (1) Background sweep `shotsSweep()` (25s after opening in idle, 15s after coming back, when back online): every game, playing → On deck → waiting → the rest, 12 per batch 1.2s apart, next batch 2 min later while games remain; due = no shots (unless `shotsNo` < 7 days), shots older than 60 days, or small copies not yet downloaded. A game you add is fetched 3s after `sgdbFillOne` (wrapped). (2) `shotsFetch` (replaced): stores via /art as before, then when fewer than 4 the RAWG screenshots, finding the RAWG id by title first when the game has none (`shotRawgId`: `search_precise`, simSame ≥ .85, `g.rawgLook` retries after 14 days) — that's why most games had none (only RAWG-linked ones could fall back). (3) Instant: `shotThumb(x)` = a small copy by host (Steam `.600x338.jpg`, PS `?w=640&h=640`, Xbox `?w=640&h=360`, Nintendo `c_scale,w_640`, RAWG `/media/resize/640/`); `shotsWarm(g)` downloads them once with `new Image()` (sw.js keeps them, cache-first), remembered in localStorage `gq:shotw` (not S); the strip draws the small copies (not lazy); the viewer shows them at once and swaps in the big copy (`b`) when it has loaded. sw.js `IMG_MAX` 350 → 1200. sw.js keeps only CORS-readable pictures (v189); hosts without CORS still come back from the HTTP cache. Testing the service worker: page.route/ctx.route don't see the SW's own fetches unless `PW_EXPERIMENTAL_SERVICE_WORKER_NETWORK_EVENTS=1`, and the SW script itself must come from a real server (python -m http.server on localhost; a routed fake host can't register a SW). Proven (scratchpad h/swc2.cjs, REALHOST=1 = fake Steam ss_ URLs with CORS): the sweep cached 24 small copies, opening a game served 4/4 from the SW cache. t238.cjs: one sweep (~12s for 9 games) gave every game with a store or RAWG match its shots, Persona 4 Revival via the RAWG title search. Sweeps 412/859/360: 0; tour 412/900; perf equal; the sweep's worst long task 70ms at 4x.

Last `<style>`/`<script>` in `index.html`, all `rm*`, overlay `#room` (z 96), opened by the 🧊 Room chip added to the Library's Grid/Swipe row (`[data-room]`, a renderLib wrapper; capture listener so the layout chip handler doesn't take it). Plain CSS 3D, no library: `rmBox(x, y, z, w, d, h, o)` makes the 5 faces of a box (no bottom) as `<i class="rf t|s|n|e|w">` in floor coordinates (x east, y south, z up; transform-origin 0 0, backface hidden, 1.5px ink outline = the reference's line-art look); `.rm-w` = `scale() rotateX(pitch) rotateZ(yaw)` is the only thing that changes per frame. Filled from your games (`rmPick`): giant case = your #1 ranked (else On deck) with the cover on its front, title on the spines, backdrop on the back; wall poster = what you're playing; small frame = next release with "in Nd"; shelf (2×3) = ranked/playing/On deck covers; plaque on the base = "<name>'s game room". The west wall is a cutaway: seen from behind it hides (`.wb`). Light comes from the viewer's upper left and stays put while the room turns: each side face has a black `<s class="sh">` whose opacity is a paused Web Animation per direction (`rmShadeWire`, `RM.an`), set by `currentTime` in `rmShade` only when a direction's value changes. Don't drive the shading with CSS vars on the room: a var change restyled all ~380 elements (10ms a frame on desktop, 40ms at 4x). Gestures: drag = spin (+ tilt 28–78°), fling = inertia, pinch / wheel = zoom .6–2.2, double tap = back to the start view, tap a game = close + openFull; arrows/Esc on a keyboard. Opening swings in (1.3s, skipped with calm()). Perf (4x, 412, dpr 2.6, 2°/frame spin): median frame 16.7ms, 1 of 120 over 34ms (same as with no shading); open 65–71ms long frame (196 the first time, images). - v240: the owner: still no screenshots on Metaphor: ReFantazio, Danganronpa, Persona 5 Royal, AI: The Somnium Files "and more" (the live helper's /art returns 18–45 shots for each, checked with price-probe.yml `art`). Client causes: (1) any failed /art call (a Worker error, a timeout, a network blip) was saved as `g.shotsNo` = "this game has none" and blocked the game for a week (v237's marks too, made with a weaker lookup) — the old build, given one failed call per title, marked all 9 test games "none"; (2) a page reached by swiping (`hubStep` builds pages with hubMarkup, not openFull) never asked; (3) the sweep did 12 games every 2 minutes with finished games last, so short visits never reached them. Now (v238 script, edited in place): `artBad(art)` (error, or every store errored) → one retry 4s later with the cached answer dropped (`ART_C` keys for that title), then `SHOT_ERR` and no mark (next pass / 10-minute re-sweep); "none" counts only when v240's lookup said so (`g.shotsNoV = 240`, `shotsNoNow`, 3 days); a gdShots wrapper asks for any game drawn without screenshots (current page and swipe neighbours; `SHOT_TRY` ≤1/min per game; `SHOT_AUTO`); `shotsSweep` does the whole library at once in 3 lanes (12s after opening, 8s after coming back, on online; 9 games 1.5s vs 11s, same long-frame total at 4x). Steam screenshot URLs end in `?t=…`, so `gpBig`/`shotThumb` regexes accept a query. Nexi learned: a game page shown 15s with no screenshots, not marked none, nothing in flight → `look` "A game page never got its screenshots" (`at` = the error or "never asked"; not for an old helper). Planted (auto-ask + sweep off, swipe g1→g2) → reported "never asked"; fixed → 4 shots, 0. Sweeps 412/859/360 with the fake helper: 0. Tour 412/900 fine. Harness (scratchpad h/): t239.cjs (FAILN=n failures per title, real-shaped `?t=` URLs, swipe path, v237 mark), t239s.cjs (PLANT=1), sweep4.cjs, swperf.cjs.
- v241 (helper `HELPER_V = 17`): hours to beat from HowLongToBeat (the owner: "most hours I type by hand, the automatic ones are super wrong"). Two guessing sources are gone: v219's AI (`hbRun` → now just calls `hlRun`) and RAWG's `playtime` (what RAWG users logged, not time to beat; it filled the Add form and Edit's RAWG pull — `fill`/`rawgLookup` wrappers drop it). Helper `/hours` now reads HowLongToBeat (`hltbFind`): GET `/api/search/site/init?t=<ms>` → token (bound to the caller's IP + User-Agent, so both calls send the same UA; reused 5 min; a 401/403 gets a fresh one), then POST `/api/search/site` (header `x-auth-token`, body = the site's own search JSON) → `data[]` with `comp_main/comp_plus/comp_100` in seconds, `comp_all_count`, `game_alias`, `release_world`. Picked by `artSame` over the name + aliases (different numbers = another game; extra words cost), year ±1, players; also searches `artQs` forms. Answer `{src: "hltb", res[k] = {id, n, m, x, c, cnt, sure: 1} | {none, sure: 0[, id, n]} | {err}}` (old apps still read m/sure). Found with `.github/scripts/hltbprobe.mjs` + `hltb-probe.yml` (runs the helper's real code against the real site on every helper push; input browser=yes records the site's own requests in a headless browser): if hours stop coming back, run it and compare. App (last script, `hl*`): `g.hl = {id, n, m, x, c, cnt, at} | {none, at} | {err, at}`; whose number (`hlSrc`): "hltb" (`g.hrsSrc`), "me" (typed in Edit/Add after v241: #e-save/#f-save capture listeners), "ai" (v219's `g.hbV`, unchanged), "old" (before v241: typed or RAWG, can't tell), "none". none/ai/rawg/hltb follow HLTB by themselves (`hlUse`); "old" ones >15% and 2h+ off (`hlDiff`) go in `hlReview()` (Use / Keep / all; Keep stores `g.hlKeep` = that HLTB number), which Nexi offers once per set (`S.hlSaid`, bubble act "hltb"); "me" is never touched. Game page: the Length glance tile is a button (`.gd-hl[data-hltb]`, small "HLTB" mark when that's the source) → `hlSheet(id)`: main / extras / 100% (your play style ringed), players counted, Use HowLongToBeat's / Keep mine, link to howlongtobeat.com/game/<id>, Check again. Edit shows "⏱ HowLongToBeat: m · x · c h" + Use these (`#e-hl`). Settings → Extra features: "Hours to beat from HowLongToBeat" (same `S.hbOff`) + status `hlStatus()` + Review button (`[data-hlrev]`). Runs 20s after opening, 9s after coming back, on online, 2.5s after adding a game; 8 games per call, 1.5s apart; re-check found games after 45 days (7 while it's within 90 days of release or upcoming), not found 14 days, errors 6h; a helper without `src: "hltb"` → nothing applied, retried in an hour. Tests (scratchpad h/): t1.cjs (fill, AI replaced, typed kept, review, Edit use, typed → "me"), t2.cjs (old helper ignored; automatic run), sweep.cjs (0 reports at 412/859/360 with the sheets, review, Edit, Settings), tour.cjs (412/900), perf.cjs (open game 4x equal to v240); helper: hlt.mjs (route in Node with HLTB-shaped fakes: expired token refreshed, Hades ≠ Hades II, Elden Ring ≠ Nightreign, accents).
- v241 fix (no app version): the live helper's first HowLongToBeat search got a 403 even after one fresh token (a Worker's calls can leave from different IPs, and the token is bound to the IP). `hltbSearch` now tries up to 3 fresh tokens, and `hlRun` asks the games that came back with an error once more at the end of the same run (a queue, not index batches). Probe `hltb-probe.yml` also calls the live helper's /hours: 7/8 right before the fix, the 8th the 403. Then still 2/8 refused with 3 fresh tokens, always right after a new token, while later games using that same token passed: the site seems to reject a token used too soon after it's issued (Worker → HLTB is much faster than a browser or GitHub). `hltbTokenAged` waits until a new token is 1.2s old (2.5s on a retry).

Direction: dragging right now turns the room right (`RM.yaw = start.yaw - dx * .42`; fling and arrow keys flipped too). Nexi learned it: 4 invisible corner markers (`.rm-mk`); at a sideways drag's start `rmFront()` takes the floor corner lowest on screen (nearest you), and after 24px `rmDirCheck` reports `look` "The game room turned the opposite way to the swipe" if that corner moved against the finger (planted old sign → reported at 412/859, fixed → 0). New in the room (ideas from interactive dioramas online: day/night lighting, hidden objects, gyro parallax, tappable objects): real day/night (`rmTime()` by the hour: dawn 5–7, day 7–17, dusk 17–19, night) → `#room.rmt-<time>`, a window with that sky (sun / moon + stars), a sunlight patch on the floor by day, everything dimmer at night (`RM.dim`, added on top of the side shading; top faces have overlays too); the lamp (`[data-rmact=lamp]`) switches on/off (`#room.rm-off`, `rmDimTo` eases the dim); lamp beam with dust = a billboard. Billboards (`rmBB`, `.rm-bb`, `RM.bb`) are flat sprites re-faced to the camera every frame (`rotateZ(-yaw) rotateX(-pitch)`, the beam also `scaleY(sin pitch)` to match the lamp's height): Nexi on the couch (tap = hop + `rmLine()`, room-aware lines through nxVoice, shown as a caption at the bottom `.rm-say`), the pile's "N waiting" tag, the season's emoji (`ssnNow().hat`) on the table. The giant case has no front face: its lid (`.rm-lid`, tap) swings 104° on the spine (CSS transition), behind it a tray with a disc of the cover that spins 3 times; tap the disc = open the game. Shelf games slide out before their page opens (`.rm-pop`). Your queue = a pile on the floor (one striped box, a stripe per game, + the top case turned; tap = Queue). 9+ played games = gold cups on a wall shelf. A golden controller behind the case (`[data-rmact=secret]`, confetti, `S.rmFound`). Phone tilt (`rmOri`, deviceorientation): Android by itself, iPhone via the 📱 button (permission needs a tap). `rmTick()` vibrates 4ms every 30° (Android). Perf lessons (4x, 412, dpr 2.6): (1) the shade overlays animated opacity → each one became a compositor layer (484 layers); they animate background-color now (233 layers). (2) Per-piece drop-in animations (9 preserve-3d groups animating transform) cost 6–9 frames of 50ms at 1x → removed; the opening is a swing-in with overshoot on the world transform (`rmTween(..., back)`), only Nexi pops in. (3) Shade steps 1/12. (4) Dust only with the lamp on and after the swing-in. (5) Room pictures use light copies (`liteFor(u, 1)`) and the case's back/lid/disc use the cover, not the 4K backdrop. Result: spinning equal to v239 (median 16.7ms at 4x, A/B 3 runs each); opening ~11 vs ~8 frames over 34ms at 4x, 0–2 at 1x. Sweeps 412/859/360 day and night: 0 (one "faint Library title" at 859 didn't come back in 3 more runs, v239 0/3 too). Harness (scratchpad h/): act.cjs/act2.cjs (every tap, LoAF per action, NIGHT=1), dirplant.cjs, count.cjs (elements + layers), lid.cjs, jump.cjs, ab.sh + v239/ copy (ROOT= env in h.cjs), tour2.cjs. Headless LoAFs of ~200ms with no script/style time are the software rasterizer (v239 shows them too). Detector fix: the faint-text check (lookDesign #4) multiplied a fading-in title's opacity into its contrast and reported the Library title at 1.7:1 now and then (1 in 4 loads at 859; before the room even opened) → it now skips text while `bugMoving(el)` (0 in 8; a planted faint title is still reported). - Problem-report fixes (daily fixer, no app version: detector only, nothing to see): (1) an iPad session filed 24 "A tap took Nms to respond on a game page" issues in one minute (and 12 for Library, 10 for Queue, 7 Insights, 6 Settings the day before) because the slow-tap report's signature included the element touched (`at`: h2, the description, a tag row… — mostly touches that start a scroll). `bugReport` takes `x.sigAt` to replace `at` in the signature; the slow-tap observer passes `sigAt: ""`, so it's one report per screen per day (the issue still names the first element). - WebKit check (no app version): Playwright's WebKit page sometimes dies mid-walk on GitHub's runners ("Target page, context or browser has been closed" in `scrollBy`, after the first device had passed; 983e68c, v231, 2ba6dd7 — each fine on a re-run). webkitcheck.mjs now walks each device in `walk(name, dev)`; a crash is printed ("the WebKit page crashed … try 1 of 2"), the browser is relaunched if it died, and that device is walked once more; a second crash on the same device fails the run. Dry-run with planted crashes (local Chromium): once → logged, re-walked, exit 0; twice → exit 1.

- v245 (daily fixer): #301 (220ms Queue stutter on the Fold, `pnShelf ← FrameRequestCallback`, script 77ms): renderPlayed (rebuilt in the background while you're on another screen, v206) schedules `pnShelf` 30ms later, and its first `getBoundingClientRect` forced a full layout of the freshly redrawn screen you were on. `pnShelf` now returns while `#shelf-row` is inside `.panel:not(.on)` (a DOM check, no layout), reads every item's position before writing styles, and a MutationObserver on each panel places the shelf when its screen opens. t301.cjs (411×902 dpr 2.63, 4x, 40 scored games): pnShelf right after a Queue redraw 130ms median / 191 worst → 0.1 / 1.1; visible on Played 5.8 → 2.8 median; all 40 items placed on opening Played. #305 (160ms "stutter" on a game page, `DOMWindow.onresize`, folded 411px): folding/unfolding lays out every screen again → the long-animation-frame check skips frames that overlap the 0.8s after a resize (`LAG.rz`); t305.cjs: a planted 260ms frame right after a resize is no longer reported (old build reported it), the same frame without a resize or 2s later still is. Triage: #302/#303 (iPad: "Restore from a code" tapped 9 times, one tap 2.1s): probed in real WebKit on GitHub (a throwaway branch `probe/restore-webkit` running a restore-only webkitcheck.mjs through webkit-check.yml; couldn't delete it from here: the proxy refuses ref deletes and git push --delete hangs): the sheet opens at once (37–69ms, code field focused) on iPad/iPad landscape/iPhone 11; on portrait iPad the sheet sits at the bottom with the Restore button still visible above it, and a second tap there lands on the backdrop and closes the sheet — likely what happened if the keyboard hid the sheet. Left open (can't confirm without a real iPad). #304 (340ms tap on the rail's ⚙ into Settings, iPad) = first Settings open, as #154. Tour 412/900; audit at 350ms: 0 of 145 missing.
- v246: (1) #309 (owner: "remove the touch animation, when I touch anything an animation happened, delete that"): no press effect anywhere. A last-script pass strips transform / scale / translate / rotate / filter / opacity from every rule whose selector has `:active` (57 rules across ~25 scripts, incl. the `:hover, :active` pairs; colour changes stay), at start and for `<style>` added to `<head>` later (MutationObserver, childList only). ~8ms for ~4000 rules unthrottled. Test: CSSOM walk finds 0 movement left on `:active` rules; pressed Queue row / button / nav keep their rest transform. (2) Slow-tap detector on Safari: WebKit's event timing starts when the finger lands, so a held button or a touch that started a scroll read as 0.3–5.5s "taps" (#212, #213, #233–#258, #283, #290–#304, iPad; none of those iPads ever reported a scroll stutter, whose rAF checker runs there too). `LAG.wk` (AppleWebKit, not Chrome/Android) counts only processing + drawing (`duration - (processingStart - startTime)`); entries with `interactionId === 0` (a touch that became a scroll) are skipped everywhere; every slow-tap report now carries `name: waited / ran / drew` ms in its stack. Planted 400ms click handler → still reported (Chrome and with `LAG.wk = true`), a 1.5s hold with a quick handler → nothing. Sweep 412/859/360: no new reports (an occasional 150–340ms "rendering" stutter in headless software raster shows up on v245 too: long-frame counts of v245, v246 and v246-without-the-pass are the same range). The owner decided (7 Oct 2026) Play next stays personal (owner + friend): no store release; CLAUDE.md's selling plan is parked.

(1) Release days in your calendar: Settings → Extra features → "Release days in your calendar" (`#s-xcalw`, `calBlock()`): Turn on makes `S.calId` (24 random base64url chars) and sends `calEv()` (not dropped / played, a full YYYY-MM-DD date from 30 days ago to 3 years ahead, `status` tba left out, rumoured = `r: 1`) to helper `POST /cal/put`; then "Add to my calendar" opens Google Calendar's add-by-URL (`calendar.google.com/calendar/r?cid=webcal://…`) on Android or `webcal://` on iPhone, plus Copy link / Stop (`{id, off: 1}` deletes it). Re-sent by itself when the list changes: 6s after opening, 4s after coming back, and at most 5s after a save (`save` wrapper; first version restarted a 5s debounce on every save and never fired, since something saves all the time: v202's lesson again), compared by a hash in localStorage `gq:calsig` (+ `gq:calsign` = count). Helper keeps `cal:<id>` in KV 400 days and serves `GET /cal/<id>.ics` (all-day events, UID `<game id>@playnext`, 9am alarm, `REFRESH-INTERVAL` 6h, lines folded at 75 bytes, CRLF). (2) Game news from Steam (`#s-xnw`, `S.newsOff`): every 6h (localStorage `gq:newsAt`; 25s after open, 12s after coming back) `nwPick()` (not dropped, `stIdOf(g) > 0`, playing first, played last, 24 max) → helper `POST /news` → Steam `ISteamNews/GetNewsForApp/v2` with `feeds=steam_community_announcements` (developer posts only), kinds patch / dlc / demo / launch / trailer / news from Steam's `patchnotes` tag + the title. First look at a game stores `g.nwSeen` (newest post's date) without telling; later posts under 10 days old go in `g.nw` (3 max) and Nexi says so (bubble act "news" → `nwSheet()`, a dy sheet with Read links). (`?nudge=next`, already handled), Queue (`?nudge=queue`); icons `sc-add.png` / `sc-pick.png` / `sc-queue.png` drawn from the v247 icons; the WebAPK picks them up when Chrome next refreshes the manifest.
