- v282: loops keep running while you scroll (the owner: "App freezes alot when I move or scroll and nexi animation and rail stops its bad so fix it and find a better solution cuz I dont want animation to stop while schrolling"). Measured with the new `tools/scenarios/trace-loops.mjs` (859, real CPU, six Queue/Library/game-page swipes, arms: loops running / all paused / one paused / v280's hold): every endless loop left (Nexi's pnBreath/blob/nxmHype/pnZz, the rail's rlvDot + strip) is transform/opacity, so mid-scroll they cost one small style pass a frame (~0.3ms; a main frame ~2.0ms vs ~1.0ms with every loop paused, inside 120Hz's 8.3ms), and pausing any single one changes nothing (the pass costs the same for one loop as for seven). v280's hold cost more than it saved: its first `mvhMove` (document.getAnimations() forces a style pass, the `.mvh` class restyles all of Nexi) and its release (every loop handed back to the GPU at once) were the longest main-thread tasks of a swipe: 22ms on Queue, 43ms on a game page (now 10ms at most), i.e. a hitch right as a scroll starts and stops, and Nexi/the rail stood still in between; on Library it saved nothing. Removed: the `mvh*` style + script and v280's "loops hold still" WHATS_NEW entry (false now); budget important 168 → 166. Also removed: Library cards tilting and shrinking (`scale(.97)`) under the finger (`tilt`/`fxt` in the FX pack, v-old; #309: it measured the card on every touch and sprang back with a transition as each scroll began; 48 animation restyles per Library swipe set). Guard: `kit smooth` flipped: fails if Nexi's or the rail's loops stop mid-swipe, if a loop running mid-swipe animates anything but transform/opacity/translate/scale/rotate, or if what the finger landed on gets an inline transform or animates; v281 fails it 5× (the hold at 859 on Queue/Library/game page + power-up, the card tilt at 412). Not changed: rest mode (60s idle), the hidden-screen hold (OFFHOLD), the rail's own pause while you drag it. Field hitch reports (#72, #207: 110–310ms on the Fold) aren't reproduced here (GPU side); watch whether they come back after v282.
- v283: scroll stutters back after being closed (#134 Month, #136 Insights; #416/#333/#367 switching to Library/Queue). The Chrome DevTools MCP couldn't start on the PC (no Chrome installed, it only looks for chrome.exe), so the same traces ran through Playwright + Edge 155 (the owner's engine; `CHROME=".../msedge.exe"`) with the new `tools/scenarios/trace-stutter.mjs`: 60 games, 4x CPU, real touch swipes on the screen's own scroller, each long task split into script (function:line) / style / layout / paint, whole-phase totals per function and forced style/layout (with the JS stack), plus the webperf-interaction skill's Long-Animation-Frame and Forced-Synchronous-Layout snippets in the page and a getBoundingClientRect counter by caller. Cause #1 by far: Nexi's own "blank while scrolling" check (v216 block, `check`) ran on every scroll frame and called getBoundingClientRect on every skipped `content-visibility:auto` element in the app (Library `.row.slab`, Queue `.pnb-row`, hundreds in hidden panels) before its `.panel:not(.on)` test, and read `innerHeight` (forces style) first: 2000-2700 reads per Insights scroll, 418ms script + 204ms forced style at 4x (Psych 513+160, Month 101+9). Now: hidden screen / screen under a game page or sheet skipped before measuring, `innerHeight` read lazily, at most one check per 100ms: Insights 46ms, Psych 42, Month 5; all script during the swipes 2902→1875 (Insights), 2530→1381 (Psych), 831→554 (Month). Also: the AI design look (`lookReview`, 3s after a screen settles) never checked for touch and could fire mid-scroll (84ms at 4x): it now waits for 3s with no finger (`bugLastIn`); the stuck-tap watcher's MutationObserver asked getClientRects/checkVisibility right after the tap's redraw (22ms forced style on a Queue/Library pill tap, #367's "drew 255ms"): candidates are judged after the frame (rAF + setTimeout) now, removals still count at once. Tab switches themselves showed no long frame here (Edge on the PC, 4x), so (M11) scroll and screen-switch stutter reports now carry `lagSplit()` (v198 block; `LAG.lo` = last 40 long-animation-frame entries): long frames, script / forced style+layout / style+layout / render ms and the longest script's function ← invoker, or "no long frame: drawing (GPU)"; bugsync.mjs adds the latest Timing block to every "Seen N more times" comment of a Slow issue. Proof: `tools/scenarios/stutter-detectors.mjs` (412/859, old copy too): a dead button tapped twice still reported (pill taps aren't), a planted skipped row on screen still reported (hidden-screen ones not), a planted 130ms scroll handler's report names `onscroll` with the split. Remaining per-scroll cost is a dozen small capture scroll listeners (rest timer, draw arm, hint bubble, light pictures), ~30-90ms each per swipe set at 4x: candidates for one shared listener.
- Mods, no version (blast-radius 0.2.0): on the PC, Git Bash `cd "C:/…worktree"; rm old.html` was held as "outside the repo: /C:/…/C:/…/old.html". Cause: the user-scope install was still the Oct 13 copy (0.1.0, from before the Windows fixes in #458–#460). The version was never bumped and the plugin cache is keyed by version, so `claude plugin update` kept the old `join()`, which knew no drive letters and glued the cwd on twice. Fixes: version 0.2.0 (bump a shared mod's version with every change, or installs never get it); `join()` also reads Git Bash's `/c/…` and `/cygdrive/c/…` as `C:/…` when the repo is on a drive; `~` in a held `rm` uses the real home. Test 11 (Windows worktree, `cd "C:/…"` and `cd /c/…`, then a relative rm). Seen on the way: `segments()` splits a heredoc's body on `;`, so prose after a `;` that starts with "rm" reads as a delete (still true in 0.2.0).
- Scroll listeners folded (no version, nothing visible): 10 capture `scroll` listeners on window (light-picture kick, Nexi's shelf, `bugLastIn`, the stuck-tap watcher, blank-while-scrolling, rest timer, design/stacked-layer/picture check arms, the smoothness probe, picture-fit retry) plus the hint bubble's bubbling one were each a separate call on every scroll frame, most also clearing and setting a timer every frame. Now one: `onScrolled(fn)` (core script, after `extend`; fn(e, first, now), `first` = nothing scrolled for 250ms) and `scrollWait(ms)`: a job that waits for scrolling to stop arms once and its check (`drawCheck`, `stackCheck`, `picCheck`, `restGo`, the fit retry, the bubble) waits on by `scrollWait` if the scroll went on. The hint bubble still listens to the page's own scroll only and tracks a redrawn bubble. Trace (Chrome 155, 4x, 3 alternating runs, trace-stutter ONLY=insights,psych): script during the swipes Insights 2166→1518ms, Psych 2634→1940ms; the listeners' 30-140ms each became one at 28-84ms. Proof: `tools/scenarios/scroll-hub.mjs` (412/859: one window scroll listener, a swipe reaches it, the checks wait mid-scroll and run once still, the bubble shrinks/returns at 550ms); stutter-detectors.mjs still passes. Tour ring `follow` (only during a tour) and per-element scrollers keep their own.
- Mods, no version (second-look 0.2.0): same stale-cache problem as blast-radius 0.2.0. - Mods, no version (blast-radius 0.2.1): `segments()` split a heredoc's body like commands, so a `cat >> docs/LOG.md <<'EOF'` whose prose had a semicolon followed by the word rm was held as a delete of every word after it. It now drops heredoc bodies (`<<WORD`, `<<-WORD` with leading tabs, `<<'WORD'`, `<<"WORD"`, several on one line, CRLF, unterminated = to the end; not the `<<<` here-string) and keeps splitting the rest of the starting line and everything after the terminator. Test 12: a body with `; rm x | rm -rf y && git reset --hard` gives no risks, a real `rm` on the starting line or after the terminator is still found.
- Mods, no version (learn 0.2.0, auto-handoff 0.2.1): the learn mod's end-of-turn triggers (asked for something automatic, corrections) read text the person never typed: a system-reminder saying a linked session "runs on its own", a side-session note quoting a child's report about making something automatic, a handoff brief (L22 ×2, M8, three times on 2026-10-14/15). `promptSignals` now strips system-reminder / side-sessions-event / task-notification / agent-message / ci-monitor-event / pasted_content / command blocks, quoted lines and long quoted reports, and returns nothing for a message from another session (origin outside composer/bridge/sdk/unclassified, the app's "(from the session that started this one)" brief, or an auto-handoff note, whose first line is now "Handoff note from an earlier chat"). Two tests (both fail on the old code); L22 promoted, M8 done.
- v284: "More for you" on Home was an empty band on the Fold (the owner's screenshot, unfolded: "it should have 2 stuff"). Cause: v281's reveal by the browser itself (`pnTrig`, `PN_TRIG`, Chrome 145+ `timeline-trigger: view()` + `animation-trigger: play-once`) left cards paused on their first frame (opacity 0). Two ways, both seen in Chrome for Testing 148 (the bundled Chromium 141 has no triggers, so every sweep passed): (1) `view()` follows the nearest scroll box, and `.wrap` is one (`overflow-x: hidden`) that never scrolls (the document does), so Home's right-hand stack (`.sC`) never triggered; (2) with that fixed (`overflow-x: clip`), Insights' top card (`.ix-hero.dzc`) still went from drawn to invisible on a 25px layout shift with no script call, and a wider range (`cover 0% cover 100%`) made every card on screen fade in on each open. So the CSS reveal is removed (its @supports block, `PN_TRIG`; v281's what's-new line now only mentions the anchored tour ring) and Home/Insights use the script reveal (`revealSetup`, `ixWire`) everywhere, as Safari always did; game pages have no reveal again. `.wrap` unchanged. Also learned: in quirks mode class names are case-insensitive, so `.sb` (shelf covers) matches the `sB` stack (it gets `sbUp` + a drop-shadow; v281's `:not(.sb)` skipped it). Nexi learned it: an animation on the open screen that's paused/not started while holding a 40x40+ element at opacity ≤ .05 that's 40%+ on screen, still so 0.7s later untouched, is reported ("A card stayed invisible on screen"; not OFFHOLD/rest-mode holds). It found the Insights card in its first Chrome 148 sweep. Proof: `tools/scenarios/more-for-you.mjs` (stacks + Insights; run it with `CHROME=<Chrome 145+>` too), `sweep --plant` (a paused opacity-0 card: reported on 4 screens), `v281.mjs` updated (its tour-ring check fails on main too, unrelated).
- Mods, no version (heads-up 0.9, crew 0.3, the owner: "heads up doesnt summarize or tell me importent stuff always and crew only shows price which i dont care about much i care about what the agents are doing and how"): heads-up only ever read `turn.complete`'s answer, the turn's LAST message; in the desktop app that is often a one-line closer after the wrap-up recap, so long turns were skipped (<300 chars) or came out as "Nothing needs you", and the prompt told haiku empty lists were "common". Now a `turn.start`/`turn.step` pair collects every main-loop message of the turn plus the `wrap_up` recap and question, the model always writes a one-sentence summary (25 words) and fills spare room with key `points` (grey tiles), a short turn is its own summary (no model call), and a failed/junk model answer falls back to the turn's opening and its last question instead of nothing. Crew: prices and token counts are gone (band, panel cards, /agents-info); each helper keeps its task (first line of its prompt), what it last said (`turn.step` answer), its last 4 steps in plain words (`toolLabel`: "Reading index.html", "Searching for 'x' in y", a Bash call's description) and the first line of its report; the band lists each running helper with its current step, the panel shows Now / Task / Says / earlier steps / Found, cards are Running / Finished / Time. Tests: heads-up 21, crew 11.
- Problem reports, no version (bugsync only, the inbox clean-up during the Oct 15–22 feature pause): about 200 of the ~270 automatic reports ever filed were "Slow", most a single 110–300ms hitch, many from the iPad at 60Hz, plus smoothness checks / self-tests that showed nothing wrong. `.github/scripts/bugsync.mjs` now files a timing report (`HITCH`: "A Nms stutter/freeze on", "A tap took", "Scrolling stutters on", "… stuttered (N hitch", "Opening the app froze for") only after `REPEAT` = 3 sightings (a sighting = one phone, one day: the app sends each problem at most once a day), or at once when it's `BIG_MS` = 400ms+ (the app's freeze line). Until then it isn't acked, so the helper keeps counting (a one-off is forgotten 45 days after its last sighting, `BUG_TTL`). A closed stutter reopens only after `REOPEN` = 2 new sightings. A smoothness check / self-test becomes an issue only with 25%+ of frames dropped somewhere or a 100ms+ gap; otherwise its numbers go to the job's log. Crashes, cut-offs, stuck taps and the structural speed checks (GPU animations, non-passive listeners, big pictures) file as before. Why the filing side and not the app: it sees both phones and every day, works for every installed version (the iPad trails behind) and needs no release. Testing: `BUGS_FILE` / `ISSUES_FILE` / `DRY=1` run it offline; `node tools/bugsync-check.mjs` checks 18 made-up cases (a looser `REPEAT = 1` fails 5 of them). Inbox: 15 of the 21 automatic issues closed (scroll ones "Fixed in v283", old-signature ones retired by v273, iPad one-offs, measurements); kept open because seen on v280+: #60, #62, #116, #207, #333, #334. Not fixed: "1 hitch" vs "2 hitches" give different signatures in the app (#429/#430). Lessons L31 ×2, L36.
- v285 (#177, the friend's report: no sounds at all on an iPhone 11 and an iPad 9th gen, installed to the home screen): the cause is the platform, not the app's sound code (every sound starts from a tap and is woken again on the first tap back, as Safari needs): iPhone/iPad web-app sounds follow the Silent switch (the iPad's bell in Control Center). Settings › Sound and reactions › "Play sounds in Silent mode" (`S.silentPlay`, off by default, in `GROUP_KEYS`) sets `navigator.audioSession.type` (Safari 16.4+/iOS 17) to "playback" while sounds are on, "auto" otherwise; the template hook `silSetHtml()` returns "" without `navigator.audioSession`, so Android/the Fold see nothing new. Why "playback": WebKit's `DOMAudioSession.cpp` maps "ambient" and "transient" to `AmbientSound` and "transient-solo" to `SoloAmbientSound`, both muted by the Silent switch; only "playback" (`MediaPlayback`) and "play-and-record" (mic, earpiece routing) play through it, and "playback" doesn't mix, so music from another app pauses when a sound plays (said in the switch's note; hence off by default). `silApply()` runs at load (sounds need a tap, so it's always before the first one), on every pointerdown/touchend/keydown (capture, a string compare) and on visibilitychange; switching it on closes the core `AC` so the next sound starts a fresh engine under the new type. The what's-new step points at the switch on iPhone/iPad (at the Sounds chips elsewhere). Proof: `tools/scenarios/silent-sound.mjs` (a recording `navigator.audioSession` stub: hidden without it, off by default, On → playback, re-set on a tap / coming back / at load, sounds off → auto, the tour step rings the switch), in Chromium at 412/900 and in WebKit via webkit-check.yml. Can't be detected by Nexi: a muted phone looks the same as a quiet app.
- Wrap clean-up 1, no version (step 3a of the Oct 15–22 feature pause: about 85% of bugs come from our own code, often a later script patching an earlier function): 23 of the 92 wrap layers are `extend()` hooks now (wraps 92 → 69): renderQueue (rows glide, dust + logo fit, the open card's size watcher), pnBubShow (quiet during the tour, dressed, leaves the seat), lookDesign (three of Nexi's look checks), dyShow (flicker mark, lag watch, keyboard fit), closeFull (flying covers, lag watch), setSub (old Taste subs → Insights, logo fit), gdShots (asks for screenshots, the "never got them" check), render (Now playing theme), renderLib (Room chip), renderPlayed (row entrance). They're removed, and `kit.mjs syntax` now fails when a function given to extend() is assigned again later (that loses every hook). Every layer's job is checked in `tools/scenarios/extend-hooks-2.mjs`, which gives the same answers on the old wrapped copy (`--file`).

- Idle work, no version (step 3b of the Oct 15–22 feature pause: stop the work done while nothing on screen needs it; measured with the new `tools/scenarios/idle-work.mjs`, which sorts every running endless loop into on screen / hidden / off screen / covered and counts every setInterval, whole-subtree MutationObserver and frame callback that runs while nobody touches the phone, per screen, in the background and with Motion off): (1) the live countdown rewrote its digits every second on hidden screens (Home's, while you're on Queue), which woke all five whole-page observers and a frame callback every second on every screen: a hidden screen's countdown now waits, and `onNav` ticks it the moment its screen shows (`cdTick`); (2) the picture fitter re-armed its 0.7s/2.5s retry for every picture it couldn't see, and a hidden screen's pictures never can, so it checked every 0.7s all day: only pictures new to a pass get the timed retries now (`FIT.again`), the rest wait for a screen change, a skipped row coming back, a scroll stop or an opened section (new `toggle` hook); (3) a Chrome quirk: a CSS loop that offHold/rest paused and later set going with `play()` stops obeying its CSS, so with Motion off Home's orb and Nexi's blinking eyes kept running (4–5 loops): `offOrphans()` cancels replayed loops (`OFFPLAYED`) whose CSS no longer names them, at once when Motion is switched and in the 2s look-around; (4) that 2s look-around skips when the page's animations and their states are the same as last time (a full look still runs every 10s); (5) Grumble's 0.8s side check only runs while he's switched on (off by default), and his smoke's `gMorph` squashes (`scale`) instead of animating `border-radius` (main thread every frame, speed rule); (6) leftovers of removed features: the old Home `.ring`/`.trio` loops, Nexi's unused `.ring` ping (`bubPing`), the whistle mood (gone since v274) and the Grumble HP bar's `hpflow` (switched off by a later `!important`). - v286 (the owner picked bullets 1, 3 and 4 of the code-check issue #469: "Yes: do all three"): (1) Grumble's "SHADOW OF THE BACKLOG · HP" bar (the `onSave` after-hook by `bossNow`) shows only while Grumble is switched on (`S.grum`); the CLEAR! banner for a finished game is untouched; (2) Nexi's sweat drop (`#pnbub.stress .sweat`, pnTear) no longer loops all day once more than 15 games wait: `pnBubReact` sets `stress` for 5.4s (three drops) when a game is added past 15 (`pnSweatT`), and clears it at 15 or fewer; (3) the Out now tag shine (`.tag.st-fresh::after`, freshSweep) sweeps 3 times instead of forever, and a hidden screen's tags get `animation-name: none`, so showing the screen sweeps them again (pure CSS). - Detector-only fix after v286 (no version; #333, "Switching to Queue stuttered", v283 sighting: "longest script 109ms: (anonymous) ← TimerHandler:setTimeout"): the v268 slow-picture check `picWait` ran right after every screen switch (rAF + setTimeout 0) and called getBoundingClientRect on every loading <img> and url() element, forcing the new screen's layout inside the check: new `tools/scenarios/trace-timers.mjs` (home → games at 4x, timer callbacks over 8ms with their forced style/layout) showed it as 51-63ms with 36-51ms forced layout at 859. Moving it to requestIdleCallback didn't help (it hit its timeout with layout still dirty: 40-49ms, 34-39ms forced). Now it collects candidates without reading layout and lets an IntersectionObserver say which are on screen (from the browser's own layout pass): the callback is gone from the over-8ms list. Proof the check still works: `tools/scenarios/picwait-slow.mjs` (a picture answering after 3.5s, also its wsrv.nl sized copy, which the kit otherwise blocks): reported at 412 and 859, as before. `kit.mjs all` clear.
- v287 (#60 Home, #62 game page, #207 Played, #116 blank on Queue; the stutters that kept coming back): the phone's own timing of a Played stutter said "script 0ms, style+layout 1ms, render 5ms" in a 128ms frame, so the time went to drawing. A GPU layer census (CDP LayerTree + compositingReasons, `tools/scenarios/layers.mjs`) found screens drawn as 7-15 screens' worth of layers: a piece with its own layer under later content (side scrollers, loops, finished fill-both entry animations, will-change, and main's leftover `#pnorbs`, v68's two drifting glows at 12-16% under every screen) cuts the content above it into "Overlap" layers. Fixed: `#pnorbs` removed (the owner's v273 "no moving lights" rule; bg-gradient.mjs now fails on anything under the page); `#shelf-row`/`.pbd-row` z-index 1, `.hero`/`.nxh-face`/`.dxq .box` z-index 2; `.hero, .pb, .sb` fill backwards (quirks mode: `.sb` also matches `.sB`); shelf covers will-change only while swiped (`#shelf-row.moving`). At 859: Played 14.5→9.1, Queue 7.2→4.3, Home 7.8→5.1, game page 6.8→5.8 screens. Tried and dropped: hiding the screen under a fully open game page (`html.fullup`, visibility or content-visibility): every close then had an 80-130ms frame (`tools/scenarios/trace-close-game.mjs`, 17-33ms before). New: `node tools/kit.mjs layers` (in `all`, budget `tools/layers-budget.txt`, fails all 10 screens on main's build); Nexi's layer-pile check (`layerMakers()`, last script: makers in view that something is drawn over, fixed overlays with z-index > 0 skipped; reports at 25%+ of the screen; plant `tools/plants/layer-pile.js` reported on every screen, clean sweep 0); a stutter with a free main thread adds `drawLoad()` (pictures MP in view + layer makers) to its lagSplit line (extend map; `stutter-detectors.mjs` case 4); the probe's new arm "nolayer" removes Played's animations (paused ones still hold layers: Played 67→10 layers with it). look-same.mjs is now stable old vs old (hides Nexi's bubble/card/sparks, toasts, nxEvo off, waits for pictures, fixed clock): main vs v287 differs only by the removed glows and a sub-pixel shift in the scrolled Queue's top card. Review fix before release: the layer-pile check measured the screen under an open game page (#full covers it, so all of it looked drawn over: a false 38% on scrolled Played); it now measures only the top screen (#full when open, nothing while a sheet is open), proved by `tools/scenarios/layer-pile-game.mjs` (red before, green after).
- Detector-only fix after v287 (no version; second-look note on the merge): the layer-pile check skipped an open sheet only where `checkVisibility` exists, so on Safari before 17.4 an open Settings/Add sheet could make everything under it look "drawn over"; it now skips on `#sheet.on, #diary.on` like the app's other checks.
- v288 (the owner: "add more personality to Nexi … like the Spy x Family ending where they try to drink tea but never succeed, something always breaks the moment"): Nexi's tea break (`teaTry`, last script). After 25s with no touch, scroll or screen change (and 30+ min since the last try, Nexi on screen: the floating bubble, else Home's face; motion on, no tour, not 1–6am), he brings out a drink (tea, coffee, juice, the Elixir he's saving, soda), closes his eyes, lifts it… and something happens: your touch or scroll (the real interruption, it reacts at once), a sneeze, a bug lands in it, too hot, empty (Grumble, if on), it slips (no hands), a hiccup, he remembers an unscored game or a release ≤14 days out (tap the bubble to go there), or real news (any other `pnBubSay`, via an `extend` before-hook). Never the same kind twice in a row; every 5th try adds "(Try #N. Still zero sips.)". Tries live in localStorage `gq:tea` (bookkeeping, not S). Cup and extras are Web Animations of transform/opacity with no fill (pose kept in the style), removed by `teaStop` on every path (screen change, Settings off). Folded / iPhone: Home's face speech box says the line for 4.8s. Settings › Extra features › Tea breaks (`S.teaOff`). `calm-nexi.mjs` turns it off (it's the one idle bit asked for); `tools/scenarios/tea-break.mjs` checks it.
- No-version fix after v288 (second-look notes on the merge): Home's speech box now gets its own words back after a tea line with an apostrophe (it compared against escaped text, so "didn't"/"It's" lines stuck until the next redraw); the hiccup can't throw on a bubble without `.core` (that would have left the cup mid-lift and tea breaks stuck until a reload); `teaTick`'s 6s timer stops while Tea breaks is off and restarts from the switch.
- v289 (the owner: "see if any lags or bugs or glitches or stutters need fixing … I want it perfect"; #334 "Opening the app froze for 300ms", open since v249, came back after two closes): traced the app start for the first time (`tools/scenarios/trace-start.mjs`: cold reload, 60 games, 4x CPU, every long task split into script/style/layout/paint with the functions and forced layouts behind it; `NAMES=1 ALLCAT=1` lists the raw trace events inside the big tasks, which is how the canvas one was found: it had no JS in it). Three causes: (1) `pnLd` (the loading-shimmer stop, v143/v263) called `i.getAnimations()` per picture right after writing `data-ld`, so every picture forced its own style pass; `pnSweep` (120ms after any DOM addition) did that for every loaded cover at once: 260-420ms tasks at 4x, 4-5 times in the first 6s. Now `pnLd` only marks the picture and one `pnShimOff` per frame cancels every pnShim whose picture has `data-ld` from `document.getAnimations()` (one style pass, shared with the frame's own). (2) `fitDraw` (v267 picture fit, your own pictures: repo-root files and uploads) packed its copies with the page's `canvas.toBlob`; the canvas snapshot behind it (`CanvasResourceProviderBitmap::Snapshot`) held the main thread ~410ms at 4x for the 1536px base copy, on every start (the copies aren't kept between opens). It now runs in a blob Worker (`fitWorker`: createImageBitmap with resize + OffscreenCanvas.convertToBlob webp, PNG where webp can't be made), falling back to the old page path when OffscreenCanvas/Worker is missing, the worker errors or takes 15s+. Same copies made (fit-worker.mjs: 9 copies, same sizes, old vs new). (3) Two screen-size reads at start that forced a style+layout pass after the first redraw: the sparks canvas (`#fx`, FX pack) is now sized when sparks first fly and emptied after (frees a ~12MB full-screen canvas the rest of the time); Grumble (hidden by default) is placed only when switched on. Also `layerMakers` checks a piece's size before walking up its parents' styles. Result (trace-start, 2 runs each, 859 and 412): main-thread script 7.4-8.9s → 5.0-6.6s, style 1.9-2.2s → 1.2-1.5s, tasks ≥50ms 33-37 → 23-31 in the first 8.5s; the 260-420ms picture tasks and the 410ms canvas task are gone. Left: the boot task itself (~650ms at 4x: parsing + running 26k lines and the first layout) and the idle panel warm-up (`step`, ~180ms per big panel at 4x, by design).
- No-version fix after v289 (second-look note on the merge): a picture-copy job that takes 15s+ now stops the worker (`terminate`, `FIT.wk = false`, other waiting jobs go to the page path) before the page path unpacks the same picture, so two full-size unpacks never run at once (that ran Safari out of memory on the iPad check, v267).
- No-version fix (second-look note on #532): a worker that timed out is replaced by a fresh one for the next copy and turned off only after a second timeout, so one slow picture no longer sends every later copy back to the page path. Its other note (several page-path unpacks at once) does not apply: every copy runs through `fitQueue`, one at a time.

On today's look it reports what the owner's screenshot shows: logos spill up to 23px onto the next row, run off the screen edge (`--rax` -30px), a near-black logo at 1.3:1, a 5.5x size spread, and at 412px names squeezed to 0px with "Playing now" on two lines. Four fix drafts (logo in its own upright slot with optical sizing + dark logos turned light + two-line folded rows; logo as the name; quiet watermark; no logo on rows) shown to the owner as a demo page; nothing in index.html changed yet. Noise the checker had to learn to ignore: the app's smooth scroll still gliding between screenshots (scroll with behavior "instant", wait until still), Nexi's sparks and bubble (hide every fixed layer before each picture, count only pixels inside the logo's own box) and the logo fitting still measuring (wait for lfQ to empty).
- No version (the owner: "the skill about the design and stuff", picked "house style + light check"): `.claude/skills/play-next-design/SKILL.md`, a plain skill (not a mod, so it loads in cloud chats too) with every look rule in one place: before you design, a reference → a 5-point brief (layout, style, our colours, what we leave out, both widths), colour (one still background, `--u-pan/--u-card/--u-card2`, `--u-line`, `var(--volt)`), shape and type (html.ui2, `.btn`/`.chip`/`details.grp`, `--u-r`/`--u-rs`, Poppins 500/600/700, upright sentence case), touch and motion (#309, v280, transform/opacity, v282 loops), where the code goes, and the check. `tools/design-check.mjs` reads the branch's added index.html lines with release-guard's `scanLines`/`scanBlock` (imported from `hooks/rules.ts`, so the two never disagree) plus design notes (hex surfaces, other fonts, a class name that already exists, skipping the shared `.btn`/`.chip`/… pieces) and runs `kit.mjs syntax`. Proved on a planted press effect, height transition, italic, Arial, hex surface and a reused `.dash` (3 must + kit failure, exit 1) and on a clean branch (exit 0). A shallow clone can lack the merge-base: it then compares with origin/main itself and says so.
- v290 (the owner picked look A of four demos, "make A"; their screenshot: logos hanging over the next row, cut by the screen edge, a black logo invisible, sizes all over): Queue row logos sit upright in their own slot inside the row (`.rowart`: 8px from the row's top/bottom, 14px from its right, `--rslot` = 36% of the row or 1.6x Settings' "how big", 28% folded, 26% under 380px, `overflow: hidden`). The logo fitting (`lfRule`, v221, a switch off by default since v223) is always on: optical sizing (every logo the same area of real ink, a long one-liner at least 30% of the slot tall, nothing past 86% of its height or its width), right-aligned, cropped to its ink +2% (object-view-box; Safari gets the whole picture moved into place, the slot clips the rest). `lfMeasure` also reads how bright a logo's solid pixels are (6th number; entries from before are measured again): under 50 of 255 it's drawn as a light silhouette. Folded rows (max-width 500px): the inner line is `.rowin` (was an inline flex style), name on line 1 (two lines at most instead of cut), `.rowmeta` (date, hours, sort value, tag) under it, wrapping rather than running under the logo. Gone from Settings: tilt, past the right edge, above and below, "Fit each logo automatically" (keys deleted from S). Nexi learned it (`lfLook`, last script, with the other look checks and again 350ms after a scroll on the Queue comes to rest, because the logo rows usually start below the first screen): a logo spilling out of its row, off the screen, cut by its slot, on the words, still near-black, 5x the size of another; a name squeezed under 60px, a squashed cover, a date or tag broken over two lines. The squashed-picture check now measures a cropped logo against its cropped shape. Proved with `tools/plants/logo-spill.js` (v289's look put back: reported at 412/859/360) and a clean sweep; the sweep waits 1.1s after scrolling down (was 0.7s, the smooth scroll alone takes ~0.5s). Seed games 1-8 now have the made-up logos (`tools/standin-logos.mjs`, the long one-liner made 8:1, real wordmarks stop near there), so every sweep checks them. `tools/scenarios/logo-look.mjs`: LOOK=now must be clean, LOOK=old (v289's CSS) must be flagged. Poppins 700 only; blue / deep blue / pink + black and white. `pnReel` (between REEL-START and REEL-END, in a small script by #pnboot) draws any time t on a canvas from t alone; the boot script runs its clock (rAF, DPR max 2, ~1.5ms a frame at 4x slower CPU). It replaces v252's card intro on #pnboot (pcdHTML stays for the release-day and calling cards). A tap skips it; the what's-new tour waits for it (extend nxTour); off with Motion off or Settings › This build › Opening reel (`S.reelOff`). Under automation (navigator.webdriver) it stays away so the kit can tap the screens; `localStorage gq:reel = "1"` forces it: `node tools/kit.mjs run tools/scenarios/opening-reel.mjs --widths 412,859`. A tap anywhere unlocks sound and starts the reel with its soundtrack through the app's own `audio()` context (so Sound off = silent reel); the soundtrack is `pnReel.audio()` (moved from tools/reel/audio.js into the REEL block, so the video and the app share it), rendered in an OfflineAudioContext 300ms after load (~0.85s at 4x slower CPU, off the main thread mostly) while the screen waits; the picture starts 60ms + the output latency after the sound is scheduled, so they line up. A tap while it plays or Skip ends it (sound fades over .25s).

- v294 (the owner's screenshot: a whole Queue of rows tinted the same blue though no cover was blue: "make it smarter and add more colours"): a game's colour was only ever read from a picture you uploaded yourself (`colourFrom`, one brightest pixel, so one blue speck won); covers from RAWG/SteamGridDB kept the default `PALETTE[0]`. Now (1) `colourFrom` lets every vivid pixel vote for its hue (36 bins, weight saturation² × brightness × nearness to the middle), pools neighbouring hues, averages the winner and snaps it to the nearest swatch (`palNear`: OKLab, mostly hue, lifted 25% for vividness); grey/dark art → the steel swatch. (2) `PALETTE` 16 → 32 colours (new ones appended, so `PALETTE[0]`/`[1]` keep their meaning), shown round the colour wheel (`palRow`). (3) `colJob` (last script) colours every game still on the default blue, or whose colour came from its art (`g.colourOf` = `colKey` of the picture), once per picture: remote covers as a 64px copy via wsrv.nl (CORS-clean, nothing big unpacked), own uploads via a blob; one at a time, waits for the scroll to rest, one save + the colour set in place on the rows already drawn (a full `render()` there left extra GPU layers on Queue/Played, `kit layers` caught it); a picture that won't share is tried 3 times (`gq:colfail`, own key). Runs 5s after start and 2.5s after a save. A hand pick (`g.colourHand`, from any swatch or the custom picker) is never touched; older non-default colours stay as they are; Edit's colour row has ✨ first (pressed while the colour comes from the art) to hand it back. Scenario `tools/scenarios/art-colours.mjs` (orange art with a blue speck → orange, green, grey → steel, red, pink; hand/older kept; Edit shows ✨ + 32). Not a Nexi detector case: which colour suits a cover is taste.

The tap takes what's ready (`PNREEL.use`) and loading stops, so nothing pops in and nothing loads or collects garbage under the reel. Drawing: `tex()` in the REEL block maps a picture onto a card's projected quad in two affine halves (first clipped to the quad, second on top clipped to its triangle, so the seam is picture over picture), then a black overlay shades it like the card. `pnReel.draw(ctx, t, W, H, n, shutter, A)`: A is optional, so tools/reel/render.mjs is unchanged. Worst shelf frame with 9 covers at 4x slower CPU: 4.2ms at 412, 1.8ms at 859 (budget 16). opening-reel.mjs checks covers load while it waits, the centre one is the game being played, the frame budget and an untainted canvas; `tools/scenarios/reel-frames.mjs` (OUT, TIMES) saves frames to look at.

Settings and every Customise are now one scroll of open groups: `srlBuild` (onSettings "after") opens every `details.grp`, gives each `.setgroup` an id `sg-<name>` and moves them into `.srlwrap > .srlbody` beside `.srlnav`, a `role="navigation"` div labelled "On this page" (not a `<nav>`: 20+ bare `nav` rules style the tab bar, fixed in the rail, `display: none !important` under `html.pnrail`). One IntersectionObserver on `#sheet`: rootMargin cuts off the sticky `.ehead` (its CSS `top` + height, measured, so the zone follows it) and keeps the next 40% of the sheet; the first group in it is lit (`aria-current="location"`, exactly one), a second observer on `.srlend` lights the last group at the very bottom. `.srlbar` (transform only) and the lit tile follow it; `#srltag` (fixed, on body, because the rail scrolls itself on short screens and would clip it) shows the name 1.5s. A tap scrolls the sheet (smooth, instant under `html.still`), locks the observer until it lands and corrects the landing up to 3 times. Groups off screen aren't drawn where the browser has scroll anchoring (`@supports (overflow-anchor: auto)`: Chrome; Safari has none, so a jump landed off there and it draws them all, found by webkit-check) (`content-visibility: auto`, 560px guess, each group's real height kept per sheet width in `SRL_H` for the next open): all groups open had doubled opening Settings (562 → 1134ms at 4x CPU, `kit.mjs perf`); with it, the same as main before it (748/710 vs 741/762ms, `tools/scenarios/trace-settings.mjs`). Kept working: the v178 one-at-a-time toggle returns early under `.srl` (Edit still drills in), a tap on a group's title doesn't close it, search hides the rail, `setJump` for a whole group (Most used › Backup…) scrolls there (extend), Live preview in Settings docks once you change something in a look group (`#sheet-in[data-srl-lvp]`, read by lvpSync) and stays docked until Settings is drawn again, so it never docks and undocks mid-scroll. No buttons on the front: the cover (`data-hub`) opens the game page, the rest of the front carries `data-flip`, so a tap anywhere turns it; the flip handlers now ignore taps on any button or link on either side. Entrance (only on `.heroin`, now 1.5s): picture settles, slab sweeps in, cover drops, stamp lands, the words rise; transform/opacity only. Every open card is one height (324px folded, 555px from 620px). Ring captions on the back no longer break inside a word ("ST OR Y": nowrap, no uppercase). The pinned logo (`artBlock`) and the corner platforms (`S.platPos`) no longer draw on the open card. Test: `node tools/kit.mjs run tools/scenarios/queue-card.mjs --widths 412,859`.

A Queue row logo ran past its spot at 412/360 (Nexi's own `cut` check caught it once the shorter card brought that row on screen; main had it too): `lfApply` read the slot's size once while the rows were still settling (115px for a 106px slot) and never again; it now re-reads it in a frame on every call and on every tab switch and rebuilds the sizes only when it changed.

Taps: the capture click handler hands `.hflip.qkf` to `qkTap` (one tap after 320ms = `qkShut`, collapse into the row; two = `qkTurn`, WAAPI on both plates, no fill, visibility of the hidden side delayed .38s). Sounds `sfx("qkopen"|"qkflip"|"qkshut")` (tone() takes an attack now). Entrance runs ~1.9s, so heroin stays 2s. `tools/scenarios/queue-card.mjs` rewritten for it.

Your score fills a controller in `g.colour` and shows on its little screen (the old version put the number over the D-pad; the owner: "the numbers … design is kinda off"); each of the 8 areas (`RATINGS`) fills one round face button with its number inside; liquid = a wave path in an SVG clipPath moved by `translateY` (`--y`, fill-up `qbfill` from `--bt`), bubbles once. Under it the four numbers (unplayed: countdown, hours, hype, we'd expect/critics; played: my score, it deserved, played ×, critics), platform logos + evenings, Learn / Play it, Like this. Top row: box art, logo, drawn move up/down (manual sort only), Edit and ⋯ (Diary, Competitive with drawn icons; `.hmenu` opens downward here). The rings, uppercase kicker, grey stat tiles and text platform chips are gone from the card. Sound `sfx("qkback")` after `qkflip` (click, bubbles, start jingle). Scenario `queue-card.mjs` checks the back (score on the screen, 8 buttons with their numbers inside, nothing past the sides, no emoji, own scores vs Nexi's guess) and taps the back on its sub line: at 360 the top middle is near the move arrows (Chrome's touch adjustment snapped the tap onto ↑) and the lower half is under the tab bar. The seed's played scores used `characters`/`pacing`, not the app's `chars`/`pace`: fixed.

Nexi learned (lookDesign after-hook, last script): a short label saying undefined/NaN/[object Object]; a button running out of its box onto a sibling; a ≤12-char button word cut with an ellipsis (game titles let off). - v304 (the owner: "the more I use the app the more it lags … keeps getting worse"): a leak. New `tools/scenarios/soak.mjs` loops the app's screens, six game pages and Settings and prints DOM nodes / listeners / heap / animations / screen-switch time per round: nodes grew 2,150 and listeners 155 every round (5.4k → 32k nodes in 12 rounds); `soak-which.mjs` pinned it on opening Settings (tabs and game pages stayed flat), `soak-detached.mjs` showed live nodes flat but the engine's total climbing (detached nodes kept alive), and `tools/heap-retain.mjs` (heap snapshot from `soak-snapshot.mjs`, shortest retainer chain) named the holder: `SK`, the Set in "Nexi learned (2): blank while scrolling" (`contentvisibilityautostatechange`) collects every skipped `content-visibility: auto` row; Settings redraws its 26 groups on every open, a row removed while skipped never fires another event, and the Set was only cleaned when a later scroll happened to run `check()`. Each dead Settings page (~2,100 nodes + their listeners and the Text children) stayed alive. Fixed: `prune()` drops rows that left the page on `onSettings("after")`, `onNav("after")` and when the Set passes 60. After: 0 detached nodes after four full rounds, 10 rounds flat at 8,865 nodes / 775 listeners. Lesson: a Set/Map/array of elements kept for a detector must be pruned when the page it points into is redrawn, not only when the detector next runs.
- v305 (stutters: #333/#347 switching to Queue, #72/#116 Queue scroll, #62 game page, #207 Played, #60 Home, #136 Insights, #334 start; the phone's timing said script ~0, so the time was drawing): measured by 9 helpers (tools/scenarios/measure-*.mjs, switch-layers.mjs). (1) Pictures: the picture fit (v267) left any spot over 1536 device px alone, so unfolded the Queue's open card (front and back), Played's rows and the game page hero unpacked originals (the seed's 12 MP art in a 2.9 MP spot) → steps 1280/1792/2048/2560; a screen that had just shown was sized one frame late (onNav → rAF → fitRetry → another rAF), so its first frame unpacked every original → `fitNow()` sizes it in the first frame; `warmGame` unpacked `g.key` (a 12 MP key art the dash page only shows with S.gdFig) for the game and both neighbours → it warms what the page shows at the size its spot last got (`FIT.role`, `fitWarm`), and a swiped-in page is sized before it's decoded (`fitLike`). Queue 859: on-screen pictures mid-scroll 25.3 → 6.6 MP decoded, at rest 14.6 → 5.0; game page with key art 12.2 → 2.6 MP (859), 2.5 → 1.8 (412). (2) Layers: every scroll-linked (view()) animation is a GPU layer whether it moves or not (Chromium has to move it mid-swipe; fill none doesn't help): Played had 54 (4 per row + 24 locked badges) → `.nr` only near where each plays (rows: just above to half a screen below; badges: in the shelf and half a shelf right): Played 859 113 → 92 layers. The rest of Played (to ~4.3 screens) needs the per-row effects gone: #469 item 6, the owner's call. Panel entry animations (cut/smR/smL/qfswap/qfcv/pbd-row) fill backwards, not both (a finished identity transform holds no layer in Chromium, so "held #p-queue 84%" in reports was a false lead, but nothing needs the end state). Not changed: the Queue switch frame's one full-list layer is the panel's own slide (the "slash" tab style); the fixed background glow (`html.paper::before`) makes the page an Overlap copy (a look change to move it: owner's call); the start (#334: lfApply's first layout read, the idle tab warm-up, dzPlay's second Queue draw) is code structure, left for later. (3) Nexi learned: url() backgrounds count in picLook/picCheck (the 12 MP card never showed: <img> only) and in the stutter report's drawing line (`drawLoad`: only the screen on top now, plus MP shown); a screen with over 40 scroll-linked animations → kind "layout" (`scrollLinked`). Proof: tools/scenarios/draw-detectors.mjs, `kit.mjs sweep --plant tools/plants/scroll-pile.js --expect`, clean sweep. (4) The design eye (tools/scenarios/design-eye.mjs) now skips Nexi's timed first power-up: at 15s it flew dots across whatever screen the walk had reached, which read as a Library card picture spilling onto its words (4 of 5 runs here, 0 of 4 on main, only because the walk timing differed).
- v306 (the owner, Fold screenshots of open Queue cards: "the numbers or version looks bad and into each other … double tapping the card has a slow animation and shift and too slow … old sounds that was for the rating"): platform versions on the open card (`.qkpl`, back `.qbpf`) hung outside their 18px logo box (`position: absolute; left: 100%`), so "4·Vita" and "One·360" ran over the next logo; now each `.pfm` is a flex box with the version in flow, raised by a negative margin, and `.qkpl .pfms` wraps inside the strap (folded logos 16px / gap 8px so five systems still fit one line at 412; unfolded 22px). `qkTurn` is 420ms straight 0→180° (was 760ms with a swing to 187° and back, the "shift"); the back's fills, `qkflip`/`qkback` sounds and the shine follow the shorter turn. Removed `revealSound` (v-old hype pips + rating rings ticks that still played on every card open next to `qkopen`), its timing helpers (PD/PTAIL/RD/RDUR/pace), `lastHero`, and Settings › Opening a card (`S.ticks/tickVol/tickPitch/pace`, its GICON and tools/icons.mjs drawing); countUp keeps its old 850/1100ms. New `tools/scenarios/card-turn.mjs`: no platform part overlaps another or runs past the strap's text column, the turn is ≤450ms and the card's box and the scroll don't move while it turns, opening plays only the card's own tones, no revealSound (fails 10× on v304; numbered v306 because main's stutter release took v305 meanwhile).
- v307 (the owner: "Again I spent time on the game queue and it lagged a lot and the games stopped opening and everything went bad"): not a leak this time (new `tools/scenarios/soak-queue.mjs`: real touch, open/turn/turn back/game page/close for N rounds: nodes, listeners, heap, timers all flat). With 60 games: (1) tapping a row below the open card closed that card first, so everything under it moved up by the card's extra height (~440px unfolded) and the card you opened landed above the screen (−50 to −88px top at 859); the next tap hit whatever row was there now. `qKeep` (row onclick) + the renderQueue glide hook: in the next frame the tapped row is scrolled back under the finger, and an open card that runs under the tab bar slides up (never past its own top). (2) The glide hook measured every row before and right after each open/close (a forced layout of the whole list inside the tap, ~100ms of a ~420ms tap at 4x) although opening never changes the order: it now returns when the order is the same (tap handler ~100 → ~50ms, tap→screen median 376 → 328ms at 4x). (3) `qkTap` timed the double tap from when the page handled each click, so on a busy phone the second tap of a double tap (handled 150–330ms late) read as a single tap and closed the card: it uses the click's `timeStamp` now. Nexi learned (last script): a Queue card you opened that sits above the screen or under the tab bar 0.9s later is reported ("ended up off the screen"). `tools/scenarios/queue-open-place.mjs` (412/859): three tap heights land on screen, no false report, a planted post-open scroll is reported; fails 3× on v306. Note for soak-queue: at CPU=4 the test's own two taps can land 300ms+ apart (CDP), so those turns aren't checked.
- After v307 (no version; second-look's notes on #570): Nexi's "ended up off the screen" check skips a card the finger scrolled on from before the check (touchmove/wheel between the tap and 0.9s), which would have filed false reports; the row onclick clears `qKeep` in a finally, so a Queue redraw that stops early can't leave a stale place for the next one. queue-open-place.mjs gained the scroll-on case (reported on v307, clean now).
- After v307 (no version; open idea M5: "a sweep can't tell whether a detector still works"): every plant in `tools/plants/*.js` now runs on each PR through `tools/plants/run.mjs` (app-check step after "Check the app", ~100s for 7 plants). Each plant's header says what it needs (`sweep: --widths 412 --screens queue`) and which reports it must cause (`expect:` regexes on "[kind] screen: msg @ at", all must match, so an unrelated report can't pass for it). kit.mjs sweep gained `--screens` (only those tabs / game / settings / edit / chat) and `--match`. New plants: `thrown-error.js` (a crashing onNav hook and an uncaught promise: [error] + [promise]), `button-cut.js` (Queue tabs past the right edge, row names cut without "…"), `pic-squash.js` (squashed Library covers). Proved the other way too: a copy with the squash and promise checks off fails both plants. Not planted yet: the blurry-picture check (the seed's pictures never take the light-copy path, and its 3s re-check outlasts a sweep's stay on a screen, L47) and a broken tour step (the tour run has no --expect).
- v308 (the owner, after "do you recommend anything": "Do whatever you can and the best I trust you"; #469 item 6, #334, #207): (1) Played's rows simply appear: the v156 scroll-linked rise / art drift / title and score slides (`pnbRise`, `pnbPara`, `pnbFromL/R`, four `view()` timelines a row) and the v155 IntersectionObserver glide (`.wait`/`.in`, `pnbGlide`) are gone, with the dead `pnbIn`/`pnbPop` entries and the empty v157 style block; the v305 near-screen observer now only watches the badge shelf (`pbdCar` stays). Layers: Played 9 → 4 screens at 412, 8.7 → 4.5 at 859. (2) The glow behind the screens stays as it is (#469's "let it scroll with the page"): measured three ways (`tools/scenarios/bg-scroll-paint.mjs`): the glow on html with `background-attachment: fixed` repainted half the screen or more on every scroll frame (86 paints in three swipes), scrolling with the page saves ~2 screens of layers but one is plain black and the glow is painted once, so the owner's look won; only v149's `html.fast::before{transform:translateZ(0)}` (from the moving-background era) went. (3) Start (#334): `bootRedraw(name)` (core, next to extend) collects the redraws later scripts asked for while the page loads and runs each once at DOMContentLoaded: Played was drawn 4 times and Taste 3 times as the app opened, now 2 each (`tools/scenarios/start-calls.mjs` counts the draws with V8 coverage). lfApply's rAF `getBoundingClientRect()` on the hidden Queue's logo tile forced a full layout of the Queue (content-visibility doesn't stop a forced read) on every Queue draw, ~470ms twice at 4x: the tile is now measured by a ResizeObserver (`lfRO`, `lfSize`) when the Queue is first laid out, and the logo rules land before that frame is painted (old code: a size taken while hidden, wrong at the first frame, fixed a moment later); new logos are measured in idle time from the start as before (`lfNext` no longer waits for the tile). `tools/scenarios/logo-first-frame.mjs`. Long frames in the first 8s at 4x: ~6.2s → ~5.15s. Not changed: the idle warm-up of hidden tabs (it moves the switch's first layout into idle time) and pnBubble's one forced layout during the parse (~35ms at 1x, layout the first frame needs anyway). Also tried and dropped: hiding `.wrap` with content-visibility until DOMContentLoaded (#pnboot covers the screen while loading) saved ~7%, not worth a second boot path. Lessons tidy (99 → 52, a helper chat) and the plant runner (M5) merged in.

The key now adds the screen's element count, and a pass that skipped a finding because it was still moving doesn't count as seen (`lookEyeRun` returns false). Probe: one walk per screen at rest, the second game page its own; plant still caught.
- No version (Oct 18, second-look note on #576 + a kit flake): the phone's design eye could walk again on every layout pass when a finding never stops moving or the element count keeps changing (a probe with every finding "moving" and the count flipping: 15 walks in 30 passes on main) → at most 3 tries of the same content and 8 walks per screen and width (`LOOK_TRIES`; the probe: 3). `tools/eye.mjs` (the kit's pixel eye) waits for on-screen pictures and looks again once if the pictures moved during its shots: the 859 scrolled Library's 39px "spill" came and went between runs (main too); 3/3 clean after, plant eye-broken.css still caught.
- No version (Oct 18, CI on #577: the button-cut plant went unreported once, the layer-pile plant once on #576): the screen-edge button check in `bugLayoutPass` skipped a cut button that was still sliding in (`bugMoving`) and never looked again, so on a slow runner (or phone) the Queue bar mid-slide hid a tab past the edge. Now it looks once more when that slide ends (`bugMoveLeft(el)` = ms left on its finite animations, capped 2.5s; one retry per scheduled pass, `BUG.edgeAgain`, reset by bugLayoutSoon). Probe (the bar kept moving 2.2s): reported 3/3, main 0/3; all 8 plants caught.
- No version (Oct 19, second-look on #577 + a probe): the phone's checks skipped game pages. (1) v249's once-a-day gate (`gq:bugdone`) and the eye's `LOOK_SEEN` keyed a game page on screen + size + element count only, so a game with the same element count as one already checked was never checked at all (the key now holds the game id, `hubAt`). (2) The eye's 8-walk cap per screen and width counted finished walks too, so after ~8 game pages it stopped: now 8 walks skipped as moving and 40 in all. (3) The screen-edge retry timer (`BUG.et`) is cleared by bugLayoutSoon, so a retry from the screen before can't fire on the new one. Probe (14 game pages, 412 + 859): 14 looked at (main 8), an old screen's retry 0 (main 1), 30 never-settling passes with the count changing: 8 walks.
